




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

saṃyuttanikāyau


khandhavaggau


1. khandhasaṃyuttaṃ

1. nakulapituvaggau

1. nakulapitusuttaṃ

1. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā bhaggaisu viharati susumāragirai suṃsumāragirai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhaisakaḷāvanai migadāyai| atha khau nakulapitā gahapati yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau nakulapitā gahapati bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘ahamasmi, bhantai, jiṇṇau vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau āturakāyau abhikkhaṇātaṅkau| anic‍cadassāvī khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau manaubhāvanīyānañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ| auvadatu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā; anusāsatu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā; yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, gahapati, aivamaitaṃ, gahapati! āturau hāyaṃ, gahapati, kāyau aṇḍabhūtau pariyaunaddhau| yau  hi, gahapati, imaṃ kāyaṃ pariharantau muhuttampi āraugyaṃ paṭijānaiyya, kimañ‍ñatra bālyā? tasmātiha tai,  gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – ‘āturakāyassa mai satau cittaṃ anāturaṃ bhavissatī’ti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabba’’nti|

atha khau nakulapitā gahapati bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā  anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau nakulapitaraṃ gahapatiṃ āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca – ‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, gahapati, indriyāni; parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau| alattha nau aj‍ja bhagavatau sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ savanāyā’’ti?

‘‘kathañhi nau siyā, bhantai! idānāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya amataina abhisittau’’ti| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, gahapati, bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya amataina abhisittau’’ti? ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucaṃ – ‘ahamasmi, bhantai, jiṇṇau vuḍḍhau mahal‍lakau addhagatau vayauanuppattau āturakāyau abhikkhaṇātaṅkau| anic‍cadassāvī khau panāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau manaubhāvanīyānañ‍ca bhikkhūnaṃ| auvadatu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā; anusāsatu maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā; yaṃ mamassa dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāyā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ vuttai, maṃ, bhantai, bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘aivamaitaṃ, gahapati, aivamaitaṃ, gahapati! āturau hāyaṃ, gahapati, kāyau aṇḍabhūtau pariyaunaddhau| yau hi, gahapati, imaṃ kāyaṃ pariharantau  muhuttampi āraugyaṃ paṭijānaiyya, kimañ‍ñatra bālyā? tasmātiha tai gahapati, aivaṃ sikkhitabbaṃ – āturakāyassa mai satau cittaṃ anāturaṃ bhavissatīti| aivañhi tai, gahapati, sikkhitabba’nti| aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā dhammiyā kathāya amataina abhisittau’’ti|

‘‘na hi pana taṃ, gahapati, paṭibhāsi bhagavantaṃ taṃ bhagavantaṃ (sī॰) uttariṃ paṭipucchituṃ – ‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, āturakāyau caiva hauti āturacittau ca, kittāvatā ca pana āturakāyau hi khau hauti nau ca āturacittau’’’ti? ‘‘dūrataupi  khau mayaṃ, bhantai, āgacchaiyyāma āyasmatau sāriputtassa santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| sādhu vatāyasmantaṃyaiva sāriputtaṃ paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau’’ti|

‘‘taina hi, gahapati, suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau nakulapitā gahapati āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausi| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, āturakāyau caiva hauti, āturacittau ca? idha, gahapati, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ  vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ rūpaṃ, mama rūpa’nti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ rūpaṃ, mama rūpa’nti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā vaidanaṃ, vaidanāya vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ vaidanā, mama vaidanā’ti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ vaidanā, mama vaidanā’ti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, sā vaidanā vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa vaidanāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati, sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā sañ‍ñaṃ, sañ‍ñāya vā  attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñā, mama sañ‍ñā’ti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñā, mama sañ‍ñā’ti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, sā sañ‍ñā vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa sañ‍ñāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā saṅkhārai, saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ saṅkhārā, mama saṅkhārā’ti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ saṅkhārā, mama saṅkhārā’ti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, tai saṅkhārā vipariṇamanti  añ‍ñathā haunti| tassa saṅkhāravipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā  uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, mama viñ‍ñāṇa’nti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, mama viñ‍ñāṇa’nti pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, āturakāyau caiva hauti āturacittau ca|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, āturakāyau hi khau hauti nau ca āturacittau? idha, gahapati, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ rūpaṃ, mama rūpa’nti na pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ rūpaṃ, mama rūpa’nti apariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, na vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā vaidanaṃ, na vaidanāya vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ vaidanā, mama vaidanā’ti na pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ vaidanā, mama vaidanā’ti apariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, sā vaidanā vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa vaidanāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘na  sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati, na sañ‍ñāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā sañ‍ñaṃ, na sañ‍ñāya vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñā, mama sañ‍ñā’ti na pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñā, mama sañ‍ñā’ti apariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, sā sañ‍ñā vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa sañ‍ñāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

``na  saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, na saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā saṅkhārai, na saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ saṅkhārā, mama saṅkhārā’ti na pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ saṅkhārā, mama saṅkhārā’ti apariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, tai saṅkhārā vipariṇamanti añ‍ñathā haunti| tassa saṅkhāravipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘na  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ‘ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, mama viñ‍ñāṇa’nti na pariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyī hauti| tassa ‘ahaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, mama viñ‍ñāṇa’nti apariyuṭṭhaṭṭhāyinau, taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, āturakāyau hauti nau ca āturacittau’’ti|

idamavauca āyasmā sāriputtau| attamanau nakulapitā gahapati āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. daivadahasuttaṃ

2. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu sakyaisu (ka॰) viharati daivadahaṃ nāma sakyānaṃ nigamau| atha khau sambahulā pacchābhūmagamikā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘icchāma mayaṃ, bhantai, pacchābhūmaṃ janapadaṃ gantuṃ, pacchābhūmai janapadai nivāsaṃ kappaitu’’nti|

‘‘apalaukitau  pana vau, bhikkhavai, sāriputtau’’ti? ‘‘na khau nau, bhantai, apalaukitau āyasmā sāriputtau’’ti| ‘‘apalaukaitha, bhikkhavai, sāriputtaṃ| sāriputtau, bhikkhavai, paṇḍitau, bhikkhūnaṃ anuggāhakau sabrahmacārīna’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ  bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ|

taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā sāriputtau bhagavatau avidūrai añ‍ñatarasmiṃ aiḷagalāgumbai nisin‍nau hauti| atha khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā sāriputtaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ  sāraṇīyaṃ sārāṇīyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘icchāma mayaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, pacchābhūmaṃ janapadaṃ gantuṃ, pacchābhūmai janapadai nivāsaṃ kappaituṃ| apalaukitau nau satthā’’ti|

‘‘santi  hāvusau, nānāvairaj‍jagataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchitārau – khattiyapaṇḍitāpi brāhmaṇapaṇḍitāpi gahapatipaṇḍitāpi samaṇapaṇḍitāpi| paṇḍitā hāvusau, manussā vīmaṃsakā – ‘kiṃvādī panāyasmantānaṃ kiṃvādāyasmantānaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) satthā kimakkhāyīti, kac‍ci vau āyasmantānaṃ dhammā sussutā suggahitā sumanasikatā sūpadhāritā suppaṭividdhā pañ‍ñāya, yathā byākaramānā āyasmantau vuttavādinau caiva bhagavatau assatha, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyātha, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyātha, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau vādānupātau (aṭṭhakathāyaṃ pāṭhantaraṃ) gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’’ti?

‘‘dūrataupi khau mayaṃ, āvusau, āgacchaiyyāma āyasmatau sāriputtassa santikai aitassa bhāsitassa atthamañ‍ñātuṃ| sādhu vatāyasmantaṃyaiva sāriputtaṃ paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau’’ti| ‘‘taina hāvusau, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā sāriputtau aitadavauca –

‘‘santi  hāvusau, nānāvairaj‍jagataṃ bhikkhuṃ pañhaṃ pucchitārau – khattiyapaṇḍitāpi  …pai॰… samaṇapaṇḍitāpi| paṇḍitā hāvusau, manussā vīmaṃsakā  – ‘kiṃvādī panāyasmantānaṃ satthā kimakkhāyī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, āvusau, aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘chandarāgavinayakkhāyī khau nau, āvusau, satthā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ byākataipi khau, āvusau, assuyaiva uttariṃ pañhaṃ pucchitārau – khattiyapaṇḍitāpi…pai॰… samaṇapaṇḍitāpi| paṇḍitā hāvusau, manussā vīmaṃsakā – ‘kismiṃ panāyasmantānaṃ chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, āvusau, aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘rūpai khau, āvusau, chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ byākataipi khau, āvusau, assuyaiva uttariṃ pañhaṃ pucchitārau – khattiyapaṇḍitāpi…pai॰… samaṇapaṇḍitāpi| paṇḍitā hāvusau, manussā vīmaṃsakā – ‘kiṃ panāyasmantānaṃ ādīnavaṃ disvā rūpai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, āvusau, aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘rūpai khau, āvusau, avigatarāgassa avītarāgassa (syā॰ kaṃ॰) avigatachandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa avigatataṇhassa tassa rūpassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu avigatarāgassa…pai॰… avigatataṇhassa taisaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā  uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| viñ‍ñāṇai avigatarāgassa avigatachandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa avigatataṇhassa tassa viñ‍ñāṇassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, ādīnavaṃ disvā rūpai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā’’’ti|

‘‘aivaṃ  byākataipi khau, āvusau, assuyaiva uttariṃ pañhaṃ pucchitārau – khattiyapaṇḍitāpi brāhmaṇapaṇḍitāpi gahapatipaṇḍitāpi samaṇapaṇḍitāpi| paṇḍitā hāvusau, manussā vīmaṃsakā – ‘kiṃ panāyasmantānaṃ ānisaṃsaṃ disvā rūpai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhā tumhai, āvusau, aivaṃ byākaraiyyātha – ‘rūpai khau, āvusau, vigatarāgassa vigatachandassa vigatapaimassa vigatapipāsassa vigatapariḷāhassa vigatataṇhassa tassa rūpassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu vigatarāgassa vigatachandassa vigatapaimassa vigatapipāsassa vigatapariḷāhassa vigatataṇhassa taisaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| viñ‍ñāṇai vigatarāgassa vigatachandassa vigatapaimassa vigatapipāsassa vigatapariḷāhassa vigatataṇhassa tassa viñ‍ñāṇassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti  saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| idaṃ khau nau, āvusau, ānisaṃsaṃ disvā rūpai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai chandarāgavinayakkhāyī satthā’’’ti|

‘‘akusalai cāvusau, dhammai upasampaj‍ja viharatau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai sukhau vihārau abhavissa avighātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā, nayidaṃ bhagavā akusalānaṃ  dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ vaṇṇaiyya| yasmā ca khau, āvusau, akusalai dhammai upasampaj‍ja viharatau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai dukkhau vihārau savighātau saupāyāsau sapariḷāhau, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā, tasmā bhagavā akusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ pahānaṃ vaṇṇaiti|

‘‘kusalai cāvusau, dhammai upasampaj‍ja viharatau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai dukkhau vihārau abhavissa savighātau saupāyāsau  sapariḷāhau, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā duggati pāṭikaṅkhā, nayidaṃ bhagavā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ vaṇṇaiyya| yasmā ca khau, āvusau, kusalai dhammai upasampaj‍ja viharatau diṭṭhai caiva dhammai sukhau vihārau avighātau anupāyāsau apariḷāhau, kāyassa ca bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugati pāṭikaṅkhā, tasmā bhagavā kusalānaṃ dhammānaṃ upasampadaṃ vaṇṇaitī’’ti|

idamavaucāyasmā sāriputtau| attamanā tai bhikkhū āyasmatau sāriputtassa bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| dutiyaṃ|

3. hāliddikānisuttaṃ

3. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cānau avantīsu viharati kuraragharai kulagharai (ka॰) papātai pabbatai| atha khau hāliddikāni hāliddakāni (sī॰), haliddikāni (syā॰) gahapati yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau hāliddikāni gahapati āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā aṭṭhakavaggiyai māgaṇḍiyapañhai –

‘‘aukaṃ pahāya anikaitasārī,

gāmai akubbaṃ akrubbaṃ (ka॰) muni santhavāni sandhavāni (ka॰)|

kāmaihi rittau apurakkharānau apuraikkharānau (sī॰ suttanipātaipi) mauggal‍lānai 5-135 suttampi aulaukaitabbaṃ,

kathaṃ na viggayha janaina kayirā’’ti||

‘‘imassa nu khau, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘rūpadhātu  khau, gahapati, viñ‍ñāṇassa aukau| rūpadhāturāgavinibandhañ‍ca … vinibaddhañ‍ja (pī॰ sī॰ aṭṭha॰) pana viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aukasārī’ti vuc‍cati| vaidanādhātu khau, gahapati, viñ‍ñāṇassa aukau| vaidanādhāturāgavinibandhañ‍ca pana viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aukasārī’ti vuc‍cati| sañ‍ñādhātu  khau, gahapati, viñ‍ñāṇassa aukau| sañ‍ñādhāturāgavinibandhañ‍ca pana viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aukasārī’ti vuc‍cati| saṅkhāradhātu khau, gahapati, viñ‍ñāṇassa aukau| saṅkhāradhāturāgavinibandhañ‍ca pana viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aukasārī’ti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ  khau, gahapati, aukasārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, anaukasārī hauti? rūpadhātuyā khau, gahapati, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī nandi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā upāyupādānā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā anabhāvakatā (sī॰ pī॰), anabhāvaṃgatā (syā॰ kaṃ॰) āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā tathāgatau ‘anaukasārī’ti vuc‍cati| vaidanādhātuyā khau, gahapati… sañ‍ñādhātuyā khau, gahapati… saṅkhāradhātuyā khau, gahapati… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā khau, gahapati, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā  taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā tai tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā tathāgatau ‘anaukasārī’ti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, anaukasārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, nikaitasārī hauti? rūpanimittanikaitavisāravinibandhā khau, gahapati, ‘nikaitasārī’ti vuc‍cati| saddanimitta…pai॰… gandhanimitta… rasanimitta… phauṭṭhabbanimitta… dhammanimittanikaitavisāravinibandhā khau, gahapati, ‘nikaitasārī’ti vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, nikaitasārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, anikaitasārī hauti? rūpanimittanikaitavisāravinibandhā khau, gahapati, tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā tathāgatau ‘anikaitasārī’ti vuc‍cati| saddanimitta… gandhanimitta… rasanimitta… phauṭṭhabbanimitta… dhammanimittanikaitavisāravinibandhā khau, gahapati, tathāgatassa pahīnā ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā  āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| tasmā  tathāgatau ‘anikaitasārī’ti  vuc‍cati| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, anikaitasārī hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, gāmai santhavajātau sandhavajātau (ka॰) hauti? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cau gihīhi gihi (ka॰) saṃsaṭṭhau viharati sahanandī sahasaukī, sukhitaisu sukhitau, dukkhitaisu dukkhitau, uppan‍naisu kic‍cakaraṇīyaisu attanā taisu yaugaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, gāmai santhavajātau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, gāmai na santhavajātau hauti? idha, gahapati, bhikkhu gihīhi gihi (ka॰) asaṃsaṭṭhau viharati na sahanandī na sahasaukī na sukhitaisu sukhitau na dukkhitaisu dukkhitau, uppan‍naisu kic‍cakaraṇīyaisu na attanā taisu yaugaṃ āpaj‍jati| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, gāmai na santhavajātau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, kāmaihi arittau hauti? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmaisu avigatarāgau  hauti avigatachandau avigatapaimau avigatapipāsau avigatapariḷāhau avigatataṇhau| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, kāmaihi arittau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, kāmaihi rittau hauti? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cau kāmaisu vigatarāgau hauti vigatachandau vigatapaimau vigatapipāsau vigatapariḷāhau vigatataṇhau| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, kāmaihi rittau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, purakkharānau hauti? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, purakkharānau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, apurakkharānau hauti? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau  siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, apurakkharānau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, kathaṃ viggayha janaina kattā hauti? idha, gahapati, aikac‍cau aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kattā hauti – ‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi; ahaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāmi| kiṃ tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānissasi? micchāpaṭipan‍nau tvamasi; ahamasmi sammāpaṭipan‍nau| purai vacanīyaṃ pacchā avaca; pacchā vacanīyaṃ purai avaca| sahitaṃ mai, asahitaṃ tai| adhiciṇṇaṃ tai viparāvattaṃ| āraupitau tai vādau; cara vādappamaukkhāya| niggahitausi; nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’ti| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, kathaṃ viggayha janaina kattā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, gahapati, kathaṃ na viggayha janaina kattā hauti? idha, gahapati, bhikkhu na aivarūpiṃ kathaṃ kattā hauti – ‘na tvaṃ imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ ājānāsi…pai॰… nibbaiṭhaihi vā sacai pahausī’ti| aivaṃ khau, gahapati, kathaṃ na viggayha janaina kattā hauti|

‘‘iti  khau, gahapati, yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā aṭṭhakavaggiyai māgaṇḍiyapañhai –

‘‘aukaṃ pahāya anikaitasārī,

gāmai akubbaṃ munisanthavāni|

kāmaihi rittau apurakkharānau,

kathaṃ na viggayha janaina kayirā’’ti||

‘‘imassa khau, gahapati, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyahāliddikānisuttaṃ

4. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā mahākac‍cānau avantīsu viharati kuraragharai papātai pabbatai| atha  khau hāliddikāni gahapati yaināyasmā mahākac‍cānau…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau hāliddikāni  gahapati āyasmantaṃ mahākac‍cānaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vuttamidaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā sak‍kapañhai – ‘yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttā, tai ac‍cantaniṭṭhā ac‍cantayaugakkhaiminau ac‍cantabrahmacārinau ac‍cantapariyausānā saiṭṭhā daivamanussāna’’’nti|

‘‘imassa nu khau, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa kathaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti?

‘‘rūpadhātuyā  khau, gahapati, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘vaidanādhātuyā khau, gahapati… sañ‍ñādhātuyā khau, gahapati… saṅkhāradhātuyā khau, gahapati… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā  khau, gahapati, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, taisaṃ khayā virāgā niraudhā cāgā paṭinissaggā cittaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati|

‘‘iti khau, gahapati, yaṃ taṃ vuttaṃ bhagavatā sak‍kapañhai – ‘yai tai samaṇabrāhmaṇā taṇhāsaṅkhayavimuttā tai ac‍cantaniṭṭhā ac‍cantayaugakkhaiminau ac‍cantabrahmacārinau ac‍cantapariyausānā saiṭṭhā daivamanussāna’’’nti|

‘‘imassa khau, gahapati, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samādhisuttaṃ

5. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai  anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘samādhiṃ, bhikkhavai, bhāvaitha; samāhitau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, vaidanāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, sañ‍ñāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, saṅkhārānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca’’|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, rūpassa samudayau, kau vaidanāya samudayau, kau sañ‍ñāya samudayau, kau saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau, kau viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati? rūpaṃ abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa rūpaṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| yā rūpai nandī tadupādānaṃ| tassupādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā  jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā sambhavanti| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘vaidanaṃ  abhinandati…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ abhinandati… saṅkhārai abhinandati… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandati abhivadati ajjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandatau abhivadatau ajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau uppaj‍jati nandī| yā viñ‍ñāṇai nandī tadupādānaṃ| tassupādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā…pai॰…  aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti|

‘‘ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpassa samudayau; ayaṃ vaidanāya samudayau; ayaṃ sañ‍ñāya samudayau; ayaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ samudayau; ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau|

‘‘kau ca, bhikkhavai, rūpassa atthaṅgamau, kau vaidanāya… kau sañ‍ñāya… kau saṅkhārānaṃ… kau viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau?

idha, bhikkhavai, nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati? rūpaṃ nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa rūpaṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau yā rūpai nandī sā nirujjhati| tassa nandīniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘vaidanaṃ nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa vaidanaṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausā  tiṭṭhatau yā vaidanāya nandī sā nirujjhati| tassa nandīniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘sañ‍ñaṃ nābhinandati…pai॰… saṅkhārai nābhinandati nābhivadati nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa  saṅkhārai anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau yā saṅkhāraisu nandī sā nirujjhati| tassa nandīniraudhā upādānaniraudhau; upādānaniraudhā bhavaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nābhinandati nābhivadati  nājjhausāya tiṭṭhati| tassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anabhinandatau anabhivadatau anajjhausāya tiṭṭhatau yā viñ‍ñāṇai nandī sā nirujjhati| tassa nandīniraudhā upādānaniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hauti|

‘‘ayaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpassa atthaṅgamau, ayaṃ vaidanāya atthaṅgamau, ayaṃ sañ‍ñāya atthaṅgamau, ayaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ atthaṅgamau, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paṭisal‍lāṇasuttaṃ

6. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘paṭisal‍lāṇai, bhikkhavai, yaugamāpaj‍jatha| paṭisal‍līṇau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| kiñ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti? rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, vaidanāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, sañ‍ñāya samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, saṅkhārānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca, viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca’’…pai॰… (yathā paṭhamasuttai tathā vitthāraitabbau|) chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. upādāparitassanāsuttaṃ

7. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘upādāparitassanañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anupādāaparitassanañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti, khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upādāparitassanā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa  akauvidau sappurisadhammai  avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā rūpavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā uttāsavā ca hauti vighātavā ca apaikkhavā ca upādāya ca paritassati|

‘‘vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā vaidanaṃ, vaidanāya vā attānaṃ| tassa sā vaidanā vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa vaidanāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā vaidanāvipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa vaidanāvipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā uttāsavā ca hauti vighātavā ca apaikkhavā ca upādāya ca paritassati|

‘‘sañ‍ñaṃ  attatau samanupassati…pai॰… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā saṅkhārai, saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ| tassa tai saṅkhārā vipariṇamanti añ‍ñathā haunti| tassa saṅkhāravipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā saṅkhāravipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa saṅkhāravipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā uttāsavā ca hauti vighātavā ca apaikkhavā ca upādāya ca paritassati|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau  samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā  viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau pariyādānā uttāsavā ca hauti vighātavā ca apaikkhavā ca upādāya ca paritassati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, upādāparitassanā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anupādāaparitassanā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ  vā attānaṃ; na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na rūpavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa na rūpavipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau apariyādānā na caivuttāsavā na caiva uttāsavā (pī॰ ka॰) hauti na ca vighātavā na ca apaikkhavā, anupādāya ca na paritassati|

‘‘na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, na vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā vaidanaṃ, na vaidanāya vā attānaṃ| tassa sā vaidanā vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa vaidanāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na vaidanāvipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa na vaidanāvipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau apariyādānā na caivuttāsavā hauti na ca vighātavā na ca apaikkhavā, anupādāya ca na paritassati|

‘‘na sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… na saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, na saṅkhāravantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā saṅkhārai, na saṅkhāraisu vā attānaṃ| tassa tai saṅkhārā vipariṇamanti añ‍ñathā haunti| tassa saṅkhāravipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na saṅkhāravipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa na saṅkhāravipariṇāmānuparivattijā  paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau apariyādānā na caivuttāsavā hauti na ca vighātavā na ca apaikkhavā, anupādāya ca na paritassati|

‘‘na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ…pai॰… tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā na viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivatti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hauti| tassa na viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmānuparivattijā paritassanā dhammasamuppādā cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhanti| caitasau apariyādānā na caivuttāsavā hauti na ca vighātavā na ca apaikkhavā, anupādāya ca na paritassati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anupādā aparitassanaṃ hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaupādāparitassanāsuttaṃ

8. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘upādāparitassanañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi anupādāaparitassanañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha…pai॰… kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, upādāparitassanā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| vaidanaṃ aitaṃ mama…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ aitaṃ mama… saṅkhārai aitaṃ mama… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati| tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, upādāparitassanā hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anupādāaparitassanā hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa  rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| vaidanaṃ naitaṃ mama… sañ‍ñaṃ naitaṃ mama… saṅkhārai naitaṃ mama… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassati| tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati añ‍ñathā hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā nuppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, anupādāaparitassanā hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. kālattayaanic‍casuttaṃ

9. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ rūpasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ rūpaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| vaidanā anic‍cā…pai॰…  sañ‍ñā anic‍cā… saṅkhārā anic‍cā atītānāgatā; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītaisu saṅkhāraisu anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai saṅkhārai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. kālattayadukkhasuttaṃ

10. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau  pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ rūpasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ rūpaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti| vaidanā dukkhā… sañ‍ñā dukkhā… saṅkhārā dukkhā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  dukkhaṃ atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. kālattayaanattasuttaṃ

11. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ rūpasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ rūpaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau  hauti| vaidanā anattā… sañ‍ñā anattā… saṅkhārā anattā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā atītānāgataṃ; kau pana vādau pac‍cuppan‍nassa! aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau atītasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hautī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

nakulapituvaggau paṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nakulapitā daivadahā, dvaipi hāliddikāni ca|

samādhipaṭisal‍lāṇā, upādāparitassanā  duvai|

atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā, vaggau taina pavuc‍cati||

2. anic‍cavaggau

1. anic‍casuttaṃ

12. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – sāvatthiyaṃ| tatra khau…pai॰… ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, vaidanā anic‍cā, sañ‍ñā anic‍cā, saṅkhārā anic‍cā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dukkhasuttaṃ

13. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ, vaidanā dukkhā, sañ‍ñā dukkhā, saṅkhārā dukkhā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. anattasuttaṃ

14. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā, vaidanā anattā, sañ‍ñā anattā, saṅkhārā anattā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. yadanic‍casuttaṃ

15. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanā anic‍cā| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| sañ‍ñā anic‍cā…pai॰… saṅkhārā anic‍cā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. yaṃdukkhasuttaṃ

16. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanā dukkhā… sañ‍ñā dukkhā… saṅkhārā dukkhā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ| yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. yadanattāsuttaṃ

17. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanā anattā… sañ‍ñā anattā… saṅkhārā anattā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sahaituanic‍casuttaṃ

18. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau rūpassa uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ kutau nic‍caṃ bhavissati! vaidanā anic‍cā| yaupi haitu, yaupi pac‍cayau vaidanāya uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā kutau nic‍cā bhavissati! sañ‍ñā anic‍cā… saṅkhārā anic‍cā| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau saṅkhārānaṃ uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā kutau nic‍cā bhavissanti! viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, saupi anic‍cau| anic‍casambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ kutau nic‍caṃ bhavissati! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. sahaitudukkhasuttaṃ

19. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau rūpassa uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ kutau sukhaṃ bhavissati! vaidanā dukkhā… sañ‍ñā dukkhā… saṅkhārā dukkhā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  dukkhaṃ| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, saupi dukkhau| dukkhasambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ kutau sukhaṃ bhavissati! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sahaituanattasuttaṃ

20. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau rūpassa uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ kutau attā bhavissati! vaidanā anattā… sañ‍ñā anattā… saṅkhārā anattā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| yaupi haitu yaupi pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇassa uppādāya, saupi anattā| anattasambhūtaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ kutau attā bhavissati! aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. ānandasuttaṃ

21. sāvatthiyaṃ … ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā ānandau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘niraudhau niraudhau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamaisānaṃ khau, bhantai, dhammānaṃ niraudhau niraudhā (sī॰ pī॰) ‘niraudhau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, ānanda, anic‍caṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammaṃ| tassa niraudhau niraudhā (sī॰ pī॰) ‘niraudhau’ti vuc‍cati| vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā| tassā niraudhau ‘niraudhau’ti vuc‍cati| sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā  khayadhammā vayadhammā virāgadhammā niraudhadhammā| taisaṃ niraudhau ‘niraudhau’ti vuc‍cati| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ saṅkhataṃ paṭic‍casamuppan‍naṃ khayadhammaṃ vayadhammaṃ virāgadhammaṃ niraudhadhammaṃ| tassa  niraudhau ‘niraudhau’ti vuc‍cati| imaisaṃ khau, ānanda, dhammānaṃ niraudhau ‘niraudhau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

anic‍cavaggau dutiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā, yadanic‍cāparai tayau|

haitunāpi tayau vuttā, ānandaina ca tai dasāti||

3. bhāravaggau

1. bhārasuttaṃ

22. sāvatthiyaṃ … tatra khau … ‘‘bhārañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi bhārahārañ‍ca bhārādānañ‍ca bhāranikkhaipanañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bhārau? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā tissa vacanīyaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhārau’’|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, bhārahārau? puggalau tissa vacanīyaṃ| yvāyaṃ āyasmā aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau; ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhārahārau|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhārādānaṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā paunaubbhavikā (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) nandīrāgasahagatā nandirāgasahagatā (sabbattha) tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhārādānaṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhāranikkhaipanaṃ? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhāranikkhaipana’’nti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna vatvā (sī॰) aivamīdisaisu ṭhānaisu sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘bhārā havai pañ‍cakkhandhā, bhārahārau ca puggalau|

bhārādānaṃ dukhaṃ laukai, bhāranikkhaipanaṃ sukhaṃ||

‘‘nikkhipitvā  garuṃ bhāraṃ, añ‍ñaṃ bhāraṃ anādiya|

samūlaṃ taṇhamabbuyha taṇhamabbhuyha (pī॰ ka॰), nicchātau parinibbutau’’ti|| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pariñ‍ñasuttaṃ

23. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pariñ‍ñaiyyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi pariñ‍ñañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, vaidanā pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, sañ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, saṅkhārā pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñā? yau, bhikkhavai, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. abhijānasuttaṃ

24. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya; vaidanaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya; sañ‍ñaṃ anabhijānaṃ… saṅkhārai anabhijānaṃ  aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anabhijānaṃ aparijānaṃ avirājayaṃ appajahaṃ abhabbau dukkhakkhayāya| rūpañ‍ca khau, bhikkhavai, abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāya; vaidanaṃ abhijānaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhijānaṃ parijānaṃ virājayaṃ pajahaṃ bhabbau dukkhakkhayāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. chandarāgasuttaṃ

25. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpasmiṃ chandarāgau taṃ  pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| yau vaidanāya chandarāgau taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ sā vaidanā pahīnā bhavissati ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yau sañ‍ñāya chandarāgau taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ sā sañ‍ñā pahīnā bhavissati ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yau saṅkhāraisu chandarāgau taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ tai saṅkhārā pahīnā bhavissanti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgau taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhamma’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. assādasuttaṃ

26. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pubbaiva pubbai (pī॰ ka॰) mai, bhikkhavai, sambaudhā anabhisambuddhassa baudhisattassaiva baudhisattassa (pī॰ ka॰) satau aitadahausi – ‘kau nu khau rūpassa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau vaidanāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau sañ‍ñāya assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau saṅkhārānaṃ assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ? kau viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’nti? tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhavai, aitadahausi – ‘yaṃ khau rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ rūpassa assādau| yaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ rūpassa ādīnavau| yau rūpasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau  chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ vaidanāya assādau yā (ka॰)| yaṃ vaidanā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavau| yau vaidanāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati…pai॰… yaṃ saṅkhārai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ assādau| yaṃ yai (sī॰ ka॰) saṅkhārā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā, ayaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ ādīnavau| yau saṅkhāraisu chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau| yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ, idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ’’’|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ abhisambuddhau (sī॰)| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ aivaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai  sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰); ayamantimā jāti; natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaassādasuttaṃ

27. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau rūpassa assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā rūpassa assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| rūpassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau rūpassa ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā rūpassa ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| rūpassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ| vaidanāyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… sañ‍ñāyāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… saṅkhārānāhaṃ, bhikkhavai… viñ‍ñāṇassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, assādapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| viñ‍ñāṇassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādīnavapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yau viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau pañ‍ñāya mai sau sudiṭṭhau| viñ‍ñāṇassāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, nissaraṇapariyaisanaṃ acariṃ| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ tadajjhagamaṃ| yāvatā viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ pañ‍ñāya mai taṃ sudiṭṭhaṃ| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ assādañ‍ca  assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ…pai॰… abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ| ñāṇañ‍ca pana mai dassanaṃ udapādi – ‘akuppā mai vimutti caitauvimutti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰); ayamantimā jāti; natthi dāni punabbhavau’’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyaassādasuttaṃ

28. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpassa assādau abhavissa nayidaṃ  sattā rūpasmiṃ sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi rūpassa assādau, tasmā sattā rūpasmiṃ sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpassa ādīnavau abhavissa nayidaṃ sattā rūpasmiṃ nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi rūpassa ādīnavau, tasmā sattā rūpasmiṃ  nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa nayidaṃ sattā rūpasmā nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā rūpasmā nissaranti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, vaidanāya…pai॰… nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñāya… nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā saṅkhāraihi nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi saṅkhārānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā saṅkhāraihi nissaranti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau, tasmā sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ sāraj‍janti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau, tasmā sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ nibbindanti| nau caidaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ abhavissa, nayidaṃ sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmā nissaraiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, atthi viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ, tasmā sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmā nissaranti|

‘‘yāvakīvañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sattā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñaṃsu nābbhañ‍ñiṃsu (sī॰); naiva tāva, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā  sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya nissaṭā visaṃyuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina caitasā vihariṃsu| yatau ca khau, bhikkhavai, sattā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ assādañ‍ca assādatau ādīnavañ‍ca ādīnavatau nissaraṇañ‍ca nissaraṇatau yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñaṃsu; atha, bhikkhavai, sattā sadaivakā laukā samārakā sabrahmakā sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya  nissaṭā visaṃyuttā vippamuttā vimariyādīkataina caitasā viharanti’’| sattamaṃ|

8. abhinandanasuttaṃ

29. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau vaidanaṃ abhinandati… yau sañ‍ñaṃ abhinandati… yau saṅkhārai abhinandati… yau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau abhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ abhinandati, aparimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmi| yau vaidanaṃ nābhinandati… yau sañ‍ñaṃ nābhinandati… yau saṅkhārai nābhinandati… yau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nābhinandati, dukkhaṃ sau nābhinandati| yau dukkhaṃ nābhinandati, parimuttau sau dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. uppādasuttaṃ

30. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpassa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau raugānaṃ ṭhiti jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau vaidanāya…pai॰… yau sañ‍ñāya…pai॰… yau saṅkhārānaṃ…pai॰… yau viñ‍ñāṇassa  uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau raugānaṃ ṭhiti jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau raugānaṃ vūpasamau jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau vaidanāya  …pai॰… yau sañ‍ñāya… yau saṅkhārānaṃ… yau viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau raugānaṃ vūpasamau jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. aghamūlasuttaṃ

31. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘aghañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi aghamūlañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca  bhikkhavai aghaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, aghaṃ, vaidanā aghaṃ, sañ‍ñā aghaṃ, saṅkhārā aghaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aghaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, aghaṃ| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, aghamūlaṃ? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā nandīrāgasahagatā nandirāgasahagatā (sabbattha) tatratatrābhinandinī; saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, aghamūla’’nti| dasamaṃ|

11. pabhaṅgusuttaṃ

32. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pabhaṅguñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi appabhaṅguñ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, pabhaṅgu, kiṃ appabhaṅgu? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, pabhaṅgu| yau tassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, idaṃ appabhaṅgu| vaidanā pabhaṅgu| yau tassā niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, idaṃ appabhaṅgu| sañ‍ñā pabhaṅgu… saṅkhārā pabhaṅgu| yau taisaṃ niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, idaṃ appabhaṅgu| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pabhaṅgu| yau tassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, idaṃ appabhaṅgū’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

bhāravaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

bhāraṃ  pariñ‍ñaṃ abhijānaṃ, chandarāgaṃ catutthakaṃ|

assādā ca tayau vuttā, abhinandanamaṭṭhamaṃ|

uppādaṃ aghamūlañ‍ca, aikādasamau pabhaṅgūti||

4. natumhākaṃvaggau

1. natumhākaṃsuttaṃ

33. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya  sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| vaidanā na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| sā vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| sañ‍ñā na tumhākaṃ… saṅkhārā  na tumhākaṃ, tai pajahatha| tai vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissanti| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yaṃ imasmiṃ jaitavanai tiṇakaṭṭhasākhāpalāsaṃ taṃ janau haraiyya vā ḍahaiyya vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karaiyya| api nu tumhākaṃ aivamassa – ‘amhai janau harati vā ḍahati vā yathāpac‍cayaṃ vā karautī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kissa haitu’’? ‘‘na hi nau aitaṃ, bhantai, attā vā attaniyaṃ vā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| vaidanā na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| sā vau pahīnā hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| sañ‍ñā na tumhākaṃ… saṅkhārā na tumhākaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissatī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyanatumhākaṃsuttaṃ

34. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| vaidanā na tumhākaṃ… sañ‍ñā na tumhākaṃ… saṅkhārā na tumhākaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na tumhākaṃ, taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, na tumhākaṃ taṃ pajahatha| taṃ vau pahīnaṃ hitāya sukhāya bhavissatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. añ‍ñatarabhikkhusuttaṃ

35. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu; yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau, appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anusaiti, taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati; yaṃ nānusaiti, na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ, sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ cai, bhantai, anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| sañ‍ñaṃ cai anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| saṅkhārai cai anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| rūpaṃ cai, bhantai, nānusaiti na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai… sañ‍ñaṃ cai… saṅkhārai cai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai nānusaiti  na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ cai, bhikkhu, anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai… sañ‍ñaṃ cai… saṅkhārai cai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai anusaiti taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| rūpaṃ cai, bhikkhu, nānusaiti na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai… sañ‍ñaṃ cai… saṅkhārai cai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai nānusaiti na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina, bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā  anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi|

atha khau sau bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja vihāsi| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaañ‍ñatarabhikkhusuttaṃ

36. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā  saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anusaiti taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ anumīyati taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| yaṃ nānusaiti na taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ nānumīyati na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchatī’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ, sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ cai, bhantai, anusaiti taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ anumīyati taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai anusaiti… sañ‍ñaṃ cai anusaiti… saṅkhārai cai anusaiti… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai anusaiti taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ anumīyati taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| rūpaṃ cai, bhantai, nānusaiti na taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ nānumīyati  na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai nānusaiti… sañ‍ñaṃ cai nānusaiti… saṅkhārai cai nānusaiti… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai nānusaiti na taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ nānumīyati na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ cai, bhikkhu, anusaiti taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ anumīyati taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai, bhikkhu… sañ‍ñaṃ cai, bhikkhu… saṅkhārai cai, bhikkhu… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai, bhikkhu, anusaiti taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ anumīyati taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| rūpaṃ cai, bhikkhu, nānusaiti na taṃ  anumīyati; yaṃ nānumīyati na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ cai nānusaiti… sañ‍ñaṃ cai nānusaiti… saṅkhārai cai nānusaiti… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ cai nānusaiti na taṃ anumīyati; yaṃ nānumīyati na taina saṅkhaṃ gacchati| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā  saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| catutthaṃ|

5. ānandasuttaṃ

37. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sacai  taṃ, ānanda, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaisaṃ, āvusau ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa ṭhitānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, ānanda, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai  maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaisaṃ, āvusau ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘rūpassa khau, āvusau, uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa  añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, ānanda! rūpassa khau, ānanda, uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| imaisaṃ khau, ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatīti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, ānanda, aivaṃ byākaraiyyāsī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyaānandasuttaṃ

38. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘sacai taṃ, ānanda, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaisaṃ, āvusau ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha? katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati? katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, ānanda, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘katamaisaṃ, āvusau ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha? katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa  añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati? katamaisaṃ dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘yaṃ khau, āvusau, rūpaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| yā vaidanā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā; tassā uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitāya añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā; taisaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha’’’|

‘‘yaṃ khau, āvusau, rūpaṃ ajātaṃ apātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| yā vaidanā ajātā apātubhūtā; tassā uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitāya añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| yā sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yai saṅkhārā ajātā apātubhūtā; taisaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ajātaṃ apātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati|

‘‘yaṃ khau, āvusau, rūpaṃ jātaṃ pātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| yā vaidanā jātā pātubhūtā…pai॰… yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā jātā pātubhūtā; taisaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ jātaṃ pātubhūtaṃ tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| imaisaṃ khau, āvusau, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatī’ti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, bhantai, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sādhu, sādhu, ānanda! yaṃ khau, ānanda, rūpaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| yā vaidanā … yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha| imaisaṃ khau, ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyittha, vayau pañ‍ñāyittha, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyittha|

‘‘yaṃ khau, ānanda, rūpaṃ ajātaṃ apātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| yā vaidanā… yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ajātaṃ apātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati| imaisaṃ khau, ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyissati, vayau pañ‍ñāyissati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyissati|

‘‘yaṃ khau, ānanda, rūpaṃ jātaṃ pātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| yā vaidanā jātā pātubhūtā… yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ jātaṃ pātubhūtaṃ; tassa uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyati| imaisaṃ khau, ānanda, dhammānaṃ uppādau pañ‍ñāyati, vayau pañ‍ñāyati, ṭhitassa añ‍ñathattaṃ pañ‍ñāyatīti| aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, ānanda, aivaṃ byākaraiyyāsī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anudhammasuttaṃ

39. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ayamanudhammau hauti yaṃ rūpai nibbidābahulau nibbidābahulaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) viharaiyya, vaidanāya nibbidābahulau viharaiyya, sañ‍ñā nibbidābahulau viharaiyya, saṅkhāraisu nibbidābahulau viharaiyya, viñ‍ñāṇai nibbidābahulau viharaiyya| yau rūpai nibbidābahulau viharantau, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu nibbidābahulau viharantau, viñ‍ñāṇai nibbidābahulau viharantau rūpaṃ parijānāti, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānāti, sau rūpaṃ parijānaṃ, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānaṃ parimuc‍cati rūpamhā, parimuc‍cati vaidanā, parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, parimuc‍cati  jātiyā jarāmaraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyaanudhammasuttaṃ

40. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ayamanudhammau hauti yaṃ rūpai anic‍cānupassī viharaiyya…pai॰… parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyaanudhammasuttaṃ

41. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ayamanudhammau hauti yaṃ rūpai dukkhānupassī viharaiyya…pai॰…  parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. catutthaanudhammasuttaṃ

42. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau ayamanudhammau hauti yaṃ rūpai anattānupassī viharaiyya, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai anattānupassī viharaiyya| yau rūpai anattānupassī viharantau…pai॰… rūpaṃ parijānāti, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānāti, sau rūpaṃ parijānaṃ, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānaṃ parimuc‍cati rūpamhā, parimuc‍cati vaidanāya, parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāmaraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi, parimuc‍cati dukkhasmāti vadāmī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

natumhākaṃvaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

natumhākaina  dvai vuttā, bhikkhūhi aparai duvai|

ānandaina ca dvai vuttā, anudhammaihi dvai dukāti||

5. attadīpavaggau

1. attadīpasuttaṃ

43. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘attadīpā, bhikkhavai, viharatha attasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā, dhammadīpā dhammasaraṇā anañ‍ñasaraṇā| attadīpānaṃ, bhikkhavai, viharataṃ attasaraṇānaṃ anañ‍ñasaraṇānaṃ, dhammadīpānaṃ dhammasaraṇānaṃ anañ‍ñasaraṇānaṃ yauni upaparikkhitabbā| kiṃjātikā saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā, kiṃpahautikā’’ti?

‘‘kiṃjātikā  ca, bhikkhavai, saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā, kiṃpahautikā? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau, rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā ca hauti| tassa rūpavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, vaidanāvantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā vaidanaṃ, vaidanāya vā attānaṃ| tassa sā vaidanā vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā ca hauti| tassa vaidanāvipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā| sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa  taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vipariṇamati, añ‍ñathā ca hauti| tassa viñ‍ñāṇavipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā|

‘‘rūpassa tvaiva, bhikkhavai, anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmaṃ virāgaṃ niraudhaṃ vipariṇāma virāga niraudhaṃ (sī॰), pubbai caiva rūpaṃ aitarahi ca sabbaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammanti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yai saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā na paritassati, aparitassaṃ sukhaṃ viharati, sukhavihārī bhikkhu ‘tadaṅganibbutau’ti vuc‍cati| vaidanāya tvaiva, bhikkhavai, anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmaṃ virāgaṃ niraudhaṃ, pubbai caiva vaidanā aitarahi ca sabbā vaidanā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammāti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ  sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yai saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā na paritassati, aparitassaṃ sukhaṃ viharati, sukhavihārī bhikkhu ‘tadaṅganibbutau’ti vuc‍cati| sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ tvaiva, bhikkhavai, anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmaṃ virāgaṃ niraudhaṃ, pubbai caiva saṅkhārā aitarahi ca sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammāti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yai saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā na paritassati, aparitassaṃ sukhaṃ viharati, sukhavihārī bhikkhu ‘tadaṅganibbutau’ti vuc‍cati| viñ‍ñāṇassa tvaiva, bhikkhavai, anic‍cataṃ viditvā vipariṇāmaṃ virāgaṃ niraudhaṃ, pubbai caiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ aitarahi ca sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammanti, aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya  passatau yai saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā  tai pahīyanti| taisaṃ pahānā na paritassati, aparitassaṃ sukhaṃ viharati, sukhavihārī bhikkhu ‘tadaṅganibbutau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṭipadāsuttaṃ

44. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sak‍kāyasamudayagāminiñ‍ca  vau, bhikkhavai, paṭipadaṃ daisaissāmi, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyasamudayagāminī paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau, rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ attatau… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘sak‍kāyasamudayagāminī paṭipadā, sak‍kāyasamudayagāminī paṭipadā’ti| iti hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati ‘dukkhasamudayagāminī samanupassanā’ti| ayamaivaittha atthau’’|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? idha, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau, na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| na vaidanaṃ  attatau… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ‘sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’ti| iti hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, vuc‍cati ‘dukkhaniraudhagāminī samanupassanā’ti| ayamaivaittha atthau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. anic‍casuttaṃ

45. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau cittaṃ viraj‍jati vimuc‍cati anupādāya āsavaihi| vaidanā anic‍cā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau cittaṃ viraj‍jati vimuc‍cati anupādāya āsavaihi| rūpadhātuyā cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cittaṃ virattaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti anupādāya āsavaihi, vaidanādhātuyā…pai॰… sañ‍ñādhātuyā… saṅkhāradhātuyā… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau cittaṃ virattaṃ vimuttaṃ hauti anupādāya āsavaihi| vimuttattā ṭhitaṃ| ṭhitattā santusitaṃ santussitaṃ (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰ ka॰)| santusitattā na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ  karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaanic‍casuttaṃ

46. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanā anic‍cā… sañ‍ñā anic‍cā… saṅkhārā anic‍cā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau pubbantānudiṭṭhiyau na haunti| pubbantānudiṭṭhīnaṃ asati, aparantānudiṭṭhiyau  na haunti| aparantānudiṭṭhīnaṃ asati, thāmasau thāmasā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) parāmāsau na hauti| thāmasai thāmasā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), thāmasau (ka॰) parāmāsai asati rūpasmiṃ… vaidanāya … sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ cittaṃ viraj‍jati vimuc‍cati anupādāya āsavaihi| vimuttattā ṭhitaṃ| ṭhitattā santusitaṃ| santusitattā na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samanupassanāsuttaṃ

47. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ attānaṃ samanupassamānā samanupassanti, sabbaitai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai samanupassanti, aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? idha, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ  vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ’’|

‘‘iti ayañ‍caiva samanupassanā ‘asmī’ti cassa avigataṃ adhigataṃ (bahūsu) hauti| ‘asmī’ti khau pana, bhikkhavai, avigatai pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ avak‍kanti hauti – cakkhundriyassa sautindriyassa ghānindriyassa jivhindriyassa kāyindriyassa| atthi, bhikkhavai, manau, atthi dhammā, atthi avij‍jādhātu| avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitaina phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa ‘asmī’tipissa hauti; ‘ayamahamasmī’tipissa hauti; ‘bhavissa’ntipissa hauti; ‘na bhavissa’ntipissa hauti; ‘rūpī bhavissa’ntipissa hauti; ‘arūpī bhavissa’ntipissa hauti; ‘sañ‍ñī bhavissa’ntipissa hauti; ‘asañ‍ñī bhavissa’ntipissa hauti; ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’ntipissa hauti’’|

‘‘tiṭṭhantaiva  khau tiṭṭhanti khau pana (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, tatthaiva tathaiva (katthaci) pañ‍cindriyāni| athaittha sutavatau ariyasāvakassa avij‍jā pahīyati, vij‍jā uppaj‍jati| tassa avij‍jāvirāgā vij‍juppādā ‘asmī’tipissa na hauti; ‘ayamahamasmī’tipissa na hauti; ‘bhavissa’nti… ‘na bhavissa’nti… rūpī… arūpī … sañ‍ñī… asañ‍ñī… ‘naivasañ‍ñīnāsañ‍ñī bhavissa’ntipissa na hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. khandhasuttaṃ

48. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, khandhai daisaissāmi, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cakkhandhā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati rūpakkhandhau| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikā vā sukhumā vā…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati saṅkhārakkhandhau| yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cakkhandhā’’|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā? yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sāsavaṃ upādāniyaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati rūpupādānakkhandhau| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā dūrai santikai vā sāsavā upādāniyā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati vaidanupādānakkhandhau| yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yā dūrai santikai vā sāsavā upādāniyā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau| yai kaici saṅkhārā…pai॰… sāsavā upādāniyā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau| yaṃ  kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰…  yaṃ dūrai santikai vā sāsavaṃ upādāniyaṃ, ayaṃ vuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sauṇasuttaṃ

49. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha  khau sauṇau gahapatiputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami  …pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sauṇaṃ gahapatiputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yai hi kaici, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anic‍caina rūpaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa adassanā? anic‍cāya vaidanāya dukkhāya vipariṇāmadhammāya ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa adassanā? anic‍cāya sañ‍ñāya… anic‍caihi saṅkhāraihi dukkhaihi vipariṇāmadhammaihi ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa adassanā? anic‍caina viñ‍ñāṇaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘sadisauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; ‘hīnauhamasmī’ti vā samanupassanti; kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa adassanā?

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anic‍caina rūpaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti; ‘sadisauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti; ‘hīnauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti; kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa dassanā? anic‍cāya vaidanāya… anic‍cāya sañ‍ñāya… anic‍caihi saṅkhāraihi… anic‍caina viñ‍ñāṇaina dukkhaina vipariṇāmadhammaina ‘saiyyauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti; ‘sadisauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti; ‘hīnauhamasmī’tipi na samanupassanti; kimañ‍ñatra yathābhūtassa dassanā?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, sauṇa, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍cā, bhantai’’… ‘‘sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ  vipariṇāmadhammaṃ kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, sauṇa, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ  kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ  vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, sauṇa, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasauṇasuttaṃ

50. aivaṃ mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau sauṇau gahapatiputtau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau sauṇaṃ gahapatiputtaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘yai hi kaici, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā rūpaṃ nappajānanti, rūpasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, rūpaniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; vaidanaṃ nappajānanti, vaidanāsamudayaṃ nappajānanti, vaidanāniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ  nappajānanti; sañ‍ñaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… saṅkhārai nappajānanti, saṅkhārasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ nappajānanti, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nappajānanti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ nappajānanti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ  nappajānanti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānanti| na mai tai, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā rūpaṃ pajānanti, rūpasamudayaṃ pajānanti, rūpaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti; vaidanaṃ pajānanti…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ pajānanti… saṅkhārai pajānanti… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pajānanti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ pajānanti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ pajānanti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānanti| tai ca khau mai, sauṇa, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. nandikkhayasuttaṃ

51. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘anic‍cañ‍ñaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpaṃ anic‍canti passati| sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| anic‍cañ‍ñaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu vaidanaṃ anic‍canti passati| sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā  nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| anic‍caiyaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sañ‍ñaṃ anic‍canti passati…pai॰… anic‍caiyaiva bhikkhavai, bhikkhu saṅkhārai anic‍cāti passati| sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| anic‍cañ‍ñaiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍canti passati| sāssa hauti sammādiṭṭhi| sammā passaṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍catī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyanandikkhayasuttaṃ
