52. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha, rūpānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasi karauntau, rūpānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau rūpasmiṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| vaidanaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha, vaidanānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| vaidanaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasi  karauntau, vaidanānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau vaidanāya nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| sañ‍ñaṃ bhikkhavai… saṅkhārai, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha, saṅkhārānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| saṅkhārai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasi karauntau, saṅkhārānic‍cataṃ yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau saṅkhāraisu nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍cati| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, yaunisau manasi karautha, viñ‍ñāṇānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassatha| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu yaunisau manasi karauntau, viñ‍ñāṇānic‍catañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ samanupassantau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ nibbindati| nandikkhayā rāgakkhayau, rāgakkhayā nandikkhayau| nandirāgakkhayā cittaṃ vimuttaṃ suvimuttanti vuc‍catī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

attadīpavaggau pañ‍camau|

tassuddānaṃ –

attadīpā paṭipadā, dvai ca haunti anic‍catā|

samanupassanā khandhā, dvai sauṇā dvai nandikkhayaina cāti||

mūlapaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa mūlapaṇṇāsakassa vagguddānaṃ –

nakulapitā  anic‍cau ca, bhārau natumhākaina ca|

attadīpaina pañ‍ñāsau, paṭhamau taina pavuc‍catīti||

6. upayavaggau

1. upayasuttaṃ

53. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘upayau upāyau (bahūsu), bhikkhavai, avimuttau, anupayau vimuttau| rūpupayaṃ rūpūpāyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), rūpupāyaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, rūpārammaṇaṃ rūpappatiṭṭhaṃ  nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| vaidanupayaṃ vā…pai॰… sañ‍ñupayaṃ vā…pai॰… saṅkhārupayaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ saṅkhārappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya’’|

‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamañ‍ñatra rūpā añ‍ñatra vaidanāya añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya añ‍ñatra saṅkhāraihi viñ‍ñāṇassa āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā cutiṃ vā upapattiṃ vā vuddhiṃ vā virūḷhiṃ vā vaipul‍laṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘rūpadhātuyā cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahānā vaucchij‍jatārammaṇaṃ patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| vaidanādhātuyā cai, bhikkhavai… sañ‍ñādhātuyā cai bhikkhavai… saṅkhāradhātuyā cai bhikkhavai… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahānā vaucchij‍jatārammaṇaṃ patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| tadappatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ anabhisaṅkhac‍cavimuttaṃ| vimuttattā ṭhitaṃ| ṭhitattā santusitaṃ| santusitattā na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. bījasuttaṃ

54. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimāni, bhikkhavai, bījajātāni| katamāni pañ‍ca? mūlabījaṃ, khandhabījaṃ, aggabījaṃ, phalubījaṃ, bījabījañ‍ñaiva pañ‍camaṃ| imāni cassu, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca bījajātāni akhaṇḍāni apūtikāni avātātapahatāni sārādāni sārādāyīni (katthaci) sukhasayitāni, pathavī paṭhavī (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ca nāssa, āpau ca nāssa; api numāni api nu imāni (sī॰ pī॰), bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca bījajātāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyu’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘imāni cassu, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca bījajātāni akhaṇḍāni…pai॰… sukhasayitāni, pathavī ca assa, āpau ca assa; api numāni, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca bījajātāni vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyyu’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātu, aivaṃ catassau viñ‍ñāṇaṭṭhitiyau daṭṭhabbā| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, āpaudhātu, aivaṃ nandirāgau daṭṭhabbau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ca bījajātāni, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sāhāraṃ daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

‘‘rūpupayaṃ, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, rūpārammaṇaṃ rūpappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| vaidanupayaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya…pai॰… sañ‍ñupayaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya…pai॰… saṅkhārupayaṃ vā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ saṅkhārappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamañ‍ñatra rūpā añ‍ñatra vaidanāya añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya  añ‍ñatra saṅkhāraihi viñ‍ñāṇassa āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā cutiṃ vā upapattiṃ vā vuddhiṃ vā virūḷhiṃ vā vaipul‍laṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘rūpadhātuyā caiva, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahānā vaucchij‍jatārammaṇaṃ patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| vaidanādhātuyā cai… sañ‍ñādhātuyā cai… saṅkhāradhātuyā cai… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā cai, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahānā vaucchij‍jatārammaṇaṃ patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| tadappatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ anabhisaṅkhac‍cavimuttaṃ| vimuttattā ṭhitaṃ| ṭhitattā santusitaṃ| santusitattā na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. udānasuttaṃ

55. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau bhagavā udānaṃ udānaisi – ‘‘‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti – aivaṃ adhimuc‍camānau  bhikkhu chindaiyya aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana, bhantai, ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti – aivaṃ adhimuc‍camānau bhikkhu chindaiyya aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī…pai॰… sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā  attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ|

‘‘sau  anic‍caṃ rūpaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anic‍caṃ vaidanaṃ ‘anic‍cā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anic‍caṃ sañ‍ñaṃ ‘anic‍cā sañ‍ñā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anic‍cai saṅkhārai ‘anic‍cā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘dukkhaṃ rūpaṃ ‘dukkhaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ… dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… dukkhai saṅkhārai… dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘anattaṃ rūpaṃ ‘anattā rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anattaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘anattā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anattaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ ‘anattā sañ‍ñā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anattai saṅkhārai ‘anattā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, anattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anattā viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘saṅkhataṃ rūpaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti, saṅkhataṃ vaidanaṃ… saṅkhataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhatai saṅkhārai… saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| rūpaṃ vibhavissatīti  yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanā vibhavissati… sañ‍ñā vibhavissati… saṅkhārā vibhavissanti… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vibhavissatīti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘sutavā  ca khau, bhikkhu, ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa  kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati|

‘‘sau anic‍caṃ rūpaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| anic‍caṃ vaidanaṃ… anic‍caṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… anic‍cai saṅkhārai… anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| dukkhaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… anattaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… anattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… saṅkhataṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| rūpaṃ vibhavissatīti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vibhavissatīti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘sau  rūpassa vibhavā, vaidanāya vibhavā, sañ‍ñā vibhavā, saṅkhārānaṃ vibhavā, viñ‍ñāṇassa vibhavā, aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti – aivaṃ adhimuc‍camānau bhikkhu chindaiyya aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ adhimuc‍camānau, bhantai, bhikkhu chindaiyya aurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanānī’’ti|

‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, assutavā puthuj‍janau atasitāyai ṭhānai tāsaṃ āpaj‍jati| tāsau haisau haisā (ka॰) bhikkhu assutavatau puthuj‍janassa – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhu, ariyasāvakau atasitāyai ṭhānai  na tāsaṃ āpaj‍jati| na haisau na haisā (ka॰), bhikkhu, tāsau sutavatau ariyasāvakassa – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti| rūpupayaṃ  vā, bhikkhu, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, rūpārammaṇaṃ rūpappatiṭṭhaṃ  nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| vaidanupayaṃ vā, bhikkhu… sañ‍ñupayaṃ vā, bhikkhu… saṅkhārupayaṃ vā, bhikkhu, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tiṭṭhamānaṃ tiṭṭhaiyya, saṅkhārārammaṇaṃ saṅkhārappatiṭṭhaṃ nandūpasaicanaṃ vuddhiṃ virūḷhiṃ vaipul‍laṃ āpaj‍jaiyya|

‘‘yau sau (sabbattha) bhikkhu aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘ahamañ‍ñatra rūpā, añ‍ñatra vaidanāya, añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya, añ‍ñatra saṅkhāraihi viñ‍ñāṇassa āgatiṃ vā gatiṃ vā cutiṃ vā upapattiṃ vā vuddhiṃ vā virūḷhiṃ vā vaipul‍laṃ vā pañ‍ñāpaissāmī’ti, naitaṃ ṭhānaṃ vij‍jati|

‘‘rūpadhātuyā cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahānā vaucchij‍jatārammaṇaṃ patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| vaidanādhātuyā cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau… sañ‍ñādhātuyā cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau… saṅkhāradhātuyā cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau… viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā cai, bhikkhu, bhikkhunau rāgau pahīnau hauti| rāgassa pahānā vaucchij‍jatārammaṇaṃ patiṭṭhā viñ‍ñāṇassa na hauti| tadappatiṭṭhitaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ avirūḷhaṃ anabhisaṅkhārañ‍ca vimuttaṃ| vimuttattā ṭhitaṃ| ṭhitattā santusitaṃ| santusitattā na paritassati| aparitassaṃ pac‍cattañ‍ñaiva parinibbāyati| ‘khīṇā jāti…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti  pajānāti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. upādānaparipavattasuttaṃ

56. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yāvakīvañ‍cāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai catuparivaṭṭaṃ yathābhūtaṃ nābbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, naiva tāvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai samārakai sabrahmakai sassamaṇabrāhmaṇiyā pajāya sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ| yatau ca khvāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai catuparivaṭṭaṃ yathābhūtaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, athāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, sadaivakai laukai…pai॰… sadaivamanussāya anuttaraṃ sammāsambaudhiṃ abhisambuddhauti pac‍cañ‍ñāsiṃ’’|

‘‘kathañ‍ca catuparivaṭṭaṃ? rūpaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, rūpasamudayaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, rūpaniraudhaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ; vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abbhañ‍ñāsiṃ|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ? cattārau ca mahābhūtā catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ| āhārasamudayā rūpasamudayau; āhāraniraudhā rūpaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau rūpaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā  vā aivaṃ rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… aivaṃ rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, rūpassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttā tai suvimuttā| yai suvimuttā tai kaivalinau| yai kaivalinau vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, vaidanākāyā  – cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā, sautasamphassajā vaidanā, ghānasamphassajā vaidanā, jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā, kāyasamphassajā vaidanā, manausamphassajā vaidanā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau; phassaniraudhā vaidanāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ vaidanaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāsamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya vaidanāya nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ vaidanaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñākāyā – rūpasañ‍ñā, saddasañ‍ñā, gandhasañ‍ñā, rasasañ‍ñā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā| phassasamudayā sañ‍ñāsamudayau; phassaniraudhā sañ‍ñāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi…pai॰… vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, caitanākāyā – rūpasañ‍caitanā, saddasañ‍caitanā, gandhasañ‍caitanā, rasasañ‍caitanā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā, dhammasañ‍caitanā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| phassasamudayā saṅkhārasamudayau; phassaniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ saṅkhārai abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ  paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya saṅkhārānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya  niraudhāya  paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ saṅkhārai abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ  abhiñ‍ñāya saṅkhārānaṃ nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttā, tai suvimuttā| yai suvimuttā, tai kaivalinau| yai kaivalinau vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? chayimai, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇakāyā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| nāmarūpasamudayā viñ‍ñāṇasamudayau; nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ  abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttā, tai suvimuttā| yai suvimuttā, tai kaivalinau| yai kaivalinau vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. sattaṭṭhānasuttaṃ

57. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sattaṭṭhānakusalau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tividhūpaparikkhī imasmiṃ dhammavinayai kaivalī vusitavā uttamapurisauti vuc‍cati| kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sattaṭṭhānakusalau hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu rūpaṃ pajānāti, rūpasamudayaṃ pajānāti, rūpaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ  paṭipadaṃ pajānāti; rūpassa assādaṃ pajānāti, rūpassa ādīnavaṃ pajānāti, rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ pajānāti; vaidanaṃ pajānāti … sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti; viñ‍ñāṇassa assādaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ? cattārau ca mahābhūtā, catun‍nañ‍ca mahābhūtānaṃ upādāya rūpaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ| āhārasamudayā rūpasamudayau; āhāraniraudhā rūpaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau  aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau rūpaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yaṃ rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ rūpassa assādau| yaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ – ayaṃ rūpassa ādīnavau| yau rūpasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya; aivaṃ rūpassa assādaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpassa ādīnavaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ rūpaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya; aivaṃ rūpassa assādaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpassa ādīnavaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ  abhiñ‍ñāya rūpassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttā, tai suvimuttā| yai suvimuttā, tai kaivalinau| yai kaivalinau vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, vaidanākāyā – cakkhusamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰…  manausamphassajā vaidanā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, vaidanā| phassasamudayā vaidanāsamudayau; phassaniraudhā  vaidanāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau vaidanāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yaṃ  vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ vaidanāya assādau| yā vaidanā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā – ayaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavau| yau vaidanāya chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘yai hi, kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ vaidanaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāsamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya; aivaṃ vaidanāya assādaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāya ādīnavaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ vaidanāya nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya vaidanāya nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ vaidanaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñākāyā – rūpasañ‍ñā, saddasañ‍ñā, gandhasañ‍ñā, rasasañ‍ñā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā, dhammasañ‍ñā| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñā| phassasamudayā sañ‍ñāsamudayau; phassaniraudhā sañ‍ñāniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi…pai॰… vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamai  ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā? chayimai, bhikkhavai, caitanākāyā – rūpasañ‍caitanā, saddasañ‍caitanā, gandhasañ‍caitanā, rasasañ‍caitanā, phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā, dhammasañ‍caitanā| imai vuc‍canti bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā| phassasamudayā saṅkhārasamudayau; phassaniraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau saṅkhāraniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yaṃ saṅkhārai paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ assādau| yai saṅkhārā anic‍cā dukkhā vipariṇāmadhammā – ayaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ ādīnavau| yau saṅkhāraisu chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ saṅkhārai abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhārasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya…pai॰… saṅkhārānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya  paṭipan‍nā tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti…pai॰… vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya|

‘‘katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ? chayimai, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇakāyā – cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ| nāmarūpasamudayā viñ‍ñāṇasamudayau; nāmarūpaniraudhā viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhau| ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi|

‘‘yaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau| yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ|

‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya; aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa assādaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nā, tai suppaṭipan‍nā| yai suppaṭipan‍nā, tai imasmiṃ dhammavinayai gādhanti|

‘‘yai  ca khau kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya; aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa assādaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya, aivaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇaṃ abhiñ‍ñāya viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttā, tai suvimuttā| yai suvimuttā, tai kaivalinau| yai kaivalinau vaṭṭaṃ taisaṃ natthi pañ‍ñāpanāya| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu sattaṭṭhānakusalau hauti|

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tividhūpaparikkhī hauti? idha, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu dhātusau upaparikkhati, āyatanasau upaparikkhati, paṭic‍casamuppādasau upaparikkhati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tividhūpaparikkhī hauti| sattaṭṭhānakusalau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu tividhūpaparikkhī, imasmiṃ dhammavinayai kaivalī vusitavā ‘uttamapurisau’ti vuc‍catī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sammāsambuddhasuttaṃ

58. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau rūpassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau sammāsambuddhauti vuc‍cati| bhikkhupi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāvimuttau rūpassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau pañ‍ñāvimuttauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau vaidanāya nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau sammāsambuddhauti vuc‍cati| bhikkhupi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāvimuttau vaidanāya nibbidā…pai॰… pañ‍ñāvimuttauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā  anupādā vimuttau sammāsambuddhauti vuc‍cati| bhikkhupi, bhikkhavai, pañ‍ñāvimuttau viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādā vimuttau pañ‍ñāvimuttauti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tatra  khau, bhikkhavai, kau visaisau, kau adhippayāsau adhippāyau (sī॰), adhippāyasau (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰), kiṃ nānākaraṇaṃ, tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pañ‍ñāvimuttaina bhikkhunā’’ti? ‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantañ‍ñaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘tathāgatau, bhikkhavai, arahaṃ sammāsambuddhau anuppan‍nassa maggassa uppādaitā, asañ‍jātassa maggassa sañ‍janaitā sañ‍jānaitā (syā॰ kaṃ॰), anakkhātassa maggassa akkhātā maggañ‍ñū, maggavidū, maggakauvidau; maggānugā ca, bhikkhavai, aitarahi sāvakā viharanti pacchāsaman‍nāgatā| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, visaisau, ayaṃ adhippayāsau, idaṃ nānākaraṇaṃ tathāgatassa arahatau sammāsambuddhassa pañ‍ñāvimuttaina bhikkhunā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anattalakkhaṇasuttaṃ

59. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā bārāṇasiyaṃ viharati isipatanai migadāyai| tatra khau bhagavā pañ‍cavaggiyai bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘bhikkhavau’’ti| ‘‘bhadantai’’ti tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā| rūpañ‍ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ rūpaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha ca rūpai – ‘aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ anattā, tasmā rūpaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati rūpai – ‘aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai rūpaṃ mā ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘vaidanā anattā| vaidanā ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ vaidanā ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha  ca vaidanāya – ‘aivaṃ mai vaidanā hautu, aivaṃ mai vaidanā mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, vaidanā anattā, tasmā vaidanā ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati vaidanāya – ‘aivaṃ mai vaidanā hautu, aivaṃ mai vaidanā mā ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘sañ‍ñā  anattā…pai॰… saṅkhārā anattā| saṅkhārā ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissaṃsu, nayidaṃ saṅkhārā ābādhāya saṃvattaiyyuṃ, labbhaitha ca saṅkhāraisu – ‘aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā hauntu, aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā mā ahaisu’nti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārā anattā, tasmā saṅkhārā ābādhāya saṃvattanti, na ca labbhati saṅkhāraisu – ‘aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā hauntu, aivaṃ mai saṅkhārā mā ahaisu’’’nti|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca hidaṃ, bhikkhavai, attā abhavissa, nayidaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattaiyya, labbhaitha ca viñ‍ñāṇai – ‘aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mā ahausī’ti| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā, tasmā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ābādhāya saṃvattati, na ca labbhati viñ‍ñāṇai – ‘aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ hautu, aivaṃ mai viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mā ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai  attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ  sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattā vā bahiddhā vā…pai॰… yā dūrai santikai vā, sabbā  vaidanā – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… yai kaici saṅkhārā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā…pai॰… yai dūrai santikai vā, sabbai saṅkhārā – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā  vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā pañ‍cavaggiyā bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanduṃ abhinandunti (ka॰)|

imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ bhañ‍ñamānai pañ‍cavaggiyānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsūti| sattamaṃ|

8. mahālisuttaṃ

60. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| atha khau mahāli licchavi yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami  …pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau mahāli licchavi bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘pūraṇau, bhantai, kassapau aivamāha – ‘natthi haitu natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; ahaitū  appac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| natthi haitu natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā visujjhantī’ti| idha, bhagavā kimāhā’’ti?

‘‘atthi, mahāli, haitu atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| atthi, mahāli, haitu, atthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhantī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, haitu katamau pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; kathaṃ sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissantī’’ti?

‘‘rūpañ‍ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantadukkhaṃ abhavissa dukkhānupatitaṃ dukkhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ sukhaina, nayidaṃ sattā rūpasmiṃ  sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, rūpaṃ sukhaṃ sukhānupatitaṃ sukhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ dukkhaina, tasmā sattā rūpasmiṃ sāraj‍janti; sārāgā saṃyuj‍janti; saṃyaugā saṃkilissanti| ayaṃ khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya; aivaṃ sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti|

‘‘vaidanā ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantadukkhā abhavissa dukkhānupatitā dukkhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā sukhaina, nayidaṃ sattā vaidanāya sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, vaidanā sukhā sukhānupatitā sukhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā dukkhaina, tasmā sattā vaidanāya sāraj‍janti; sārāgā saṃyuj‍janti; saṃyaugā saṃkilissanti| ayampi khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya| aivampi sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti|

‘‘sañ‍ñā ca hidaṃ, mahāli…pai॰… saṅkhārā ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantadukkhā abhavissaṃsu dukkhānupatitā dukkhāvak‍kantā anavak‍kantā sukhaina, nayidaṃ sattā  saṅkhāraisu sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, saṅkhārā sukhā sukhānupatitā sukhāvak‍kantā  anavak‍kantā dukkhaina, tasmā sattā saṅkhāraisu sāraj‍janti; sārāgā saṃyuj‍janti; saṃyaugā saṃkilissanti| ayampi khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya| aivampi sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti|

‘‘viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca  hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantadukkhaṃ abhavissa dukkhānupatitaṃ dukkhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ sukhaina, nayidaṃ sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ sāraj‍jaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ sukhaṃ sukhānupatitaṃ sukhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ dukkhaina, tasmā sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ sāraj‍janti; sārāgā saṃyuj‍janti; saṃyaugā saṃkilissanti| ayampi khau, mahāli, haitu ayaṃ pac‍cayau sattānaṃ saṃkilaisāya| aivampi sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissantī’’ti|

‘‘katamau pana, bhantai, haitu katamau pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā; kathaṃ sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhantī’’ti? ‘‘rūpañ‍ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantasukhaṃ abhavissa sukhānupatitaṃ sukhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ dukkhaina, nayidaṃ sattā rūpasmiṃ nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, rūpaṃ dukkhaṃ dukkhānupatitaṃ dukkhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ sukhaina, tasmā sattā rūpasmiṃ nibbindanti; nibbindaṃ viraj‍janti; virāgā visujjhanti| ayaṃ khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, sattānaṃ visuddhiyā| aivaṃ sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti’’|

‘‘vaidanā ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantasukhā abhavissa…pai॰… sañ‍ñā ca hidaṃ, mahāli…pai॰… saṅkhārā ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantasukhā abhavissaṃsu…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ca hidaṃ, mahāli, aikantasukhaṃ abhavissa sukhānupatitaṃ sukhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ dukkhaina, nayidaṃ sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ nibbindaiyyuṃ| yasmā ca khau, mahāli, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ dukkhānupatitaṃ dukkhāvak‍kantaṃ anavak‍kantaṃ sukhaina, tasmā sattā viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ nibbindanti; nibbindaṃ viraj‍janti; virāgā visujjhanti| ayaṃ  khau, mahāli, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, sattānaṃ visuddhiyā| aivampi sahaitū sappac‍cayā sattā visujjhantī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. ādittasuttaṃ

61. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, ādittaṃ, vaidanā ādittā, sañ‍ñā ādittā, saṅkhārā  ādittā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ādittaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi… sañ‍ñāyapi… saṅkhāraisupi… viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi  nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. niruttipathasuttaṃ

62. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, niruttipathā adhivacanapathā pañ‍ñattipathā asaṅkiṇṇā asaṅkiṇṇapubbā, na saṅkīyanti, na saṅkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhā samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| katamai tayau? yaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ ‘ahausī’ti tassa saṅkhā, ‘ahausī’ti tassa samañ‍ñā, ‘ahausī’ti tassa pañ‍ñatti; na tassa saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na tassa saṅkhā ‘bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yā vaidanā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā ‘ahausī’ti tassā saṅkhā, ‘ahausī’ti tassā samañ‍ñā, ‘ahausī’ti tassā pañ‍ñatti; na tassā saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na tassā saṅkhā ‘bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā ‘ahaisu’nti taisaṃ saṅkhā, ‘ahaisu’nti taisaṃ samañ‍ñā, ‘ahaisu’nti taisaṃ pañ‍ñatti; na taisaṃ saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na taisaṃ saṅkhā ‘bhavissantī’’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītaṃ niruddhaṃ vipariṇataṃ, ‘ahausī’ti tassa saṅkhā, ‘ahausī’ti tassa samañ‍ñā, ‘ahausī’ti tassa pañ‍ñatti; na tassa saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na  tassa saṅkhā ‘bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ ajātaṃ apātubhūtaṃ, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassa saṅkhā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassa samañ‍ñā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassa pañ‍ñatti; na tassa saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na tassa saṅkhā ‘ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘yā  vaidanā ajātā apātubhūtā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassā saṅkhā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassā samañ‍ñā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassā pañ‍ñatti; na tassā saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na tassā saṅkhā ‘ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā ajātā apātubhūtā, ‘bhavissantī’ti taisaṃ saṅkhā, ‘bhavissantī’ti taisaṃ samañ‍ñā, ‘bhavissantī’ti taisaṃ pañ‍ñatti; na taisaṃ saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na taisaṃ saṅkhā ‘ahaisu’’’nti|

‘‘yaṃ  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ajātaṃ apātubhūtaṃ, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassa saṅkhā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassa samañ‍ñā, ‘bhavissatī’ti tassa pañ‍ñatti; na tassa saṅkhā ‘atthī’ti, na tassa saṅkhā ‘ahausī’’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ jātaṃ pātubhūtaṃ, ‘atthī’ti tassa saṅkhā, ‘atthī’ti tassa samañ‍ñā, ‘atthī’ti tassa pañ‍ñatti; na tassa saṅkhā ‘ahausī’ti, na tassa saṅkhā ‘bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yā vaidanā jātā pātubhūtā, ‘atthī’ti tassā saṅkhā, ‘atthī’ti tassā samañ‍ñā, ‘atthī’ti tassā pañ‍ñatti; na tassā saṅkhā ‘ahausī’ti, na tassā saṅkhā ‘bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘yā sañ‍ñā… yai saṅkhārā jātā pātubhūtā, ‘atthī’ti taisaṃ saṅkhā, ‘atthī’ti taisaṃ samañ‍ñā, ‘atthī’ti taisaṃ pañ‍ñatti; na taisaṃ saṅkhā ‘ahaisu’nti, na taisaṃ  saṅkhā, ‘bhavissantī’’’ti|

‘‘yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ jātaṃ pātubhūtaṃ, ‘atthī’ti tassa saṅkhā, ‘atthī’ti tassa samañ‍ñā, ‘atthī’ti tassa pañ‍ñatti; na tassa saṅkhā ‘ahausī’ti, na tassa saṅkhā ‘bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘imai khau, bhikkhavai, tayau niruttipathā adhivacanapathā pañ‍ñattipathā asaṅkiṇṇā asaṅkiṇṇapubbā, na saṅkīyanti, na  saṅkīyissanti, appaṭikuṭṭhā samaṇaihi brāhmaṇaihi viñ‍ñūhi| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, ahaisuṃ uk‍kalā vassabhañ‍ñā auk‍kalā vayabhiñ‍ñā (ma॰ ni॰ 3.343) ahaitukavādā akiriyavādā natthikavādā, taipimai tayau niruttipathai adhivacanapathai pañ‍ñattipathai na garahitabbaṃ nappaṭik‍kausitabbaṃ amañ‍ñiṃsu| taṃ kissa haitu? nindāghaṭṭanabyārausaupārambhabhayā’’ti nindābyārausaupārambhabhayāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ma॰ ni॰ 3.343|

upayavaggau chaṭṭhau|

tassuddānaṃ –

upayau bījaṃ udānaṃ, upādānaparivattaṃ|

sattaṭṭhānañ‍ca sambuddhau, pañ‍camahāli ādittā||

vaggau niruttipathaina cāti||

7. arahantavaggau

1. upādiyamānasuttaṃ

63. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘upādiyamānau khau, bhikkhu, baddhau mārassa; anupādiyamānau muttau pāpimatau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ  bhagavā, añ‍ñātaṃ sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, upādiyamānau baddhau mārassa; anupādiyamānau muttau pāpimatau| vaidanaṃ upādiyamānau baddhau mārassa; anupādiyamānau muttau pāpimatau| sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai … viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyamānau baddhau mārassa; anupādiyamānau muttau pāpimatau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, upādiyamānau baddhau mārassa; anupādiyamānau muttau pāpimatau| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādiyamānau baddhau mārassa; anupādiyamānau muttau pāpimatau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti|

atha khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā pak‍kāmi| atha khau sau bhikkhu aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharantau nacirassaiva – yassatthāya kulaputtā sammadaiva agārasmā anagāriyaṃ pabbajanti tadanuttaraṃ – brahmacariyapariyausānaṃ diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharati| ‘‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ  karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’’ti abbhañ‍ñāsi| añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. mañ‍ñamānasuttaṃ

64. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu…pai॰… ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘mañ‍ñamānau khau, bhikkhu, baddhau mārassa; amañ‍ñamānau muttau pāpimatau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ bhagavā, añ‍ñātaṃ sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, mañ‍ñamānau baddhau mārassa; amañ‍ñamānau muttau pāpimatau| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mañ‍ñamānau baddhau mārassa; amañ‍ñamānau muttau pāpimatau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, mañ‍ñamānau baddhau mārassa; amañ‍ñamānau muttau pāpimatau| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mañ‍ñamānau baddhau mārassa; amañ‍ñamānau muttau pāpimatau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. abhinandamānasuttaṃ

65. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina…pai॰… pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘abhinandamānau khau, bhikkhu, baddhau mārassa; anabhinandamānau muttau pāpimatau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ bhagavā, añ‍ñātaṃ sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā  kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, abhinandamānau baddhau mārassa; anabhinandamānau muttau pāpimatau| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandamānau baddhau mārassa; anabhinandamānau muttau pāpimatau| imassa  khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, abhinandamānau baddhau mārassa; anabhinandamānau muttau pāpimatau| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai … viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandamānau baddhau mārassa; anabhinandamānau muttau pāpimatau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. anic‍casuttaṃ

66. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu…pai॰… ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, anic‍caṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anic‍caṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā anic‍cā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra khau tai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰…  añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| catutthaṃ|

5. dukkhasuttaṃ

67. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu…pai॰… ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, dukkhaṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau bhikkhu, dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. anattasuttaṃ

68. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu…pai॰… ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhu, anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ, sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā  kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, anattā; tatra mai  chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰…  añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anattaniyasuttaṃ

69. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu…pai॰… viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anattaniyaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ, sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, anattaniyaṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattaniyaṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, anattaniyaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā  … sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattaniyaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| sattamaṃ|

8. rajanīyasaṇṭhitasuttaṃ

70. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā…pai॰… viharaiyya’’nti| ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, rajanīyasaṇṭhitaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau’’ti| ‘‘añ‍ñātaṃ, bhagavā; añ‍ñātaṃ, sugatā’’ti|

‘‘yathā  kathaṃ pana tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsī’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, bhantai, rajanīyasaṇṭhitaṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ rajanīyasaṇṭhitaṃ; tatra mai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khvāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāmī’’ti|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu bhikkhu! sādhu khau tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa vitthāraina atthaṃ ājānāsi| rūpaṃ khau, bhikkhu, rajanīyasaṇṭhitaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ rajanīyasaṇṭhitaṃ; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau| imassa khau, bhikkhu, mayā saṃkhittaina bhāsitassa aivaṃ vitthāraina atthau daṭṭhabbau’’ti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca  pana sau bhikkhu arahataṃ ahausīti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. rādhasuttaṃ

71. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā rādhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rādha, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, rādha, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti…pai॰…  añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā rādhau arahataṃ ahausīti| navamaṃ|

10. surādhasuttaṃ

72. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā surādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti, vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ  kiñ‍ci, surādha, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādāvimuttau hauti| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ  kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā  sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādāvimuttau hauti| aivaṃ khau, surādha, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai, bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti…pai॰… añ‍ñatarau ca panāyasmā surādhau arahataṃ ahausīti| dasamaṃ|

arahantavaggau sattamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

upādiyamañ‍ñamānā, athābhinandamānau ca|

anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ anattā ca, anattanīyaṃ rajanīyasaṇṭhitaṃ|

rādhasurādhaina tai dasāti||

8. khaj‍janīyavaggau

1. assādasuttaṃ

73. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau rūpassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau rūpassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanā … sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. samudayasuttaṃ

74. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘assutavā, bhikkhavai, puthuj‍janau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca  nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyasamudayasuttaṃ

75. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sutavā, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca  assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. arahantasuttaṃ

76. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi… sañ‍ñāyapi… saṅkhāraisupi… viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattāvāsā, yāvatā bhavaggaṃ, aitai aggā, aitai saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ yadidaṃ arahantau’’ti|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘sukhinau vata arahantau, taṇhā taisaṃ na vij‍jati|

asmimānau samucchin‍nau, mauhajālaṃ padālitaṃ||

‘‘anaijaṃ  tai anuppattā, cittaṃ taisaṃ anāvilaṃ|

laukai anupalittā tai, brahmabhūtā anāsavā||

‘‘pañ‍cakkhandhai  pariñ‍ñāya, satta saddhammagaucarā|

pasaṃsiyā sappurisā, puttā buddhassa aurasā||

‘‘sattaratanasampan‍nā, tīsu sikkhāsu sikkhitā|

anuvicaranti mahāvīrā, pahīnabhayabhairavā||

‘‘dasahaṅgaihi sampan‍nā, mahānāgā samāhitā|

aitai khau saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ, taṇhā taisaṃ na vij‍jati||

‘‘asaikhañāṇamuppan‍naṃ, antimauyaṃ antimassa (ka॰) samussayau|

yau sārau brahmacariyassa, tasmiṃ aparapac‍cayā||

‘‘vidhāsu  na vikampanti, vippamuttā punabbhavā|

dantabhūmimanuppattā, tai laukai vijitāvinau||

‘‘uddhaṃ tiriyaṃ apācīnaṃ, nandī taisaṃ na vij‍jati|

nadanti tai sīhanādaṃ, buddhā laukai anuttarā’’ti|| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaarahantasuttaṃ

77. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ tadanattā; yadanattā taṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti…pai॰… aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi… sañ‍ñāyapi… saṅkhāraisupi… viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi  nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā  vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti| yāvatā, bhikkhavai, sattāvāsā, yāvatā bhavaggaṃ, aitai aggā, aitai saiṭṭhā laukasmiṃ yadidaṃ arahantau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sīhasuttaṃ

78. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sīhau, bhikkhavai, migarājā sāyanhasamayaṃ āsayā nikkhamati; āsayā nikkhamitvā vijambhati; vijambhitvā samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaiti; samantā catuddisā anuvilaukaitvā tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ nadati; tikkhattuṃ sīhanādaṃ naditvā gaucarāya pak‍kamati| yai  hi kaici, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā sīhassa migarañ‍ñau nadatau saddaṃ suṇanti; yaibhuyyaina bhayaṃ saṃvaigaṃ santāsaṃ āpaj‍janti; bilaṃ bilāsayā pavisanti; dakaṃ dakāsayā pavisanti; vanaṃ vanāsayā pavisanti; ākāsaṃ pakkhinau bhajanti| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, rañ‍ñau nāgā gāmanigamarājadhānīsu, daḷhaihi varattaihi baddhā, taipi tāni bandhanāni sañchinditvā sampadālaitvā bhītā muttakarīsaṃ cajamānā maucantā (pī॰ ka॰), yaina vā taina vā palāyanti| aivaṃ mahiddhikau khau, bhikkhavai, sīhau migarājā tiracchānagatānaṃ pāṇānaṃ, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau, aivaṃ mahānubhāvau’’|

‘‘aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yadā tathāgatau laukai uppaj‍jati arahaṃ  sammāsambuddhau vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍nau sugatau laukavidū anuttarau purisadammasārathi satthā daivamanussānaṃ buddhau bhagavā| sau dhammaṃ daisaiti – ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| yaipi tai, bhikkhavai, daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā uc‍caisu vimānaisu ciraṭṭhitikā taipi tathāgatassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā yaibhuyyaina bhayaṃ saṃvaigaṃ santāsaṃ āpaj‍janti – ‘anic‍cāva kira, bhau, mayaṃ samānā nic‍camhāti amañ‍ñimha| addhuvāva kira, bhau, mayaṃ samānā dhuvamhāti amañ‍ñimha| asassatāva kira, bhau, mayaṃ samānā sassatamhāti amañ‍ñimha| mayampi kira, bhau, anic‍cā addhuvā asassatā sak‍kāyapariyāpan‍nā’ti| aivaṃ mahiddhikau khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau  sadaivakassa laukassa, aivaṃ mahaisakkhau, aivaṃ mahānubhāvau’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā…pai॰… aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘yadā  buddhau abhiñ‍ñāya, dhammacak‍kaṃ pavattayi|

sadaivakassa laukassa, satthā appaṭipuggalau||

‘‘sak‍kāyañ‍ca  niraudhañ‍ca, sak‍kāyassa ca sambhavaṃ|

ariyañ‍caṭṭhaṅgikaṃ maggaṃ, dukkhūpasamagāminaṃ||

‘‘yaipi dīghāyukā daivā, vaṇṇavantau yasassinau|

bhītā santāsamāpāduṃ, sīhassaivitarai migā||

avītivattā  sak‍kāyaṃ, anic‍cā kira bhau mayaṃ|

sutvā arahatau vākyaṃ, vippamuttassa tādinau’’ti|| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. khaj‍janīyasuttaṃ

79. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā anaikavihitaṃ pubbainivāsaṃ anussaramānā anussaranti sabbaitai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anussaranti aitaisaṃ vā añ‍ñataraṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? ‘aivaṃrūpau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, anussaramānau rūpaṃyaiva anussarati| ‘aivaṃvaidanau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, anussaramānau vaidanaṃyaiva anussarati| ‘aivaṃsañ‍ñau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti… ‘aivaṃsaṅkhārau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti… ‘aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau ahausiṃ atītamaddhāna’nti – iti vā hi, bhikkhavai, anussaramānau viñ‍ñāṇamaiva anussarati’’|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ vadaitha? ruppatīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘rūpa’nti vuc‍cati| kaina ruppati? sītainapi ruppati, uṇhainapi ruppati, jighacchāyapi ruppati, pipāsāyapi  ruppati, ḍaṃsamakasavātātapasarīsapasamphassainapi … siriṃsapasamphassainapi (sī॰ pī॰) ruppati| ruppatīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘rūpa’nti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, vaidanaṃ vadaitha? vaidayatīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘vaidanā’ti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca vaidayati? sukhampi vaidayati, dukkhampi vaidayati, adukkhamasukhampi  vaidayati| vaidayatīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘vaidanā’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, sañ‍ñaṃ vadaitha? sañ‍jānātīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā  ‘sañ‍ñā’ti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca sañ‍jānāti? nīlampi sañ‍jānāti, pītakampi sañ‍jānāti, lauhitakampi sañ‍jānāti, audātampi sañ‍jānāti| sañ‍jānātīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘sañ‍ñā’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, saṅkhārai vadaitha? saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharauntīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘saṅkhārā’ti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharaunti? rūpaṃ rūpattāya rūpatthāya (ka॰) saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharaunti, vaidanaṃ vaidanattāya saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharaunti, sañ‍ñaṃ sañ‍ñattāya saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharaunti, saṅkhārai saṅkhārattāya saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharaunti, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ viñ‍ñāṇattāya saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharaunti| saṅkhatamabhisaṅkharauntīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘saṅkhārā’ti vuc‍cati|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vadaitha? vijānātīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘viñ‍ñāṇa’nti vuc‍cati| kiñ‍ca vijānāti? ambilampi vijānāti, tittakampi vijānāti, kaṭukampi vijānāti, madhurampi vijānāti, khārikampi vijānāti, akhārikampi vijānāti, lauṇikampi vijānāti, alauṇikampi vijānāti| vijānātīti khau, bhikkhavai, tasmā ‘viñ‍ñāṇa’nti vuc‍cati|

‘‘tatra, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi rūpaina khaj‍jāmi| atītampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva rūpaina khaj‍jiṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍naina rūpaina khaj‍jāmi| ahañ‍caiva khau pana anāgataṃ rūpaṃ abhinandaiyyaṃ, anāgatampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva rūpaina khaj‍jaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍naina rūpaina khaj‍jāmī’ti| sau iti  paṭisaṅkhāya atītasmiṃ rūpasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ rūpaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa rūpassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi vaidanāya khaj‍jāmi| atītampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva vaidanāya khaj‍jiṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍nāya  vaidanāya khaj‍jāmi| ahañ‍caiva khau pana anāgataṃ vaidanaṃ abhinandaiyyaṃ; anāgatampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva vaidanāya khaj‍jaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍nāya vaidanāya khaj‍jāmī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya atītāya vaidanāya anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ vaidanaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nāya vaidanāya nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi sañ‍ñāya khaj‍jāmi…pai॰… ahaṃ khau aitarahi saṅkhāraihi khaj‍jāmi| atītampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva saṅkhāraihi khaj‍jiṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍naihi saṅkhāraihi khaj‍jāmīti| ahañ‍caiva khau pana anāgatai saṅkhārai abhinandaiyyaṃ; anāgatampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva saṅkhāraihi khaj‍jaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍naihi saṅkhāraihi khaj‍jāmī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya atītaisu saṅkhāraisu anapaikkhau hauti; anāgatai saṅkhārai nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nānaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘‘ahaṃ khau aitarahi viñ‍ñāṇaina khaj‍jāmi| atītampi addhānaṃ aivamaiva viñ‍ñāṇaina khaj‍jiṃ, saiyyathāpi aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍naina viñ‍ñāṇaina khaj‍jāmi| ahañ‍caiva khau pana anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinandaiyyaṃ; anāgatampāhaṃ addhānaṃ aivamaiva viñ‍ñāṇaina khaj‍jaiyyaṃ, saiyyathāpi  aitarahi pac‍cuppan‍naina viñ‍ñāṇaina khaj‍jāmī’ti| sau iti paṭisaṅkhāya atītasmiṃ viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ anapaikkhau hauti; anāgataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nābhinandati; pac‍cuppan‍nassa viñ‍ñāṇassa nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā … sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na  maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ’’|

‘‘ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau apacināti, nau ācināti; pajahati nau (sī॰), na upādiyati; visinaiti nau (sī॰), na ussinaiti; vidhūpaiti nau (sī॰), na sandhūpaiti| kiñ‍ca apacināti, nau ācināti? rūpaṃ apacināti, nau ācināti; vaidanaṃ … sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ apacināti, nau ācināti| kiñ‍ca pajahati, na upādiyati? rūpaṃ pajahati, na upādiyati; vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pajahati, na upādiyati| kiñ‍ca  visinaiti, na ussinaiti? rūpaṃ visinaiti, na ussinaiti; vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ visinaiti, na ussinaiti| kiñ‍ca vidhūpaiti, na sandhūpaiti? rūpaṃ vidhūpaiti, na sandhūpaiti; vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vidhūpaiti, na sandhūpaiti|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi… sañ‍ñāyapi… saṅkhāraisupi… viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānāti|

‘‘ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu naivācināti na apacināti, apacinitvā ṭhitau naiva pajahati na upādiyati, pajahitvā ṭhitau naiva visinaiti na ussinaiti, visinaitvā ṭhitau naiva vidhūpaiti na sandhūpaiti| vidhūpaitvā ṭhitau kiñ‍ca naivācināti na apacināti? apacinitvā ṭhitau rūpaṃ  naivācināti na apacināti; apacinitvā ṭhitau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ naivācināti na apacināti| apacinitvā ṭhitau kiñ‍ca naiva pajahati na upādiyati? pajahitvā ṭhitau rūpaṃ naiva pajahati na upādiyati; pajahitvā ṭhitau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ naiva pajahati na upādiyati| pajahitvā ṭhitau kiñ‍ca naiva visinaiti na ussinaiti? visinaitvā ṭhitau rūpaṃ naiva visinaiti na ussinaiti; visinaitvā ṭhitau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ naiva visinaiti na ussinaiti| visinaitvā ṭhitau kiñ‍ca naiva vidhūpaiti na sandhūpaiti? vidhūpaitvā ṭhitau rūpaṃ naiva vidhūpaiti na sandhūpaiti; vidhūpaitvā ṭhitau vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ naiva vidhūpaiti na sandhūpaiti| vidhūpaitvā ṭhitau aivaṃvimuttacittaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhuṃ saindā daivā sabrahmakā sapajāpatikā ārakāva namassanti –

‘‘namau  tai purisājañ‍ña, namau tai purisuttama|

yassa tai nābhijānāma, yampi nissāya jhāyasī’’ti|| sattamaṃ|

8. piṇḍaulyasuttaṃ

80. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sak‍kaisu viharati kapilavatthusmiṃ nigraudhārāmai| atha khau bhagavā kismiñ‍cidaiva pakaraṇai bhikkhusaṅghaṃ paṇāmaitvā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kapilavatthuṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kapilavatthusmiṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina mahāvanaṃ tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| mahāvanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā bailuvalaṭṭhikāya mūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi|

atha khau bhagavatau rahaugatassa paṭisal‍līnassa aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘mayā khau bhikkhusaṅghau pabāḷhau| santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ| taisaṃ mamaṃ apassantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi nāma vacchassa  taruṇassa mātaraṃ apassantassa siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau, aivamaiva santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ taisaṃ mamaṃ apassantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi nāma bījānaṃ taruṇānaṃ udakaṃ alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau, aivamaiva santaittha…pai॰… taisaṃ mamaṃ alabhantānaṃ dassanāya siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yathaiva mayā pubbai bhikkhusaṅghau anuggahitau, aivamaiva aitarahi anuggaṇhaiyyaṃ bhikkhusaṅgha’’nti|

atha khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya – saiyyathāpi nāma balavā purisau samiñ‍jitaṃ sammiñ‍jitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) vā bāhaṃ pasāraiyya pasāritaṃ vā bāhaṃ samiñ‍jaiyya aivamaiva – brahmalaukai antarahitau bhagavatau puratau pāturahausi| atha  khau brahmā sahampati aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, bhagavā; aivamaitaṃ, sugata! bhagavatau, bhantai, bhikkhusaṅghau pabāḷhau| santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ| taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ apassantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi nāma vacchassa taruṇassa mātaraṃ apassantassa siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau, aivamaiva santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā  adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ apassantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau| saiyyathāpi nāma bījānaṃ taruṇānaṃ udakaṃ alabhantānaṃ siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau, aivamaiva santaittha bhikkhū navā acirapabbajitā adhunāgatā imaṃ dhammavinayaṃ, taisaṃ bhagavantaṃ alabhantānaṃ dassanāya siyā añ‍ñathattaṃ siyā vipariṇāmau| abhinandatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ; abhivadatu, bhantai, bhagavā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ| yathaiva bhagavatā pubbai bhikkhusaṅghau anuggahitau, aivamaiva aitarahi anuggaṇhātu bhikkhusaṅgha’’nti|

adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau brahmā sahampati bhagavatau adhivāsanaṃ viditvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyi|

atha khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina nigraudhārāmau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā pañ‍ñattai āsanai nisīdi| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā tathārūpaṃ iddhābhisaṅkhāraṃ abhisaṅkhāsi abhisaṅkhāraisi (syā॰ kaṃ॰), abhisaṅkhāyi (pī॰), abhisaṅkharauti (ka॰) yathā tai bhikkhū (aikadvīhikāya sāraj‍jamānarūpā yaināhaṃ yaina bhagavā (?) tainupasaṅkamaiyyuṃ| taipi bhikkhū ) ( ) sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pautthakaisu natthi aikadvīhikāya sāraj‍jamānarūpā yaina bhagavā  tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘antamidaṃ, bhikkhavai, jīvikānaṃ yadidaṃ piṇḍaulyaṃ| abhisāpauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukasmiṃ piṇḍaulau vicarasi pattapāṇīti| tañ‍ca khau aitaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtā upainti atthavasikā, atthavasaṃ paṭic‍ca; naiva rājābhinītā, na caurābhinītā, na iṇaṭṭā, na bhayaṭṭā, na ājīvikāpakatā; api ca khau autiṇṇāmha jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi dukkhautiṇṇā dukkhaparaitā appaiva nāma imassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa antakiriyā pañ‍ñāyaithāti|

‘‘aivaṃ pabbajitau cāyaṃ, bhikkhavai, kulaputtau| sau ca hauti abhijjhālu kāmaisu tibbasārāgau byāpan‍nacittau paduṭṭhamanasaṅkappau muṭṭhassati asampajānau asamāhitau vibbhantacittau pākatindriyau| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, chavālātaṃ ubhataupadittaṃ majjhai gūthagataṃ, naiva gāmai kaṭṭhatthaṃ pharati, nārañ‍ñai kaṭṭhatthaṃ pharati| tathūpamāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, imaṃ puggalaṃ vadāmi gihibhaugā ca parihīnau, sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca na paripūraiti|

‘‘tayaumai, bhikkhavai, akusalavitak‍kā – kāmavitak‍kau, byāpādavitak‍kau, vihiṃsāvitak‍kau| imai ca bhikkhavai, tayau akusalavitak‍kā k‍va aparisaisā nirujjhanti? catūsu vā satipaṭṭhānaisu suppatiṭṭhitacittassa viharatau animittaṃ vā samādhiṃ bhāvayatau| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhavai, alamaiva animittau samādhi bhāvaituṃ| animittau, bhikkhavai, samādhi  bhāvitau bahulīkatau mahapphalau hauti mahānisaṃsau|

‘‘dvaimā, bhikkhavai, diṭṭhiyau – bhavadiṭṭhi ca vibhavadiṭṭhi  ca| tatra khau, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau iti paṭisañ‍cikkhati – ‘atthi nu khau taṃ kiñ‍ci laukasmiṃ yamahaṃ upādiyamānau na vaj‍javā assa’nti? sau aivaṃ pajānāti – ‘natthi nu khau taṃ kiñ‍ci laukasmiṃ yamahaṃ upādiyamānau na vaj‍javā assaṃ| ahañhi rūpañ‍ñaiva upādiyamānau upādiyaiyyaṃ vaidanañ‍ñaiva… sañ‍ñañ‍ñaiva… saṅkhāraiyaiva viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ñaiva upādiyamānau upādiyaiyyaṃ| tassa mai assa ayaṃ (ka॰) upādānapac‍cayā bhavau; bhavapac‍cayā jāti; jātipac‍cayā jarāmaraṇaṃ saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā  sambhavaiyyuṃ| aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau assā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ passaṃ… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pālilaiyyasuttaṃ

81. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai| atha  khau bhagavā pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya kausambiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| kausambiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau sāmaṃ saināsanaṃ  saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya anāmantaitvā  upaṭṭhākai anapalaukaitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ aikau adutiyau cārikaṃ pak‍kāmi|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aisāvusau, ānanda, bhagavā sāmaṃ saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya anāmantaitvā upaṭṭhākai anapalaukaitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ aikau adutiyau cārikaṃ pak‍kantau’’ti| ‘‘yasmiṃ, āvusau, samayai bhagavā sāmaṃ saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya anāmantaitvā upaṭṭhākai anapalaukaitvā bhikkhusaṅghaṃ aikau adutiyau cārikaṃ  pak‍kamati, aikauva bhagavā tasmiṃ samayai viharitukāmau hauti; na bhagavā tasmiṃ samayai kainaci anubandhitabbau hautī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā anupubbaina cārikaṃ caramānau yaina pālilaiyyakaṃ pārilaiyyakaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) tadavasari| tatra sudaṃ bhagavā pālilaiyyakai viharati bhaddasālamūlai| atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘cirassutā khau  nau, āvusau ānanda, bhagavatau sammukhā dhammī kathā; icchāma mayaṃ, āvusau ānanda, bhagavatau sammukhā dhammiṃ kathaṃ sautu’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā ānandau taihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina pālilaiyyakaṃ bhaddasālamūlaṃ yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nai khau tai bhikkhū bhagavā dhammiyā kathāya sandassaisi samādapaisi samuttaijaisi sampahaṃsaisi| taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau jānatau kathaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hautī’’ti? atha khau bhagavā tassa bhikkhunau caitasā caitauparivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘vicayasau daisitau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau; vicayasau daisitā cattārau satipaṭṭhānā; vicayasau daisitā cattārau sammappadhānā; vicayasau  daisitā cattārau iddhipādā; vicayasau daisitāni pañ‍cindriyāni; vicayasau daisitāni pañ‍ca balāni; vicayasau daisitā sattabaujjhaṅgā; vicayasau daisitau ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| aivaṃ vicayasau daisitau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammau| aivaṃ vicayasau daisitai khau, bhikkhavai, mayā dhammai atha ca panidhaikac‍cassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘kathaṃ nu khau jānatau kathaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hautī’’’ti?

‘‘kathañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti? idha bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa  akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, samanupassanā saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau  kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitaina phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi taṇhā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| sāpi vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| saupi phassau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi avij‍jā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati; api ca khau rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, samanupassanā saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitaina phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi taṇhā… sāpi vaidanā… saupi phassau… sāpi avij‍jā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ  samanupassati; api ca khau attani rūpaṃ samanupassati| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, samanupassanā saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitaina phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi taṇhā… sāpi vaidanā… saupi phassau… sāpi avij‍jā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati, na attani rūpaṃ samanupassati; api ca khau rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, samanupassanā saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitai phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa  uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi taṇhā … sāpi vaidanā… saupi phassau… sāpi avij‍jā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau…pai॰… āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ, na attani rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati; api ca khau  vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, api ca khau vaidanāvantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati, api ca khau attani vaidanaṃ samanupassati, api ca khau vaidanāya attānaṃ samanupassati; api ca khau sañ‍ñaṃ… api ca khau saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati, api ca khau saṅkhāravantaṃ attānaṃ samanupassati, api ca khau attani saṅkhārai samanupassati, api ca khau saṅkhāraisu attānaṃ samanupassati; api ca khau viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, api ca khau viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ, api ca khau attani viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, api ca khau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attānaṃ samanupassati| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, samanupassanā saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau…pai॰… kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitai phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi taṇhā… sāpi vaidanā… saupi phassau … sāpi avij‍jā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati, na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati; api ca khau aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘sau attā sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’ti| yā khau pana  sā, bhikkhavai, sassatadiṭṭhi saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na vaidanaṃ … na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati; nāpi aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘sau attā  sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’ti| api ca khau aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘nau cassaṃ nau ca mai siyā nābhavissaṃ na mai bhavissatī’ti| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, ucchaidadiṭṭhi saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitaina phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau…pai॰… aivampi khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hauti|

‘‘na haiva khau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attatau samanupassati, nāpi aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘sau attā sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’ti; nāpi aivaṃdiṭṭhi hauti – ‘nau cassaṃ nau ca mai siyā nābhavissaṃ na mai bhavissatī’ti; api ca khau kaṅkhī hauti  vicikicchī aniṭṭhaṅgatau saddhammai| yā khau pana sā, bhikkhavai, kaṅkhitā vicikicchitā aniṭṭhaṅgatatā saddhammai saṅkhārau sau| sau pana saṅkhārau kiṃnidānau kiṃsamudayau kiṃjātikau kiṃpabhavau? avij‍jāsamphassajaina, bhikkhavai, vaidayitaina phuṭṭhassa assutavatau puthuj‍janassa uppan‍nā taṇhā; tataujau sau saṅkhārau| iti khau, bhikkhavai, saupi saṅkhārau anic‍cau saṅkhatau  paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi taṇhā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| sāpi vaidanā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| saupi phassau anic‍cau saṅkhatau paṭic‍casamuppan‍nau| sāpi avij‍jā anic‍cā saṅkhatā paṭic‍casamuppan‍nā| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau anantarā āsavānaṃ khayau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. puṇṇamasuttaṃ

82. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati pubbārāmai migāramātupāsādai mahatā bhikkhusaṅghaina saddhiṃ| taina khau pana samayaina bhagavā tadahupausathai pan‍narasai puṇṇāya puṇṇamāya rattiyā bhikkhusaṅghaparivutau ajjhaukāsai nisin‍nau hauti|

atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu uṭṭhāyāsanā aikaṃsaṃ uttarāsaṅgaṃ karitvā yaina bhagavā tainañ‍jaliṃ  paṇāmaitvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘pucchaiyyāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ kiñ‍cidaiva kañ‍cidaiva (?) daisaṃ, sacai mai bhagavā aukāsaṃ karauti pañhassa vaiyyākaraṇāyā’’ti? ‘‘taina hi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, sakai āsanai nisīditvā puccha yadākaṅkhasī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā sakai āsanai nisīditvā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘imai nu khau, bhantai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau’’ti|

‘‘imai khau pana, bhikkhu, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā; saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

‘‘imai khau pana, bhantai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā kiṃmūlakā’’ti? ‘‘imai khau, bhikkhu, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā chandamūlakā’’ti…pai॰… tañ‍ñaiva nu khau, bhantai, upādānaṃ tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā udāhu añ‍ñatra pañ‍cahi upādānakkhandhaihi upādānanti? ‘‘na khau, bhikkhu, tañ‍ñaiva upādānaṃ tai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā  nāpi añ‍ñatra pañ‍cahi upādānakkhandhaihi upādānaṃ, api ca yau tattha chandarāgau taṃ tattha upādāna’’nti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

‘‘siyā  pana, bhantai, pañ‍cupādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgavaimattatā’’ti? ‘‘siyā, bhikkhū’’ti bhagavā avauca – ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘aivaṃrūpau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃvaidanau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsañ‍ñau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃsaṅkhārau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhānaṃ, aivaṃviñ‍ñāṇau siyaṃ anāgatamaddhāna’nti| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, siyā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhaisu chandarāgavaimattatā’’ti? ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu…pai॰… uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

‘‘kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, khandhānaṃ khandhādhivacana’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati rūpakkhandhau| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā … yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, ayaṃ vuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhau| aittāvatā khau, bhikkhu, khandhānaṃ khandhādhivacana’’nti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu…pai॰… apucchi –

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu kau pac‍cayau rūpakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya; kau haitu kau pac‍cayau vaidanākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya; kau haitu kau pac‍cayau sañ‍ñākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya; kau haitu kau pac‍cayau saṅkhārakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya; kau haitu kau pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti? ‘‘cattārau khau, bhikkhu, mahābhūtā haitu, cattārau mahābhūtā pac‍cayau rūpakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| phassau haitu phassau pac‍cayau vaidanākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| phassau haitu phassau pac‍cayau sañ‍ñākkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| phassau  haitu, phassau pac‍cayau saṅkhārakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāya| nāmarūpaṃ haitu, nāmarūpaṃ pac‍cayau viñ‍ñāṇakkhandhassa pañ‍ñāpanāyā’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu…pai॰… apucchi –

‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ  vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi hautī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu…pai॰… apucchi –
