‘‘kathaṃ pana, bhantai, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī ariyadhammassa kauvidau ariyadhammai suvinītau, sappurisānaṃ dassāvī sappurisadhammassa kauvidau sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ; na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na  attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi na hautī’’ti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu…pai॰… apucchi –

‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, rūpassa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇaṃ; kau vaidanāya… kau sañ‍ñāya… kau saṅkhārānaṃ… kau viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau, kau ādīnavau, kiṃ nissaraṇa’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ khau, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ rūpassa assādau| yaṃ rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ – ayaṃ rūpassa ādīnavau| yau rūpasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ rūpassa nissaraṇaṃ| yaṃ vaidanaṃ paṭic‍ca… yaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ  paṭic‍ca… yai saṅkhārai paṭic‍ca… yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paṭic‍ca uppaj‍jati sukhaṃ saumanassaṃ – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa assādau| yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ – ayaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa ādīnavau| yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgavinayau chandarāgappahānaṃ – idaṃ viñ‍ñāṇassa nissaraṇa’’nti| ‘‘sādhu, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinanditvā anumauditvā bhagavantaṃ uttariṃ pañhaṃ apucchi –

‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau, kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā  kāci vaidanā… yā  kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, bhikkhu, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti|

taina  khau pana samayaina añ‍ñatarassa bhikkhunau aivaṃ caitasau parivitak‍kau udapādi – ‘‘iti kira bhau rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; anattakatāni kammāni kathamattānaṃ katamattānaṃ (pī॰), kammattānaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) phusissantī’’ti| atha khau bhagavā tassa bhikkhunau caitasā caitau parivitak‍kamañ‍ñāya bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, bhikkhavai, vij‍jati yaṃ idhaikac‍cau maughapurisau avidvā avij‍jāgatau taṇhādhipataiyyaina caitasā satthusāsanaṃ atidhāvitabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyya| iti kira, bhau, rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| anattakatāni kammāni  kathamattānaṃ phusissantīti? paṭipucchāvinītā khau mai tumhai, bhikkhavai, tatra tatra taisu taisu dhammaisu|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

‘‘dvai khandhā tañ‍ñaiva siyaṃ, adhivacanañ‍ca haitunā|

sak‍kāyaina duvai vuttā, assādaviñ‍ñāṇakaina ca|

aitai dasavidhā vuttā, hauti bhikkhu pucchāyā’’ti|| dasamaṃ|

khaj‍janīyavaggau aṭṭhamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

assādau  dvai samudayā, arahantaihi aparai dvai|

sīhau khaj‍janī piṇḍaulyaṃ, pālilaiyyaina puṇṇamāti||

9. thairavaggau

1. ānandasuttaṃ

83. sāvatthinidānaṃ| tatra khau āyasmā ānandau bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āvusau, bhikkhavai’’ti| ‘‘āvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau ānandassa pac‍cassausuṃ| āyasmā ānandau aitadavauca –

‘‘puṇṇau nāma, āvusau, āyasmā mantāṇiputtau mantāniputtau (ka॰ sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) amhākaṃ navakānaṃ sataṃ bahūpakārau hauti| sau amhai iminā auvādaina auvadati – ‘upādāya, āvusau ānanda, asmīti hauti, nau anupādāya| kiñ‍ca upādāya asmīti hauti, nau anupādāya? rūpaṃ upādāya asmīti hauti, nau anupādāya| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya asmīti hauti, nau anupādāya’’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau ānanda, itthī vā purisau vā daharau yuvā maṇḍanakajātikau ādāsai vā parisuddhai pariyaudātai acchai vā udakapattai sakaṃ mukhanimittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhamānau upādāya passaiyya, nau anupādāya; aivamaiva khau, āvusau ānanda, rūpaṃ upādāya asmīti hauti, nau anupādāya| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya asmīti hauti, nau anupādāya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau ānanda, ‘rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’’ti? ‘anic‍caṃ, āvusau’| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā … saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’ti? ‘anic‍caṃ, āvusau’| tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti  pajānātīti| puṇṇau nāma āvusau āyasmā mantāṇiputtau amhākaṃ navakānaṃ  sataṃ bahūpakārau hauti| sau amhai iminā auvādaina  auvadati| idañ‍ca pana mai āyasmatau puṇṇassa mantāṇiputtassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā dhammau abhisamitauti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. tissasuttaṃ

84. sāvatthinidānaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā tissau bhagavatau pitucchāputtau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivamāraucaiti – ‘‘api mai, āvusau, madhurakajātau viya kāyau; disāpi mai na pakkhāyanti; dhammāpi maṃ na paṭibhanti; thinamiddhañ‍ca thīnamiddhañ‍ca (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) mai cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati; anabhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carāmi; hauti ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’ti|

atha khau sambahulā bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘āyasmā, bhantai, tissau bhagavatau pitucchāputtau sambahulānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ aivamāraucaiti – ‘api mai, āvusau, madhurakajātau viya kāyau; disāpi mai na pakkhāyanti; dhammāpi maṃ na paṭibhanti; thinamiddhañ‍ca mai cittaṃ pariyādāya tiṭṭhati; anabhiratau ca brahmacariyaṃ carāmi; hauti ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā añ‍ñataraṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaisi – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, bhikkhu, mama vacanaina tissaṃ bhikkhuṃ āmantaihī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau sau bhikkhu bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā tissau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ tissaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘satthā taṃ, āvusau tissa, āmantaitī’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā tissau tassa bhikkhunau paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ tissaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tvaṃ, tissa, sambahulānaṃ  bhikkhūnaṃ aivamāraucaisi – ‘api mai, āvusau, madhurakajātau viya kāyau…pai॰… hauti ca mai dhammaisu vicikicchā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, tissa, rūpai avigatarāgassa avigatacchandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa avigatataṇhassa, tassa rūpassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu  sādhu, tissa! aivañhaitaṃ, tissa, hauti| yathā taṃ rūpai avigatarāgassa… vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu avigatarāgassa…pai॰… taisaṃ saṅkhārānaṃ vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, tissa! aivañhaitaṃ, tissa, hauti| yathā taṃ viñ‍ñāṇai avigatarāgassa avigatacchandassa avigatapaimassa avigatapipāsassa avigatapariḷāhassa avigatataṇhassa, tassa viñ‍ñāṇassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, tissa! aivañhaitaṃ, tissa, hauti| yathā taṃ viñ‍ñāṇai avigatarāgassa| taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, tissa, rūpai vigatarāgassa vigatacchandassa vigatapaimassa vigatapipāsassa vigatapariḷāhassa vigatataṇhassa, tassa rūpassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, tissa! aivañhaitaṃ, tissa, hauti| yathā taṃ rūpai vigatarāgassa… vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu vigatarāgassa… viñ‍ñāṇai vigatarāgassa vigatacchandassa vigatapaimassa  vigatapipāsassa vigatapariḷāhassa vigatataṇhassa tassa viñ‍ñāṇassa vipariṇāmañ‍ñathābhāvā uppaj‍janti saukaparidaivadukkhadaumanassupāyāsā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, tissa! aivañhaitaṃ, tissa, hauti| yathā taṃ viñ‍ñāṇai vigatarāgassa| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, tissa, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā … sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, tissa, dvai purisā – aikau purisau amaggakusalau, aikau purisau maggakusalau| tamainaṃ sau amaggakusalau purisau amuṃ maggakusalaṃ purisaṃ maggaṃ pucchaiyya| sau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘aihi, bhau purisa, ayaṃ maggau| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha| taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi dvaidhāpathaṃ, tattha vāmaṃ muñ‍citvā dakkhiṇaṃ gaṇhāhi| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha| taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi tibbaṃ vanasaṇḍaṃ| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha| taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi mahantaṃ nin‍naṃ pal‍lalaṃ| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha| taina muhuttaṃ  gantvā dakkhissasi saubbhaṃ papātaṃ| taina muhuttaṃ gaccha| taina muhuttaṃ gantvā dakkhissasi samaṃ bhūmibhāgaṃ ramaṇīya’’’nti|

‘‘upamā khau myāyaṃ, tissa, katā atthassa viñ‍ñāpanāya| ayaṃ caivaittha atthau – ‘purisau amaggakusalau’ti khau, tissa, puthuj‍janassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘purisau maggakusalau’ti khau, tissa, tathāgatassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ arahatau sammāsambuddhassa| ‘dvaidhāpathau’ti khau, tissa, vicikicchāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘vāmau maggau’ti khau, tissa, aṭṭhaṅgikassaitaṃ micchāmaggassa adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – micchādiṭṭhiyā…pai॰… micchāsamādhissa| ‘dakkhiṇau maggau’ti khau, tissa, ariyassaitaṃ aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa adhivacanaṃ, saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhiyā…pai॰… sammāsamādhissa| ‘tibbau vanasaṇḍau’ti khau, tissa, avij‍jāyaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘mahantaṃ nin‍naṃ pal‍lala’nti khau, tissa, kāmānamaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘saubbhau papātau’ti khau, tissa, kaudhūpāyāsassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| ‘samau bhūmibhāgau ramaṇīyau’ti khau, tissa, nibbānassaitaṃ adhivacanaṃ| abhirama, tissa, abhirama, tissa! ahamauvādaina ahamanuggahaina ahamanusāsaniyā’’ti ahamāmisadhammānuggahaina mamauvādaina mamānusāsaniyāti (ka॰)|

idamavauca  bhagavā| attamanau āyasmā tissau bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandīti| dutiyaṃ|

3. yamakasuttaṃ

85. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai  anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina yamakassa nāma bhikkhunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’ti|

assausuṃ khau sambahulā bhikkhū yamakassa kira nāma bhikkhunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ hauti – ‘‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’ti| atha khau tai bhikkhū yaināyasmā yamakau  tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā yamakaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ yamakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ –

‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau yamaka, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ  bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi – ‘khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘mā, āvusau yamaka, aivaṃ avaca, mā bhagavantaṃ abbhācikkhi| na hi sādhu bhagavatau abbhācikkhanaṃ| na hi bhagavā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| aivampi khau āyasmā  yamakau taihi bhikkhūhi vuc‍camānau tathaiva taṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ thāmasā parāmāsā abhinivissa vauharati – ‘‘tathāhaṃ  bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’ti|

yatau khau tai bhikkhū nāsakkhiṃsu āyasmantaṃ yamakaṃ aitasmā pāpakā diṭṭhigatā vivaicaituṃ, atha khau tai bhikkhū uṭṭhāyāsanā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yamakassa nāma, āvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunau aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’ti| sādhāyasmā sāriputtau yaina yamakau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi khau āyasmā sāriputtau tuṇhībhāvaina| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā yamakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā yamakaina saddhiṃ sammaudi…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ yamakaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘sac‍caṃ kira tai, āvusau yamaka, aivarūpaṃ pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ uppan‍naṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau  bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ  khvāhaṃ, āvusau, bhagavatā dhammaṃ  daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau yamaka, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘vaidanā nic‍cā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, āvusau’’| tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau yamaka, rūpaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’ … ‘‘vaidanaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’… ‘‘sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau yamaka, rūpasmiṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘añ‍ñatra rūpā tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘vaidanāya… añ‍ñatra vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… añ‍ñatra saṅkhāraihi… viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau yamaka, rūpaṃ… vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau yamaka, ayaṃ sau arūpī… avaidanau… asañ‍ñī… asaṅkhārau… aviñ‍ñāṇau tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘aittha ca tai, āvusau yamaka, diṭṭhaiva dhammai sac‍catau thaitatau tathatau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tathāgatai anupalabbhiyamānai tathāgatai anupalabbhamānai (?), kal‍laṃ nu tai taṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ – ‘tathāhaṃ bhagavatā dhammaṃ daisitaṃ ājānāmi, yathā khīṇāsavau bhikkhu kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍jati vinassati, na hauti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti?

‘‘ahu khau mai taṃ, āvusau sāriputta, pubbai aviddasunau pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ; idañ‍ca panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā tañ‍caiva pāpakaṃ diṭṭhigataṃ pahīnaṃ, dhammau ca mai abhisamitau’’ti|

‘‘sacai  taṃ, āvusau yamaka, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘yau sau, āvusau yamaka, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā kiṃ hautī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhau tvaṃ, āvusau yamaka, kinti byākaraiyyāsī’’ti? ‘‘sacai maṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ – ‘yau sau, āvusau yamaka, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā kiṃ hautī’ti? aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ – ‘rūpaṃ khau, āvusau, anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ taṃ  niruddhaṃ tadatthaṅgataṃ| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ; yaṃ dukkhaṃ taṃ niruddhaṃ tadatthaṅgata’nti| aivaṃ puṭṭhauhaṃ, āvusau, aivaṃ byākaraiyya’’nti|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, āvusau yamaka! taina hāvusau, yamaka, upamaṃ tai karissāmi aitassaiva atthassa bhiyyausaumattāya ñāṇāya| saiyyathāpi, āvusau yamaka, gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau; sau ca ārakkhasampan‍nau| tassa kaucidaiva purisau uppaj‍jaiyya anatthakāmau ahitakāmau ayaugakkhaimakāmau jīvitā vauraupaitukāmau| tassa aivamassa  – ‘ayaṃ khau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā aḍḍhau mahaddhanau mahābhaugau; sau ca ārakkhasampan‍nau; nāyaṃ na hāyaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) sukarau pasayha jīvitā vauraupaituṃ| yaṃnūnāhaṃ anupakhaj‍ja jīvitā vauraupaiyya’nti| sau taṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘upaṭṭhahaiyyaṃ taṃ, bhantai’ti| tamainaṃ sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā upaṭṭhāpaiyya| sau upaṭṭhahaiyya pubbuṭṭhāyī pacchānipātī kiṃkārapaṭissāvī manāpacārī piyavādī| tassa sau gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā mittataupi naṃ saddahaiyya dahaiyya (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰); suhaj‍jataupi naṃ saddahaiyya; tasmiñ‍ca vissāsaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| yadā khau, āvusau, tassa purisassa aivamassa – ‘saṃvissatthau khau myāyaṃ gahapati vā gahapatiputtau vā’ti, atha naṃ rahaugataṃ viditvā tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaiyya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, āvusau yamaka, yadā hi sau purisau amuṃ gahapatiṃ vā gahapatiputtaṃ vā upasaṅkamitvā aivaṃ āha – ‘upaṭṭhahaiyyaṃ taṃ, bhantai’ti, tadāpi sau vadhakauva| vadhakañ‍ca pana  santaṃ na añ‍ñāsi – ‘vadhakau mai’ti| yadāpi sau upaṭṭhahati pubbuṭṭhāyī pacchānipātī kiṃkārapaṭissāvī manāpacārī piyavādī, tadāpi sau vadhakauva| vadhakañ‍ca pana santaṃ  na añ‍ñāsi – ‘vadhakau mai’ti| yadāpi naṃ rahaugataṃ viditvā tiṇhaina satthaina jīvitā vauraupaiti, tadāpi sau vadhakauva| vadhakañ‍ca pana santaṃ na añ‍ñāsi – ‘vadhakau mai’’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khau, āvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau  samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ’’|

‘‘sau  anic‍caṃ rūpaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| anic‍caṃ vaidanaṃ ‘anic‍cā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| anic‍caṃ sañ‍ñaṃ ‘anic‍cā sañ‍ñā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| anic‍cai saṅkhārai ‘anic‍cā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘dukkhaṃ rūpaṃ ‘dukkhaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ… dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… dukkhai saṅkhārai… dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘anattaṃ rūpaṃ ‘anattā rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| anattaṃ  vaidanaṃ… anattaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… anattai saṅkhārai… anattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘saṅkhataṃ rūpaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| saṅkhataṃ vaidanaṃ… saṅkhataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhatai saṅkhārai… saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘vadhakaṃ rūpaṃ ‘vadhakaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vadhakaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘vadhakā vaidanā’ti… vadhakaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ ‘vadhakā sañ‍ñā’ti… vadhakai saṅkhārai ‘vadhakā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vadhakaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘vadhakaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti|

‘‘sau rūpaṃ upaiti upādiyati adhiṭṭhāti ‘attā mai’ti| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upaiti upādiyati adhiṭṭhāti ‘attā mai’ti| tassimai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā upaitā upādin‍nā dīgharattaṃ ahitāya dukkhāya saṃvattanti|

‘‘sutavā  ca khau, āvusau, ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī…pai॰… sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ attānaṃ; na attani rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ attānaṃ| na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ attānaṃ; na attani viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ attānaṃ|

‘‘sau  anic‍caṃ rūpaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| anic‍caṃ vaidanaṃ … anic‍caṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… anic‍cai saṅkhārai … anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anic‍caṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘dukkhaṃ rūpaṃ ‘dukkhaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| dukkhaṃ vaidanaṃ… dukkhaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… dukkhai saṅkhārai… dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘dukkhaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘anattaṃ rūpaṃ ‘anattā rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| anattaṃ vaidanaṃ… anattaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… anattai saṅkhārai… anattaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘anattā viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘saṅkhataṃ rūpaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| saṅkhataṃ vaidanaṃ… saṅkhataṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhatai saṅkhārai… saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘saṅkhataṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘vadhakaṃ rūpaṃ ‘vadhakaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vadhakaṃ vaidanaṃ… vadhakaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… vadhakai saṅkhārai ‘‘vadhakā saṅkhārā’’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vadhakaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘vadhakaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti|

‘‘sau rūpaṃ na upaiti, na upādiyati, nādhiṭṭhāti – ‘attā mai’ti| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ na upaiti, na upādiyati, nādhiṭṭhāti – ‘attā mai’ti| tassimai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anupaitā anupādin‍nā dīgharattaṃ hitāya sukhāya saṃvattantī’’ti| ‘‘aivamaitaṃ, āvusau sāriputta, hauti yaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ tādisā sabrahmacārinau anukampakā atthakāmā auvādakā anusāsakā| idañ‍ca pana mai āyasmatau  sāriputtassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimutta’’nti| tatiyaṃ|

4. anurādhasuttaṃ

86. aivaṃ  mai sutaṃ – aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā vaisāliyaṃ viharati mahāvanai kūṭāgārasālāyaṃ| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā anurādhau bhagavatau avidūrai  arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharati| atha khau sambahulā añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā yaina āyasmā anurādhau tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā anurādhaina saddhiṃ sammaudiṃsu| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantaṃ anurādhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yau sau, āvusau anurādha, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau imaisu catūsu ṭhānaisu pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā anurādhau tai añ‍ñatitthiyai paribbājakai aitadavauca – ‘‘yau sau āvusau tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau taṃ tathāgatau añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā, ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā’’ti| aivaṃ vuttai, añ‍ñatitthiyā  paribbājakā āyasmantaṃ anurādhaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘sau cāyaṃ bhikkhu navau bhavissati acirapabbajitau, thairau vā pana bālau abyattau’’ti| atha khau añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā āyasmantaṃ anurādhaṃ navavādaina ca bālavādaina ca apasādaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu|

atha  khau āyasmatau anurādhassa acirapak‍kantaisu taisu añ‍ñatitthiyaisu paribbājakaisu aitadahausi – ‘‘sacai khau maṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā uttariṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ| kathaṃ byākaramānau nu khvāhaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau assaṃ, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyaṃ, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’ti?

atha  khau āyasmā anurādhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā anurādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘idhāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavatau avidūrai arañ‍ñakuṭikāyaṃ viharāmi| atha khau, bhantai, sambahulā añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā yaināhaṃ tainupasaṅkamiṃsu  …pai॰… maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘yau sau, āvusau anurādha, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau taṃ tathāgatau imaisu catūsu ṭhānaisu pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti… hauti ca na ca hauti, naiva  hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’’ti?

aivaṃ vuttāhaṃ, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyai paribbājakai aitadavaucaṃ – ‘‘yau sau, āvusau, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau, taṃ tathāgatau añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi ṭhānaihi pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vā…pai॰… ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti vāti| aivaṃ vuttai, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā maṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘sau cāyaṃ bhikkhu na vau bhavissati acirapabbajitau thairau vā pana bālau abyattau’ti| atha khau maṃ, bhantai, tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā navavādaina bālavādaina ca apasādaitvā uṭṭhāyāsanā pak‍kamiṃsu’’|

‘‘tassa  mayhaṃ, bhantai, acirapak‍kantaisu taisu añ‍ñatitthiyaisu paribbājakaisu aitadahausi – ‘sacai khau maṃ tai añ‍ñatitthiyā paribbājakā uttariṃ pañhaṃ pucchaiyyuṃ| kathaṃ byākaramānau nu khvāhaṃ taisaṃ añ‍ñatitthiyānaṃ paribbājakānaṃ vuttavādī caiva bhagavatau assaṃ, na ca bhagavantaṃ abhūtaina abbhācikkhaiyyaṃ, dhammassa cānudhammaṃ byākaraiyyaṃ, na ca kauci sahadhammikau vādānuvādau gārayhaṃ ṭhānaṃ āgacchaiyyā’’’ti?

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ bhantai’’…pai॰… tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpasmiṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘añ‍ñatra rūpā tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanāya…pai॰… añ‍ñatra vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… añ‍ñatra sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… añ‍ñatra saṅkhāraihi… viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ… añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, rūpaṃ… vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, anurādha, ayaṃ sau arūpī avaidanau asañ‍ñī asaṅkhārau aviñ‍ñāṇau tathāgatauti samanupassasī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘aittha ca tai, anurādha, diṭṭhaiva dhammai sac‍catau thaitatau tathāgatai anupalabbhiyamānai kal‍laṃ nu tai taṃ vaiyyākaraṇaṃ – ‘yau sau, āvusau, tathāgatau uttamapurisau paramapurisau paramapattipattau taṃ  tathāgatau añ‍ñatra imaihi catūhi  ṭhānaihi pañ‍ñāpayamānau pañ‍ñāpaiti – hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘sādhu sādhu, anurādha! pubbai cāhaṃ, anurādha, aitarahi ca dukkhañ‍caiva pañ‍ñapaimi, dukkhassa ca niraudha’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. vak‍kalisuttaṃ

87. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina  āyasmā vak‍kali kumbhakāranivaisanai viharati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā vak‍kali upaṭṭhākai āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamatha; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatha – ‘vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau, sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaitha – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, bhagavā yaina vak‍kali bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu; anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau vak‍kalissa paṭissutvā yaina  bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau, sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandati; aivañ‍ca pana vadaiti – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, bhagavā yaina vak‍kali bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu; anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha  khau bhagavā nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaināyasmā vak‍kali tainupasaṅkami| addasā  khau āyasmā vak‍kali bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna mañ‍cakai samadhausi samañ‍causi (sī॰), samañ‍caupi (syā॰ kaṃ॰) saṃ + dhū + ī = samadhausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, vak‍kali, mā tvaṃ mañ‍cakai samadhausi| santimāni āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni; tatthāhaṃ nisīdissāmī’’ti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, vak‍kali, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti, nau abhik‍kamanti; paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau abhik‍kamau’’ti? ‘‘na mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ, na yāpanīyaṃ; bāḷhā mai dukkhā vaidanā abhik‍kamanti, nau paṭik‍kamanti; abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati, nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci tai, vak‍kali, na kiñ‍ci kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, na kauci vippaṭisārau’’ti? ‘‘taggha mai, bhantai, anappakaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ, anappakau vippaṭisārau’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci pana taṃ, vak‍kali, attā sīlatau na upavadatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau maṃ, bhantai, attā sīlatau upavadatī’’ti| ‘‘nau cai kira taṃ, vak‍kali, attā sīlatau upavadati; atha kiñ‍ca tai kuk‍kuc‍caṃ kau ca vippaṭisārau’’ti? ‘‘cirapaṭikāhaṃ, bhantai, bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamitukāmau, natthi ca mai kāyasmiṃ tāvatikā balamattā, yāvatāhaṃ yāhaṃ (sī॰), yāyāhaṃ (pī॰) bhagavantaṃ dassanāya upasaṅkamaiyya’’nti|

‘‘alaṃ, vak‍kali, kiṃ tai iminā pūtikāyaina diṭṭhaina? yau  khau, vak‍kali, dhammaṃ passati sau maṃ passati; yau maṃ passati sau dhammaṃ passati| dhammañhi, vak‍kali, passantau maṃ passati; maṃ passantau dhammaṃ passati|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, vak‍kali, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ  nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… aisau mai attāti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ iminā auvādaina auvaditvā uṭṭhāyāsanā yaina gijjhakūṭau pabbatau taina pak‍kāmi| atha khau āyasmā vak‍kali acirapak‍kantassa bhagavatau upaṭṭhākai āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha maṃ, āvusau, mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā yaina isigilipassaṃ kāḷasilā tainupasaṅkamatha| kathañhi nāma mādisau antaragharai kālaṃ kattabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau vak‍kalissa paṭissutvā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ mañ‍cakaṃ āraupaitvā yaina isigilipassaṃ kāḷasilā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu| atha khau bhagavā tañ‍ca rattiṃ tañ‍ca divāvasaisaṃ gijjhakūṭai pabbatai vihāsi| atha  khau dvai daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ gijjhakūṭaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu…pai॰… aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau aikā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu vimaukkhāya caitaitī’’ti| aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘sau hi nūna, bhantai, suvimuttau vimuc‍cissatī’’ti| idamavaucuṃ tā daivatāyau| idaṃ vatvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā padakkhiṇaṃ katvā tatthaivantaradhāyiṃsu|

atha khau bhagavā tassā rattiyā ac‍cayaina bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha tumhai, bhikkhavai, yaina vak‍kali bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatha; upasaṅkamitvā vak‍kaliṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaitha –

‘‘‘suṇāvusau tvaṃ, vak‍kali, bhagavatau vacanaṃ dvin‍nañ‍ca  daivatānaṃ| imaṃ, āvusau, rattiṃ dvai daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā abhik‍kantavaṇṇā kaivalakappaṃ gijjhakūṭaṃ aubhāsaitvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, āvusau, aikā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu vimaukkhāya caitaitīti| aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – sau hi nūna, bhantai, suvimuttau vimuc‍cissatīti| bhagavā ca taṃ, āvusau vak‍kali, aivamāha – mā bhāyi, vak‍kali; mā bhāyi, vak‍kali! apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati, apāpikā kālakiriyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai  bhikkhū  bhagavatau paṭissutvā yaināyasmā vak‍kali tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘suṇāvusau vak‍kali, bhagavatau vacanaṃ dvin‍nañ‍ca daivatāna’’nti|

atha khau āyasmā vak‍kali upaṭṭhākai āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha maṃ, āvusau, mañ‍cakā auraupaitha| kathañhi nāma mādisau uc‍cai āsanai nisīditvā tassa bhagavatau sāsanaṃ sautabbaṃ mañ‍ñaiyyā’’ti! ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau vak‍kalissa paṭissutvā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ mañ‍cakā auraupaisuṃ| ‘‘imaṃ, āvusau, rattiṃ dvai daivatāyau abhik‍kantāya rattiyā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ aṭṭhaṃsu| aikamantaṃ ṭhitā khau, āvusau, aikā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu vimaukkhāya caitaitī’ti| aparā daivatā bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘sau hi nūna, bhantai, suvimuttau vimuc‍cissatī’ti| bhagavā ca taṃ, āvusau vak‍kali, aivamāha – ‘mā bhāyi, vak‍kali; mā bhāyi, vak‍kali! apāpakaṃ tai maraṇaṃ bhavissati, apāpikā kālakiriyā’’’ti| ‘‘taina hāvusau, mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatha – ‘vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaitha – ‘rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ| tāhaṃ, bhantai, na kaṅkhāmi| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti na vicikicchāmi| yadanic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, natthi mai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vāti na vicikicchāmi| vaidanā  anic‍cā| tāhaṃ, bhantai, na kaṅkhāmi| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti na  vicikicchāmi| yadanic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, natthi mai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vāti na vicikicchāmi| sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā anic‍cā| tāhaṃ, bhantai, na kaṅkhāmi| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti na vicikicchāmi| yadanic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, natthi mai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vāti na vicikicchāmi| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| tāhaṃ, bhantai, na kaṅkhāmi| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti na vicikicchāmi| yadanic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, natthi mai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vāti na vicikicchāmī’’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau vak‍kalissa paṭissutvā pak‍kamiṃsu| atha khau āyasmā vak‍kali acirapak‍kantaisu taisu bhikkhūsu satthaṃ āharaisi|

atha khau tai bhikkhū yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘vak‍kali, bhantai, bhikkhu ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau; sau bhagavatau  pādai sirasā vandati; aivañ‍ca vadaiti – ‘rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ| tāhaṃ, bhantai, na kaṅkhāmi| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti na vicikicchāmi| yadanic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, natthi mai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vāti na vicikicchāmi| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā … viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| tāhaṃ, bhantai, na kaṅkhāmi| yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhanti na vicikicchāmi| yadanic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, natthi mai tattha chandau vā rāgau vā paimaṃ vāti na vicikicchāmī’’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘āyāma, bhikkhavai, yaina isigilipassaṃ kāḷasilā tainupasaṅkamissāma; yattha vak‍kalinā kulaputtaina satthamāharita’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| atha khau bhagavā sambahulaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ yaina isigilipassaṃ kāḷasilā tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ vak‍kaliṃ dūratauva mañ‍cakai vivattakkhandhaṃ saimānaṃ|

taina  khau pana samayaina dhūmāyitattaṃ timirāyitattaṃ gacchataiva purimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati pacchimaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uttaraṃ disaṃ, gacchati dakkhiṇaṃ disaṃ, gacchati uddhaṃ disaṃ, gacchati adhau  disaṃ, gacchati anudisaṃ| atha khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi – ‘‘passatha nau tumhai, bhikkhavai, aitaṃ dhūmāyitattaṃ timirāyitattaṃ gacchataiva purimaṃ disaṃ…pai॰… gacchati anudisa’’nti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aisau khau, bhikkhavai, mārau pāpimā vak‍kalissa kulaputtassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samanvaisati saman‍naisati (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰) – ‘kattha vak‍kalissa kulaputtassa viñ‍ñāṇaṃ patiṭṭhita’nti? appatiṭṭhitaina ca, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇaina vak‍kali kulaputtau parinibbutau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. assajisuttaṃ

88. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā assaji kassapakārāmai viharati  ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha khau āyasmā assaji upaṭṭhākai āmantaisi – ‘‘aitha tumhai, āvusau, yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamatha; upasaṅkamitvā mama vacanaina bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatha – ‘assaji, bhantai, bhikkhu ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| sau bhagavatau pādai sirasā vandatī’ti| aivañ‍ca vadaitha – ‘sādhu kira, bhantai, bhagavā  yaina assaji bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’’ti| ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau tai bhikkhū āyasmatau assajissa paṭissutvā yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkamiṃsu; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdiṃsu| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nā khau tai bhikkhū bhagavantaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘assaji, bhantai, bhikkhu ābādhikau…pai॰… sādhu kira, bhantai, bhagavā yaina assaji bhikkhu tainupasaṅkamatu anukampaṃ upādāyā’’ti| adhivāsaisi bhagavā tuṇhībhāvaina|

atha  khau bhagavā sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā assaji tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā assaji bhagavantaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna mañ‍cakai samadhausi| atha khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ assajiṃ āyasmatau assajissa (pī॰ ka॰) aitadavauca – ‘‘alaṃ, assaji, mā tvaṃ mañ‍cakai samadhausi| santimāni āsanāni pañ‍ñattāni, tatthāhaṃ nisīdissāmī’’ti| nisīdi bhagavā pañ‍ñattai āsanai| nisaj‍ja khau bhagavā āyasmantaṃ assajiṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kac‍ci tai, assaji, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci  yāpanīyaṃ…pai॰… paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau abhik‍kamau’’ti?

‘‘na  mai, bhantai, khamanīyaṃ…pai॰… abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci tai, assaji, na kiñ‍ci kuk‍kuc‍caṃ na kauci vippaṭisārau’’ti? ‘‘taggha mai, bhantai, anappakaṃ kuk‍kuc‍caṃ anappakau vippaṭisārau’’ti| ‘‘kac‍ci pana taṃ, assaji, attā sīlatau na upavadatī’’ti? ‘‘na khau maṃ, bhantai, attā sīlatau upavadatī’’ti| ‘‘nau cai kira taṃ, assaji, attā sīlatau upavadati, atha kiñ‍ca tai kuk‍kuc‍caṃ kau ca vippaṭisārau’’ti? ‘‘pubbai khvāhaṃ, bhantai, gailañ‍ñai passambhaitvā passambhaitvā kāyasaṅkhārai viharāmi, sauhaṃ samādhiṃ nappaṭilabhāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, bhantai, taṃ samādhiṃ appaṭilabhatau aivaṃ hauti – ‘nau cassāhaṃ parihāyāmī’’’ti| ‘‘yai tai, assaji, samaṇabrāhmaṇā samādhisārakā samādhisāmañ‍ñā taisaṃ taṃ samādhiṃ appaṭilabhataṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘nau cassu mayaṃ parihāyāmā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, assaji, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  …pai॰… tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātīti| sau sukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati vaidiyati (sī॰ pī॰), sā ‘anic‍cā’ti pajānāti| ‘anajjhausitā’ti  pajānāti| ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti| dukkhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā ‘anic‍cā’ti pajānāti| ‘anajjhausitā’ti pajānāti| ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti| adukkhamasukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, sā ‘anic‍cā’ti pajānāti…pai॰… ‘anabhinanditā’ti pajānāti| sau sukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati; dukkhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati; adukkhamasukhaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayati, visaṃyuttau naṃ vaidayati| sau kāyapariyantikaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| jīvitapariyantikaṃ cai vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānāti|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, assaji, tailañ‍ca paṭic‍ca, vaṭṭiñ‍ca paṭic‍ca, tailappadīpau jhāyaiyya; tassaiva tailassa ca vaṭṭiyā ca pariyādānā anāhārau nibbāyaiyya| aivamaiva khau, assaji, bhikkhu kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘kāyapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayamānau ‘jīvitapariyantikaṃ vaidanaṃ vaidayāmī’ti pajānāti| ‘kāyassa bhaidā uddhaṃ jīvitapariyādānā idhaiva sabbavaidayitāni anabhinanditāni sītībhavissantī’ti pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. khaimakasuttaṃ

89. aikaṃ samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū kausambiyaṃ viharanti ghausitārāmai| taina khau pana samayaina āyasmā khaimakau badarikārāmai viharati ābādhikau dukkhitau bāḷhagilānau| atha  khau thairā bhikkhū sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitā āyasmantaṃ dāsakaṃ āmantaisuṃ – ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau dāsaka, yaina khaimakau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā  khaimakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – kac‍ci tai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ, kac‍ci yāpanīyaṃ, kac‍ci dukkhā vaidanā paṭik‍kamanti nau abhik‍kamanti, paṭik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau abhik‍kamau’’’ti? ‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā dāsakau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaināyasmā khaimakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ khaimakaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘thairā taṃ, āvusau  khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘kac‍ci tai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ…pai॰… nau abhik‍kamau’’’ti? ‘‘na mai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ na yāpanīyaṃ…pai॰… abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā dāsakau yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘khaimakau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivamāha – ‘na mai, āvusau, khamanīyaṃ…pai॰… abhik‍kamausānaṃ pañ‍ñāyati nau paṭik‍kamau’’’ti| ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau dāsaka, yaina khaimakau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā khaimakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imaisu āyasmā  khaimakau pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassatī’’’ti?

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā dāsakau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaināyasmā khaimakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imaisu āyasmā khaimakau pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassatī’’ti? ‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imaisu khvāhaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu na kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassāmī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā dāsakau yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘khaimakau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivamāha – ‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imaisu khvāhaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu na kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassāmī’’’ti| ‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau dāsaka, yaina khaimakau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā khaimakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| nau cai kirāyasmā khaimakau imaisu  pañ‍casu  upādānakkhandhaisu kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassati| tainahāyasmā khaimakau arahaṃ khīṇāsavau’’’ti|

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā dāsakau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaināyasmā khaimakau…pai॰… thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau; nau cai kirāyasmā khaimakau imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassati, tainahāyasmā khaimakau arahaṃ khīṇāsavau’’ti| ‘‘pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imaisu khvāhaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu na kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassāmi, na camhi arahaṃ khīṇāsavau; api ca mai, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu ‘asmī’ti adhigataṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti na ca samanupassāmī’’ti|

atha  khau āyasmā dāsakau yaina thairā bhikkhū…pai॰… thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘khaimakau, āvusau, bhikkhu aivamāha – pañ‍cimai, āvusau, upādānakkhandhā vuttā bhagavatā, saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| imaisu khvāhaṃ, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu na kiñ‍ci attaṃ vā attaniyaṃ vā samanupassāmi, na camhi arahaṃ khīṇāsavau; api ca mai, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu ‘asmī’ti adhigataṃ, ‘ayamahamasmī’ti na ca samanupassāmī’’ti|

‘‘aihi tvaṃ, āvusau dāsaka, yaina khaimakau bhikkhu tainupasaṅkama; upasaṅkamitvā khaimakaṃ bhikkhuṃ aivaṃ vadaihi – ‘thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – yamaitaṃ, āvusau khaimaka, asmīti vadaisi, kimaitaṃ asmīti vadaisi? rūpaṃ asmīti vadaisi, añ‍ñatra rūpā asmīti vadaisi, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ asmīti vadaisi, añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā asmīti vadaisi| yamaitaṃ, āvusau khaimaka, asmīti vadaisi| kimaitaṃ asmīti vadaisī’’’ti?

‘‘aivamāvusau’’ti khau āyasmā dāsakau thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ paṭissutvā yaināyasmā khaimakau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ khaimakaṃ aitadavauca – thairā taṃ, āvusau khaimaka, aivamāhaṃsu – ‘‘yamaitaṃ, āvusau  khaimaka, ‘asmī’ti vadaisi, kimaitaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi? rūpaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi añ‍ñatra rūpā ‘asmī’ti vadaisi? vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā ‘asmī’ti vadaisi? yamaitaṃ, āvusau khaimaka, ‘asmī’ti vadaisi, kimaitaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi’’ti? ‘‘alaṃ, āvusau dāsaka, kiṃ imāya sandhāvanikāya! āharāvusau, daṇḍaṃ; ahamaiva yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkamissāmī’’ti|

atha khau āyasmā khaimakau daṇḍamaulubbha yaina thairā bhikkhū tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā thairaihi bhikkhūhi saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ  kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ  nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ khaimakaṃ thairā bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘yamaitaṃ, āvusau khaimaka, ‘asmī’ti vadaisi, kimaitaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi? rūpaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi, añ‍ñatra rūpā ‘asmī’ti vadaisi? vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisi, añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā ‘asmī’ti vadaisi? yamaitaṃ, āvusau khaimaka, ‘asmī’ti vadaisi, kimaitaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadaisī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, āvusau, rūpaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadāmi; napi añ‍ñatra rūpā ‘asmī’ti vadāmi| na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadāmi; napi añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā ‘asmī’ti vadāmi| api ca mai, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu ‘asmī’ti adhigataṃ ‘ayamahamasmī’ti na ca samanupassāmi’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, uppalassa vā padumassa vā puṇḍarīkassa vā gandhau| yau nu khau aivaṃ vadaiyya – ‘pattassa gandhau’ti vā ‘vaṇṇassa vaṇḍassa (katthaci) gandhau’ti vā ‘kiñ‍jakkhassa gandhau’ti vā sammā nu khau sau vadamānau vadaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, āvusau’’| ‘‘yathā kathaṃ, panāvusau, sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyyā’’ti? ‘‘‘pupphassa gandhau’ti khau, āvusau, sammā byākaramānau byākaraiyyā’’ti| ‘‘aivamaiva khvāhaṃ, āvusau, na rūpaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadāmi, napi añ‍ñatra rūpā ‘asmī’ti vadāmi| na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na  viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘asmī’ti vadāmi, napi añ‍ñatra viñ‍ñāṇā ‘asmī’ti vadāmi| api ca mai, āvusau, pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu ‘asmī’ti adhigataṃ ‘ayamahamasmī’ti na ca samanupassāmi’’|

‘‘kiñ‍cāpi, āvusau, ariyasāvakassa pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni bhavanti, atha khvassa hauti – ‘yau ca pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anusahagatau  asmīti mānau, asmīti chandau, asmīti anusayau asamūhatau| sau aparaina samayaina pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati – iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa  atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| tassimaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassinau viharatau yaupissa hauti pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anusahagatau ‘asmī’ti, mānau ‘asmī’ti, chandau ‘asmī’ti anusayau asamūhatau, saupi samugghātaṃ gacchati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, āvusau, vatthaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ malaggahitaṃ| tamainaṃ sāmikā rajakassa anupadaj‍juṃ| tamainaṃ rajakau ūsai vā khārai vā gaumayai vā sammadditvā acchai udakai vikkhālaiti| kiñ‍cāpi taṃ hauti vatthaṃ parisuddhaṃ pariyaudātaṃ, atha khvassa hauti yaiva anusahagatau ūsagandhau vā khāragandhau vā gaumayagandhau vā asamūhatau| tamainaṃ rajakau sāmikānaṃ daiti| tamainaṃ sāmikā gandhaparibhāvitai karaṇḍakai nikkhipanti| yaupissa hauti anusahagatau ūsagandhau  vā khāragandhau vā gaumayagandhau vā asamūhatau, saupi samugghātaṃ gacchati| aivamaiva khau, āvusau, kiñ‍cāpi ariyasāvakassa pañ‍caurambhāgiyāni saṃyaujanāni pahīnāni bhavanti, atha khvassa hauti yaiva pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anusahagatau ‘asmī’ti, mānau ‘asmī’ti, chandau ‘asmī’ti anusayau asamūhatau| sau aparaina samayaina pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassī viharati| ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti| tassa imaisu pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu udayabbayānupassinau viharatau yaupissa hauti pañ‍casu upādānakkhandhaisu anusahagatau ‘asmī’ti, mānau ‘asmī’ti, chandau ‘asmī’ti anusayau asamūhatau, saupi samugghātaṃ gacchatī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, thairā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ khaimakaṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘na khau na khau pana (ka॰) mayaṃ āyasmantaṃ khaimakaṃ  vihaisāpaikhā pucchimha, api cāyasmā khaimakau pahausi tassa bhagavatau sāsanaṃ vitthāraina ācikkhituṃ daisaituṃ pañ‍ñāpaituṃ  paṭṭhapaituṃ vivarituṃ vibhajituṃ uttānīkātuṃ| tayidaṃ āyasmatā khaimakaina tassa bhagavatau sāsanaṃ vitthāraina ācikkhitaṃ daisitaṃ pañ‍ñāpitaṃ paṭṭhapitaṃ vivaritaṃ vibhajitaṃ uttānīkata’’nti|

idamavauca āyasmā khaimakau| attamanā thairā bhikkhū āyasmatau khaimakassa bhāsitaṃ abhinanduṃ| imasmiñ‍ca pana vaiyyākaraṇasmiṃ  bhañ‍ñamānai saṭṭhimattānaṃ thairānaṃ bhikkhūnaṃ anupādāya āsavaihi cittāni vimuc‍ciṃsu, āyasmatau khaimakassa cāti| sattamaṃ|

8. chan‍nasuttaṃ

90. aikaṃ  samayaṃ sambahulā thairā bhikkhū bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau avāpuraṇaṃ apāpuraṇaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) ādāya vihāraina vihāraṃ upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū aitadavauca – ‘‘auvadantu maṃ āyasmantau thairā, anusāsantu maṃ āyasmantau thairā, karauntu mai āyasmantau thairā dhammiṃ kathaṃ, yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyya’’nti|

aivaṃ vuttai, thairā bhikkhū āyasmantaṃ chan‍naṃ aitadavaucuṃ – ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, āvusau chan‍na, anic‍caṃ; vaidanā anic‍cā; sañ‍ñā anic‍cā; saṅkhārā anic‍cā; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| rūpaṃ anattā; vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā; sabbai dhammā anattā’’ti|

atha khau āyasmatau chan‍nassa aitadahausi – ‘‘mayhampi khau aitaṃ aivaṃ mayhampi khau aivaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) hauti – ‘rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ, vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā … sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai dhammā anattā’ti| atha ca pana mai sabbasaṅkhārasamathai sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggai taṇhākkhayai virāgai niraudhai nibbānai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| paritassanā upādānaṃ uppaj‍jati; pac‍cudāvattati mānasaṃ – ‘atha kau carahi mai attā’ti? na khau panaivaṃ dhammaṃ passatau hauti| kau nu khau mai tathā dhammaṃ daisaiyya yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyya’’nti|

atha khau āyasmatau chan‍nassa aitadahausi – ‘‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitārāmai satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau  ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahauti ca mai āyasmā ānandau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyyaṃ; atthi ca mai āyasmantai ānandai tāvatikā vissaṭṭhi vissatthi (?)| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’’nti| atha khau āyasmā chan‍nau saināsanaṃ saṃsāmaitvā pattacīvaramādāya yaina kausambī ghausitārāmau yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmatā ānandaina saddhiṃ sammaudi…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā chan‍nau āyasmantaṃ ānandaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘aikamidāhaṃ, āvusau ānanda, samayaṃ bārāṇasiyaṃ viharāmi isipatanai migadāyai| atha khvāhaṃ, āvusau, sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau avāpuraṇaṃ ādāya vihāraina vihāraṃ upasaṅkamiṃ; upasaṅkamitvā thairai bhikkhū aitadavaucaṃ – ‘auvadantu maṃ āyasmantau thairā, anusāsantu maṃ āyasmantau thairā, karauntu mai āyasmantau thairā dhammiṃ kathaṃ yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyya’nti| aivaṃ vuttai maṃ, āvusau, thairā bhikkhū aitadavaucuṃ – ‘rūpaṃ khau, āvusau chan‍na, anic‍caṃ; vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; rūpaṃ anattā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  anattā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai dhammā anattā’’’ti|

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘mayhampi khau aitaṃ  aivaṃ hauti – rūpaṃ anic‍caṃ…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ, rūpaṃ anattā, vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| sabbai saṅkhārā anic‍cā, sabbai dhammā anattā’ti| atha ca pana mai sabbasaṅkhārasamathai sabbūpadhipaṭinissaggai taṇhākkhayai virāgai niraudhai nibbānai cittaṃ na pakkhandati nappasīdati na santiṭṭhati nādhimuc‍cati| paritassanā upādānaṃ uppaj‍jati; pac‍cudāvattati mānasaṃ – ‘atha kau carahi mai attā’ti? na khau panaivaṃ dhammaṃ passatau hauti| kau nu khau mai tathā dhammaṃ daisaiyya yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyyanti!

‘‘tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, aitadahausi – ‘ayaṃ khau āyasmā ānandau kausambiyaṃ viharati ghausitarāmai satthu caiva saṃvaṇṇitau sambhāvitau ca viñ‍ñūnaṃ sabrahmacārīnaṃ, pahauti ca mai āyasmā ānandau tathā dhammaṃ daisaituṃ yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyyaṃ| atthi ca mai āyasmantai ānandai  tāvatikā vissaṭṭhi| yaṃnūnāhaṃ yaināyasmā ānandau tainupasaṅkamaiyya’nti| auvadatu maṃ, āyasmā ānandau; anusāsatu maṃ, āyasmā ānandau; karautu mai, āyasmā ānandau dhammiṃ kathaṃ yathāhaṃ dhammaṃ passaiyya’’nti|

‘‘aittakainapi mayaṃ āyasmatau chan‍nassa attamanā api nāma taṃ attamanā abhiraddhā, taṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) āyasmā chan‍nau āvi akāsi khīlaṃ chindi pabhindi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| audahāvusau, chan‍na, sautaṃ; bhabbausi bhabbau tvaṃ (ka॰) dhammaṃ viñ‍ñātu’’nti| atha khau āyasmatau chan‍nassa tāvatakainaiva tāvadaiva (sī॰) uḷāraṃ pītipāmauj‍jaṃ uppaj‍ji – ‘‘bhabbau kirasmi dhammaṃ viñ‍ñātu’’nti|

‘‘sammukhā  maitaṃ, āvusau chan‍na, bhagavatau sutaṃ, sammukhā ca paṭiggahitaṃ kac‍cānagauttaṃ bhikkhuṃ auvadantassa – dvayanissitau khvāyaṃ, kac‍cāna, laukau yaibhuyyaina  atthitañ‍caiva natthitañ‍ca| laukasamudayaṃ khau, kac‍cāna, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā laukai natthitā, sā na hauti| laukaniraudhaṃ khau, kac‍cāna, yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passatau yā laukai atthitā, sā na hauti| upayupādānābhinivaisavinibandhau khvāyaṃ, kac‍cāna, laukau yaibhuyyaina taṃ cāyaṃ upayupādānaṃ caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayaṃ na upaiti na upādiyati nādhiṭṭhāti ‘attā mai’ti| dukkhamaiva uppaj‍jamānaṃ uppaj‍jati, dukkhaṃ nirujjhamānaṃ nirujjhatīti na kaṅkhati na vicikicchati| aparappac‍cayā ñāṇamaivassa aittha hauti| aittāvatā khau, kac‍cāna, sammādiṭṭhi hauti| sabbamatthīti khau, kac‍cāna, ayamaikau antau| sabbaṃ natthīti ayaṃ dutiyau antau| aitai tai, kac‍cāna, ubhau antai anupagamma majjhaina tathāgatau dhammaṃ daisaiti – avij‍jāpac‍cayā saṅkhārā; saṅkhārapac‍cayā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa samudayau hauti| avij‍jāya tvaiva asaisavirāganiraudhā saṅkhāraniraudhau…pai॰… aivamaitassa kaivalassa dukkhakkhandhassa niraudhau hautī’’ti|

‘‘aivamaitaṃ, āvusau ānanda, hauti yaisaṃ āyasmantānaṃ tādisā sabrahmacārayau  anukampakā atthakāmā auvādakā anusāsakā| idañ‍ca pana mai āyasmatau ānandassa dhammadaisanaṃ sutvā dhammau abhisamitau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. rāhulasuttaṃ

91. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā rāhulau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti?

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rāhula, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā … yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā…pai॰… sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, rāhula, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyarāhulasuttaṃ

92. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rāhulau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ  mānasaṃ hauti vidhāsamatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti? ‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, rāhula, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādā vimuttau hauti| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ  sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādā vimuttau hauti| aivaṃ khau, rāhula, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti| dasamaṃ|

thairavaggau navamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ānandau  tissau yamakau, anurādhau ca vak‍kali|

assaji khaimakau chan‍nau, rāhulā aparai duvai||

10. pupphavaggau

1. nadīsuttaṃ

93. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, nadī pabbataiyyā auhārinī dūraṅgamā sīghasautā| tassā ubhausu tīraisu ubhatau tīrai (sī॰), ubhatau tīraisu (syā॰ kaṃ॰) kāsā caipi jātā assu, tai naṃ ajjhaulambaiyyuṃ; kusā caipi jātā assu, tai naṃ ajjhaulambaiyyuṃ; pabbajā babbajā (sī॰ pī॰) caipi jātā assu, tai naṃ ajjhaulambaiyyuṃ; bīraṇā caipi jātā assu, tai naṃ ajjhaulambaiyyuṃ; rukkhā caipi jātā assu, tai naṃ ajjhaulambaiyyuṃ| tassā purisau sautaina vuyhamānau kāsai caipi gaṇhaiyya, tai paluj‍jaiyyuṃ| sau tataunidānaṃ anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| kusai caipi gaṇhaiyya, pabbajai caipi gaṇhaiyya, bīraṇai caipi gaṇhaiyya, rukkhai caipi gaṇhaiyya, tai paluj‍jaiyyuṃ| sau tataunidānaṃ anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jaiyya| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī ariyadhammassa akauvidau ariyadhammai avinītau, sappurisānaṃ adassāvī sappurisadhammassa akauvidau sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ rūpaṃ paluj‍jati| sau tataunidānaṃ anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| tassa taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ paluj‍jati| sau tataunidānaṃ anayabyasanaṃ āpaj‍jati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ bhantai’’| ‘‘tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. pupphasuttaṃ

94. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukaina vivadāmi, laukauva mayā vivadati| na, bhikkhavai, dhammavādī kainaci laukasmiṃ vivadati| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, natthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ, ahampi taṃ ‘natthī’ti vadāmi| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, atthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ, ahampi taṃ ‘atthī’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, natthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ, yamahaṃ ‘natthī’ti vadāmi? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ natthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ; ahampi taṃ ‘natthī’ti vadāmi| vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ natthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ; ahampi taṃ ‘natthī’ti vadāmi| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, natthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ; ahampi taṃ ‘natthī’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, atthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ, yamahaṃ ‘atthī’ti vadāmi? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ atthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ; ahampi taṃ ‘atthī’ti vadāmi| vaidanā anic‍cā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ  dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ atthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ; ahampi taṃ ‘atthī’ti vadāmi| idaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, atthisammataṃ laukai paṇḍitānaṃ; ahampi taṃ ‘atthī’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘atthi, bhikkhavai, laukai laukadhammau, taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti; abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā taṃ ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti|

‘‘kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, laukai laukadhammau, taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti, abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukai laukadhammau taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā  abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti|

‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina aivaṃ ācikkhiyamānai daisiyamānai pañ‍ñapiyamānai paṭṭhapiyamānai vivariyamānai  vibhajiyamānai uttānīkariyamānai na jānāti na passati tamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālaṃ puthuj‍janaṃ andhaṃ acakkhukaṃ ajānantaṃ apassantaṃ kinti karaumi! vaidanā, bhikkhavai, laukai laukadhammau…pai॰… sañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai… saṅkhārā, bhikkhavai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, laukai laukadhammau taṃ tathāgatau abhisambujjhati abhisamaiti| abhisambujjhitvā abhisamaitvā ācikkhati daisaiti pañ‍ñapaiti paṭṭhapaiti vivarati vibhajati uttānīkarauti|

‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, tathāgataina aivaṃ ācikkhiyamānai daisiyamānai pañ‍ñapiyamānai paṭṭhapiyamānai vivariyamānai vibhajiyamānai uttānīkariyamānai na  jānāti na passati tamahaṃ, bhikkhavai, bālaṃ puthuj‍janaṃ andhaṃ acakkhukaṃ ajānantaṃ apassantaṃ kinti karaumi!

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, uppalaṃ vā padumaṃ vā puṇḍarīkaṃ vā udakai jātaṃ udakai saṃvaḍḍhaṃ udakā ac‍cuggamma ṭhāti tiṭṭhantaṃ (ka॰) anupalittaṃ udakaina; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, tathāgatau laukai jātau laukai saṃvaḍḍhau laukaṃ abhibhuyya viharati anupalittau laukainā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. phaiṇapiṇḍūpamasuttaṃ

95. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā ayujjhāyaṃ ayaujjhāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) viharati gaṅgāya nadiyā tīrai| tatra khau bhagavā bhikkhū āmantaisi –

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ayaṃ gaṅgā nadī mahantaṃ phaiṇapiṇḍaṃ āvahaiyya| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau passaiyya nijjhāyaiyya yaunisau upaparikkhaiyya| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau  rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, phaiṇapiṇḍai sārau? aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā taṃ bhikkhu  passati nijjhāyati yaunisau upaparikkhati| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyati| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, rūpai sārau?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai thul‍laphusitakai daivai vassantai udakai udakapubbuḷaṃ udakabubbuḷaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) uppaj‍jati caiva nirujjhati ca| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau  passaiyya nijjhāyaiyya yaunisau upaparikkhaiyya| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, udakapubbuḷai sārau? aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yā kāci vaidanā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā…pai॰… yā dūrai santikai vā taṃ bhikkhu passati nijjhāyati yaunisau upaparikkhati| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyati| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, vaidanāya sārau?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gimhānaṃ pacchimai māsai ṭhitai majjhanhikai kālai marīcikā phandati| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau passaiyya nijjhāyaiyya yaunisau upaparikkhaiyya| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya…pai॰… kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, marīcikāya sārau? aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yā kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰…|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, purisau sāratthikau sāragavaisī sārapariyaisanaṃ caramānau tiṇhaṃ kuṭhāriṃ kudhāriṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ ka॰) ādāya vanaṃ pavisaiyya| sau tattha passaiyya mahantaṃ kadalikkhandhaṃ ujuṃ navaṃ akuk‍kukajātaṃ akuk‍kajātaṃ (ka॰ sī॰ pī॰), akusajātaṃ (ka॰ sī॰), akuk‍kujakajātaṃ (ka॰)| tamainaṃ mūlai chindaiyya; mūlai chaitvā aggai chindaiyya, aggai chaitvā pattavaṭṭiṃ vinibbhujaiyya| sau tassa pattavaṭṭiṃ vinibbhujantau phaiggumpi  nādhigacchaiyya, kutau sāraṃ! tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau passaiyya nijjhāyaiyya yaunisau upaparikkhaiyya| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau  rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, asārakañ‍ñaiva  khāyaiyya| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, kadalikkhandhai sārau? aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yai kaici saṅkhārā atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍nā…pai॰… yai  dūrai santikai vā taṃ bhikkhu passati nijjhāyati yaunisau upaparikkhati| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyati| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, saṅkhāraisu sārau?

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, māyākārau vā māyākārantaivāsī vā catumahāpathai cātummahāpathai (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) māyaṃ vidaṃsaiyya| tamainaṃ cakkhumā purisau passaiyya nijjhāyaiyya yaunisau upaparikkhaiyya| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyaiyya| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, māyāya sārau? aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, taṃ bhikkhu passati nijjhāyati yaunisau upaparikkhati| tassa taṃ passatau nijjhāyatau yaunisau upaparikkhatau rittakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, tucchakañ‍ñaiva khāyati, asārakañ‍ñaiva khāyati| kiñhi siyā, bhikkhavai, viñ‍ñāṇai sārau?

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi… sañ‍ñāyapi… saṅkhāraisupi … viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānāti’’|

idamavauca bhagavā| idaṃ vatvāna sugatau athāparaṃ aitadavauca satthā –

‘‘phaiṇapiṇḍūpamaṃ rūpaṃ, vaidanā bubbuḷūpamā bubbulūpamā (sī॰), pubbuḷaupamā (ka॰)|

marīcikūpamā sañ‍ñā, saṅkhārā kadalūpamā|

māyūpamañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, daisitādic‍cabandhunā||

‘‘yathā yathā nijjhāyati, yaunisau upaparikkhati|

rittakaṃ tucchakaṃ hauti, yau naṃ passati yaunisau||

‘‘imañ‍ca  kāyaṃ ārabbha, bhūripañ‍ñaina daisitaṃ|

pahānaṃ tiṇṇaṃ dhammānaṃ, rūpaṃ passatha passaitha (sī॰) chaḍḍitaṃ||

‘‘āyu  usmā ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yadā kāyaṃ jahantimaṃ|

apaviddhau apaviṭṭhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) tadā saiti, parabhattaṃ acaitanaṃ||

‘‘aitādisāyaṃ santānau, māyāyaṃ bālalāpinī|

vadhakau aisa akkhātau, sārau aittha na vij‍jati||

‘‘aivaṃ khandhai avaikkhaiyya, bhikkhu āraddhavīriyau|

divā vā yadi vā rattiṃ, sampajānau paṭissatau||

‘‘jahaiyya sabbasaṃyaugaṃ, karaiyya saraṇattanau|

caraiyyādittasīsauva, patthayaṃ ac‍cutaṃ pada’’nti|| tatiyaṃ|

4. gaumayapiṇḍasuttaṃ

96. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ yaṃ rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kāci vaidanā yā vaidanā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kāci sañ‍ñā yā sañ‍ñā…pai॰… atthi nu khau, bhantai, kaici saṅkhārā yai saṅkhārā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassanti? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ  dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, bhikkhu, kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| natthi khau, bhikkhu, kāci vaidanā… kāci sañ‍ñā… kaici saṅkhārā… kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ gaumayapiṇḍaṃ pāṇinā gahaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aittakaupi khau, bhikkhu, attabhāvapaṭilābhau natthi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| aittakau caipi, bhikkhu, attabhāvapaṭilābhau abhavissa nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau  avipariṇāmadhammau, nayidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhu, aittakaupi attabhāvapaṭilābhau  natthi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘bhūtapubbāhaṃ, bhikkhu, rājā ahausiṃ khattiyau muddhāvasittau| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītinagarasahassāni ahaisuṃ kusāvatī rājadhānippamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītipāsādasahassāni ahaisuṃ dhammapāsādappamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītikūṭāgārasahassāni ahaisuṃ mahābyūhakūṭāgārappamukhāni mahāviyūhakūṭāgārappamukhāni (dī॰ ni॰ 2.263)| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītipal‍laṅkasahassāni ahaisuṃ dantamayāni sāramayāni sauvaṇṇamayāni gauṇakatthatāni paṭikatthatāni paṭalikatthatāni kadalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇāni  kādalimigapavarapac‍cattharaṇāni (sī॰) sauttaracchadāni ubhataulauhitakūpadhānāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītināgasahassāni ahaisuṃ sauvaṇṇālaṅkārāni sauvaṇṇaddhajāni haimajālapaṭicchan‍nāni upausathanāgarājappamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītiassasahassāni ahaisuṃ sauvaṇṇālaṅkārāni sauvaṇṇaddhajāni haimajālapaṭicchan‍nāni  valāhakaassarājappamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītirathasahassāni ahaisuṃ sauvaṇṇālaṅkārāni sauvaṇṇaddhajāni haimajālapaṭicchan‍nāni vaijayantarathappamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu, rañ‍ñau satau khattiyassa muddhāvasittassa caturāsītimaṇisahassāni ahaisuṃ maṇiratanappamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu…pai॰… caturāsītiitthisahassāni ahaisuṃ subhaddādaivippamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu…pai॰… caturāsītikhattiyasahassāni ahaisuṃ anuyantāni pariṇāyakaratanappamukhāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu…pai॰… caturāsītidhainusahassāni ahaisuṃ dukūlasandanāni kaṃsūpadhāraṇāni| tassa  mayhaṃ, bhikkhu…pai॰… caturāsītivatthakauṭisahassāni ahaisuṃ khaumasukhumāni kausaiyyasukhumāni kambalasukhumāni  kappāsikasukhumāni| tassa mayhaṃ, bhikkhu…pai॰… caturāsītithālipākasahassāni ahaisuṃ; sāyaṃ pātaṃ bhattābhihārau abhihariyittha|

‘‘taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā nagarasahassānaṃ aikañ‍ñaiva taṃ nagaraṃ hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina ajjhāvasāmi – kusāvatī rājadhānī| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā pāsādasahassānaṃ  aikauyaiva sau pāsādau hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina ajjhāvasāmi – dhammau pāsādau| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā kūṭāgārasahassānaṃ aikañ‍ñaiva taṃ kūṭāgāraṃ hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina ajjhāvasāmi – mahābyūhaṃ kūṭāgāraṃ| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā pal‍laṅkasahassānaṃ aikauyaiva sau pal‍laṅkau hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina paribhuñ‍jāmi – dantamayau vā sāramayau vā sauvaṇṇamayau  vā rūpiyamayau vā| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā nāgasahassānaṃ aikauyaiva sau nāgau hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina abhiruhāmi – upausathau nāgarājā| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā assasahassānaṃ aikauyaiva sau assau hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina abhiruhāmi – valāhakau assarājā| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā rathasahassānaṃ aikauyaiva sau rathau hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina abhiruhāmi – vaijayantau rathau| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā itthisahassānaṃ aikāyaiva sā itthī hauti yā maṃ taina samayaina pac‍cupaṭṭhāti – khattiyānī vā vailāmikā vā| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā vatthakauṭisahassānaṃ aikañ‍ñaiva taṃ vatthayugaṃ hauti yamahaṃ taina samayaina paridahāmi – khaumasukhumaṃ vā kausaiyyasukhumaṃ vā kambalasukhumaṃ vā kappāsikasukhumaṃ vā| taisaṃ khau pana, bhikkhu, caturāsītiyā thālipākasahassānaṃ aikauyaiva sau thālipākau hauti yatau nāḷikaudanaparamaṃ bhuñ‍jāmi tadupiyañ‍ca sūpaiyyaṃ sūpabyañ‍janaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰)| iti khau, bhikkhu, sabbai tai saṅkhārā atītā niruddhā vipariṇatā| aivaṃ anic‍cā khau, bhikkhu, saṅkhārā| aivaṃ addhuvā khau, bhikkhu, saṅkhārā| aivaṃ anassāsikā khau, bhikkhu, saṅkhārā| yāvañ‍cidaṃ, bhikkhu, alamaiva sabbasaṅkhāraisu nibbindituṃ, alaṃ viraj‍jituṃ, alaṃ vimuc‍citu’’nti| catutthaṃ|

5. nakhasikhāsuttaṃ

97. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ yaṃ rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kāci vaidanā yā vaidanā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kāci sañ‍ñā…pai॰… kaici saṅkhārā, yai saṅkhārā nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassanti? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, bhikkhu, kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| natthi khau, bhikkhu, kāci vaidanā… kāci sañ‍ñā… kaici saṅkhārā…pai॰… kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’’ti|

atha khau bhagavā parittaṃ nakhasikhāyaṃ paṃsuṃ āraupaitvā taṃ bhikkhuṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘aittakampi khau, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ natthi nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| aittakaṃ caipi, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ abhavissa nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ, nayidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhu, aittakampi rūpaṃ natthi nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya’’|

‘‘aittakāpi  khau, bhikkhu, vaidanā natthi nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ  tathaiva ṭhassati| aittakā caipi, bhikkhu, vaidanā abhavissa nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā, na yidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhu, aittakāpi vaidanā natthi nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘aittakāpi khau, bhikkhu, sañ‍ñā natthi…pai॰… aittakāpi khau, bhikkhu, saṅkhārā natthi nic‍cā  dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassanti| aittakā caipi, bhikkhu, saṅkhārā abhavissaṃsu nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā  avipariṇāmadhammā, na yidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhu, aittakāpi saṅkhārā natthi nic‍cā dhuvā sassatā avipariṇāmadhammā, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘aittakampi khau, bhikkhu, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ natthi nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| aittakampi khau, bhikkhu, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhavissa nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ, na yidaṃ brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyaitha sammā dukkhakkhayāya| yasmā ca khau, bhikkhu, aittakampi viñ‍ñāṇaṃ natthi nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ, tasmā brahmacariyavāsau pañ‍ñāyati sammā dukkhakkhayāya|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, bhikkhu, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ  bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘tasmātiha…pai॰… aivaṃ  passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. suddhikasuttaṃ

98. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘atthi nu khau, bhantai, kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ, yaṃ rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati? atthi nu khau, bhantai, kāci vaidanā…pai॰… kāci sañ‍ñā… kaici saṅkhārā… kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’’ti? ‘‘natthi khau, bhikkhu, kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ yaṃ rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ  avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassati| natthi khau, bhikkhu, kāci vaidanā… kāci sañ‍ñā… kaici saṅkhārā… kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, yaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ dhuvaṃ sassataṃ avipariṇāmadhammaṃ sassatisamaṃ tathaiva ṭhassatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. gaddulabaddhasuttaṃ

99. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ mahāsamuddau ussussati visussati na  bhavati; na tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi| hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ sinairu pabbatarājā ḍayhati vinassati na bhavati; na tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ  sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ  dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi| hauti sau, bhikkhavai, samayau yaṃ mahāpathavī ḍayhati vinassati na bhavati; na tvaivāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ dukkhassa antakiriyaṃ vadāmi’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sā gaddulabaddhau gaddūlabandhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) daḷhai khīlai vā thambhai vā upanibaddhau tamaiva khīlaṃ vā thambhaṃ vā anuparidhāvati anuparivattati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī…pai॰… sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… vaidanaṃ attatau samanupassati… sañ‍ñaṃ attatau samanupassati… saṅkhārai attatau samanupassati… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| sau rūpañ‍ñaiva anuparidhāvati anuparivattati, vaidanañ‍ñaiva…pai॰… sañ‍ñañ‍ñaiva… saṅkhāraiyaiva… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ñaiva anuparidhāvati anuparivattati| sau rūpaṃ anuparidhāvaṃ anuparivattaṃ, vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anuparidhāvaṃ anuparivattaṃ, na parimuc‍cati rūpamhā, na parimuc‍cati vaidanāya, na parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, na parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, na parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, na parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāmaraṇaina saukaihi  paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘na parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi’’|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī…pai॰… sappurisadhammai suvinītau, na rūpaṃ attatau  samanupassati…pai॰… na vaidanaṃ… na sañ‍ñaṃ… na saṅkhārai… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati, na viñ‍ñāṇavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, na viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| sau rūpaṃ nānuparidhāvati nānuparivattati, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nānuparidhāvati nānuparivattati| sau rūpaṃ ananuparidhāvaṃ ananuparivattaṃ, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ananuparidhāvaṃ ananuparivattaṃ; parimuc‍cati rūpamhā, parimuc‍cati vaidanāya, parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāmaraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi| ‘parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyagaddulabaddhasuttaṃ

100. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘anamataggauyaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃsārau| pubbā kauṭi na pañ‍ñāyati avij‍jānīvaraṇānaṃ sattānaṃ taṇhāsaṃyaujanānaṃ sandhāvataṃ saṃsarataṃ| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sā gaddulabaddhau daḷhai khīlai vā thambhai vā upanibaddhau| sau gacchati caipi tamaiva khīlaṃ vā thambhaṃ vā upagacchati; tiṭṭhati caipi tamaiva khīlaṃ vā thambhaṃ vā upatiṭṭhati; nisīdati caipi tamaiva khīlaṃ vā thambhaṃ vā upanisīdati; nipaj‍jati caipi tamaiva khīlaṃ vā thambhaṃ vā upanipaj‍jati| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai  attā’ti samanupassati| sau gacchati caipi imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai upagacchati; tiṭṭhati caipi imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai upatiṭṭhati; nisīdati caipi imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai upanisīdati; nipaj‍jati caipi imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai upanipaj‍jati| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, abhikkhaṇaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘dīgharattamidaṃ  cittaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ rāgaina dausaina mauhainā’ti| cittasaṃkilaisā, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃkilissanti; cittavaudānā sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘diṭṭhaṃ vau, bhikkhavai, caraṇaṃ nāma citta’’nti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘tampi khau, bhikkhavai, caraṇaṃ nāma cittaṃ cittainaiva cittitaṃ| tainapi khau, bhikkhavai, caraṇaina cittaina cittañ‍ñaiva cittataraṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, abhikkhaṇaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘dīgharattamidaṃ cittaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ rāgaina dausaina mauhainā’ti| cittasaṃkilaisā, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃkilissanti; cittavaudānā sattā visujjhanti|

‘‘nāhaṃ, bhikkhavai, añ‍ñaṃ aikanikāyampi samanupassāmi aivaṃ cittaṃ| yathayidaṃ, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā, taipi khau, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagatā pāṇā cittainaiva cittitā, taihipi khau, bhikkhavai, tiracchānagataihi pāṇaihi cittañ‍ñaiva cittataraṃ| tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, abhikkhaṇaṃ sakaṃ cittaṃ pac‍cavaikkhitabbaṃ – ‘dīgharattamidaṃ cittaṃ saṃkiliṭṭhaṃ rāgaina dausaina mauhainā’ti| cittasaṃkilaisā, bhikkhavai, sattā saṃkilissanti; cittavaudānā sattā visujjhanti|
