‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, rajakau vā cittakārakau vā rajanāya vā lākhāya vā haliddiyā vā nīliyā vā mañ‍jiṭṭhāya mañ‍jaiṭṭhāya (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), mañ‍jaiṭṭhiyā (pī॰) vā suparimaṭṭhai phalakai vā bhittiyā vā dussapaṭṭai vā itthirūpaṃ vā purisarūpaṃ vā abhinimminaiyya sabbaṅgapac‍caṅgiṃ; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpañ‍ñaiva abhinibbattaintau abhinibbattaiti, vaidanañ‍ñaiva…pai॰… sañ‍ñañ‍ñaiva… saṅkhārai yaiva… viñ‍ñāṇañ‍ñaiva abhinibbattaintau abhinibbattaiti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… ‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. vāsijaṭasuttaṃ

101. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘jānatau ahaṃ, bhikkhavai, passatau āsavānaṃ khayaṃ vadāmi, nau ajānatau nau apassatau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, jānatau kiṃ passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti? ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa samudayau, iti rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa  atthaṅgamau’ti – aivaṃ khau, bhikkhavai, jānatau aivaṃ passatau āsavānaṃ khayau hauti’’|

‘‘bhāvanānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa naiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ  vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘abhāvitattā’ tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa abhāvitattā? abhāvitattā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ, abhāvitattā catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ, abhāvitattā catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ, abhāvitattā pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ, abhāvitattā pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ, abhāvitattā sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ, abhāvitattā ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā na sammā adhisayitāni, na sammā parisaiditāni, na sammā paribhāvitāni| kiñ‍cāpi tassā kuk‍kuṭiyā aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau, vata mai  kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau abhabbāva tai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi pana, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā; tāni kuk‍kuṭiyā na sammā adhisayitāni, na sammā parisaiditāni, na sammā paribhāvitāni| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanānuyaugaṃ ananuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau, vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa naiva anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘abhāvitattā’tissa  vacanīyaṃ| kissa abhāvitattā? abhāvitattā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ…pai॰…  aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘bhāvanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttassa, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau viharatau  kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘bhāvitattā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa bhāvitattā? bhāvitattā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ, bhāvitattā catun‍naṃ sammappadhānānaṃ, bhāvitattā catun‍naṃ iddhipādānaṃ, bhāvitattā pañ‍can‍naṃ indriyānaṃ, bhāvitattā pañ‍can‍naṃ balānaṃ, bhāvitattā sattan‍naṃ baujjhaṅgānaṃ, bhāvitattā ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā| tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni, sammā parisaiditāni, sammā paribhāvitāni| kiñ‍cāpi tassā kuk‍kuṭiyā na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jaiyyu’nti, atha khau bhabbāva tai kuk‍kuṭapautakā pādanakhasikhāya vā mukhatuṇḍakaina vā aṇḍakausaṃ padālaitvā sautthinā abhinibbhij‍jituṃ| taṃ kissa haitu? tathā hi pana, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuṭiyā aṇḍāni aṭṭha vā dasa vā dvādasa vā; tānassu kuk‍kuṭiyā sammā adhisayitāni, sammā parisaiditāni, sammā paribhāvitāni| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi na aivaṃ icchā uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘ahau vata mai anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍caiyyā’ti, atha khvassa anupādāya āsavaihi cittaṃ vimuc‍cati| taṃ kissa haitu? ‘bhāvitattā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| kissa  bhāvitattā? bhāvitattā catun‍naṃ satipaṭṭhānānaṃ…pai॰… bhāvitattā ariyassa aṭṭhaṅgikassa maggassa|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, palagaṇḍassa vā palagaṇḍantaivāsissa vā vāsijaṭai dissantaiva aṅgulipadāni dissati aṅguṭṭhapadaṃ| nau ca khvassa aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘aittakaṃ vata mai aj‍ja vāsijaṭassa khīṇaṃ, aittakaṃ hiyyau, aittakaṃ parai’ti| atha khvassa khīṇai khīṇantvaiva ñāṇaṃ hauti| aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttassa  bhikkhunau viharatau kiñ‍cāpi  na aivaṃ ñāṇaṃ hauti – ‘aittakaṃ vata mai aj‍ja āsavānaṃ khīṇaṃ, aittakaṃ hiyyau, aittakaṃ parai’ti, atha khvassa khīṇai khīṇantvaiva ñāṇaṃ hauti| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, sāmuddikāya nāvāya vaittabandhanabaddhāya vassamāsāni udakai pariyādāya haimantikaina thalaṃ ukkhittāya vātātapaparaitāni vaittabandhanāni| tāni pāvusakaina maighaina abhippavuṭṭhāni appakasirainaiva paṭippassambhanti  pūtikāni bhavanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, bhāvanānuyaugaṃ anuyuttassa bhikkhunau viharatau appakasirainaiva saṃyaujanāni paṭippassambhanti pūtikāni bhavantī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. anic‍casañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

102. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘anic‍casañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, bhāvitā bahulīkatā sabbaṃ kāmarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ rūparāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ bhavarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ avij‍jaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai kassakau mahānaṅgalaina kasantau sabbāni mūlasantānakāni sampadālaintau kasati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā sabbaṃ kāmarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ rūparāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ bhavarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ avij‍jaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, pabbajalāyakau pabbajaṃ lāyitvā aggai gahaitvā audhunāti niddhunāti nicchauṭaiti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā sabbaṃ kāmarāgaṃ pariyādiyati…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, ambapiṇḍiyā vaṇṭacchin‍nāya yāni  tattha ambāni vaṇṭapaṭibandhāni sabbāni tāni tadanvayāni bhavanti; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvitā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, kūṭāgārassa yā kāci gaupānasiyau sabbā tā kūṭaṅgamā kūṭanin‍nā  kūṭasamausaraṇā, kūṭaṃ tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvitā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici mūlagandhā kāḷānusārigandhau taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici sāragandhā, lauhitacandanaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici pupphagandhā, vassikaṃ taisaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yai kaici kuṭṭarājānau kuḍḍarājānau (sī॰), sabbaitai rañ‍ñau cak‍kavattissa anuyantā bhavanti, rājā taisaṃ cak‍kavatti aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, yā kāci tārakarūpānaṃ pabhā, sabbā tā candimappabhāya kalaṃ nāgghanti sauḷasiṃ, candappabhā tāsaṃ aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, saradasamayai viddhai vigatavalāhakai daivai ādic‍cau nataṃ abbhussak‍kamānau, sabbaṃ ākāsagataṃ tamagataṃ abhivihac‍ca bhāsatai ca tapatai ca viraucatai ca; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā bhāvitā bahulīkatā sabbaṃ kāmarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ rūparāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ bhavarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ avij‍jaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati|

‘‘kathaṃ  bhāvitā ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā kathaṃ bahulīkatā  sabbaṃ kāmarāgaṃ pariyādiyati…pai॰… sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanati? ‘iti rūpaṃ, iti rūpassa  samudayau, iti  rūpassa atthaṅgamau; iti vaidanā… iti sañ‍ñā… iti saṅkhārā… iti viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayau, iti viñ‍ñāṇassa atthaṅgamau’ti – aivaṃ bhāvitā khau, bhikkhavai, anic‍casañ‍ñā aivaṃ bahulīkatā sabbaṃ kāmarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ rūparāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ bhavarāgaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ avij‍jaṃ pariyādiyati, sabbaṃ asmimānaṃ samūhanatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

pupphavaggau dasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

nadī pupphañ‍ca phaiṇañ‍ca, gaumayañ‍ca nakhāsikhaṃ|

suddhikaṃ dvai ca gaddulā, vāsījaṭaṃ anic‍catāti||

majjhimapaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa majjhimapaṇṇāsakassa vagguddānaṃ –

upayau arahantau ca, khaj‍janī thairasavhayaṃ|

pupphavaggaina paṇṇāsa, dutiyau taina vuc‍catīti||

11. antavaggau

1. antasuttaṃ

103. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, antā| katamai cattārau? sak‍kāyantau, sak‍kāyasamudayantau, sak‍kāyaniraudhantau, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminippaṭipadantau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyantau? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhātissa vacanīyaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyantau’’|

‘‘katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyasamudayantau? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā nandirāgasahagatā tatratatrābhinandinī, saiyyathidaṃ – kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyasamudayantau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhantau? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhantau|

‘‘katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminippaṭipadantau? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi, sammāsaṅkappau, sammāvācā, sammākammantau, sammāājīvau, sammāvāyāmau, sammāsati, sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminippaṭipadantau| imai khau, bhikkhavai, cattārau antā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dukkhasuttaṃ

104. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘dukkhañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi dukkhasamudayañ‍ca dukkhaniraudhañ‍ca dukkhaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamañ‍ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhātissa vacanīyaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| idaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayau? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā…pai॰… kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhasamudayau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhau? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya asaisavirāganiraudhau cāgau paṭinissaggau mutti anālayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhau| katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, dukkhaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sak‍kāyasuttaṃ

105. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘sak‍kāyañ‍ca vau, bhikkhavai, daisaissāmi sak‍kāyasamudayañ‍ca sak‍kāyaniraudhañ‍ca  sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminiñ‍ca paṭipadaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyau? pañ‍cupādānakkhandhātissa vacanīyaṃ| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyasamudayau? yāyaṃ taṇhā paunaubhavikā…pai॰… kāmataṇhā, bhavataṇhā, vibhavataṇhā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyasamudayau| katamau ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhau? yau tassāyaiva taṇhāya…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhau| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā? ayamaiva ariyau aṭṭhaṅgikau maggau| saiyyathidaṃ – sammādiṭṭhi…pai॰… sammāsamādhi| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sak‍kāyaniraudhagāminī paṭipadā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pariñ‍ñaiyyasuttaṃ

106. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pariñ‍ñaiyyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi pariñ‍ñañ‍ca pariñ‍ñātāviñ‍ca puggalaṃ| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau| vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā| katamā  ca, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñā? rāgakkhayau, dausakkhayau, mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñā| katamau  ca, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñātāvī puggalau? arahātissa vacanīyaṃ| yvāyaṃ āyasmā aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, pariñ‍ñātāvī puggalau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samaṇasuttaṃ

107. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pajānanti, sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasamaṇasuttaṃ

108. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ  – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yai hi kaici, bhikkhavai, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… pajānanti, sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sautāpan‍nasuttaṃ

109. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca  ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. arahantasuttaṃ

110. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pañ‍cimai, bhikkhavai, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, bhikkhu arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. chandappahānasuttaṃ

111. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpai, bhikkhavai, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ  tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhamma’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyachandappahānasuttaṃ

112. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpai, bhikkhavai, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ…pai॰… vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu yau chandau…pai॰… aivaṃ tai saṅkhārā pahīnā bhavissanti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ  anuppādadhammā| viñ‍ñāṇai yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhamma’’nti| dasamaṃ|

antavaggau aikādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

antau dukkhañ‍ca sak‍kāyau, pariñ‍ñaiyyā samaṇā duvai|

sautāpan‍nau arahā ca, duvai ca chandappahānāti||

12. dhammakathikavaggau

1. avij‍jāsuttaṃ

113. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘avij‍jā avij‍jā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpaṃ nappajānāti, rūpasamudayaṃ nappajānāti, rūpaniraudhaṃ nappajānāti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānāti; vaidanaṃ nappajānāti… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai nappajānāti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ  vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, avij‍jā| aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vij‍jāsuttaṃ

114. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā vij‍jā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpaṃ pajānāti, rūpasamudayaṃ pajānāti, rūpaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai pajānāti…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, vij‍jā| aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dhammakathikasuttaṃ

115. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhammakathikau dhammakathikau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu  khau, bhantai, dhammakathikau hautī’’ti? ‘‘rūpassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| rūpassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| rūpassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| vaidanāya cai, bhikkhu…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya cai, bhikkhu… saṅkhārānaṃ cai, bhikkhu… viñ‍ñāṇassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| viñ‍ñāṇassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| viñ‍ñāṇassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidā virāgā  niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyadhammakathikasuttaṃ

116. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dhammakathikau dhammakathikau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, dhammakathikau hauti, kittāvatā dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau hauti, kittāvatā diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau hautī’’ti? ‘‘rūpassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| rūpassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau  hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| rūpassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidā virāgā niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| vaidanāya cai, bhikkhu…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya cai, bhikkhu… saṅkhārānaṃ cai, bhikkhu… viñ‍ñāṇassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya dhammaṃ daisaiti, ‘dhammakathikau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| viñ‍ñāṇassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidāya virāgāya niraudhāya paṭipan‍nau hauti, ‘dhammānudhammappaṭipan‍nau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāya| viñ‍ñāṇassa cai, bhikkhu, nibbidā virāgā  niraudhā anupādāvimuttau hauti, ‘diṭṭhadhammanibbānappattau bhikkhū’ti alaṃ vacanāyā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. bandhanasuttaṃ

117. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘idha bhikkhavai assutavā puthuj‍janau ariyānaṃ adassāvī…pai॰… sappurisadhammai avinītau rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; attani vā rūpaṃ, rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpabandhanabaddhau santarabāhirabandhanabaddhau atīradassī apāradassī, baddhau jīyati baddhau jāyati (sī॰ pī॰) baddhau jāyati baddhau jīyati (sī॰ aṭṭha॰ syā॰ aṭṭha॰) baddhau mīyati baddhau asmā laukā  paraṃ laukaṃ gacchati| vaidanaṃ  attatau samanupassati…pai॰… vaidanāya vā attānaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau vaidanābandhanabaddhau santarabāhirabandhanabaddhau atīradassī apāradassī, baddhau jīyati baddhau mīyati baddhau asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ gacchati| sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, assutavā puthuj‍janau viñ‍ñāṇabandhanabaddhau santarabāhirabandhanabaddhau atīradassī apāradassī, baddhau jīyati baddhau mīyati baddhau asmā laukā paraṃ laukaṃ gacchati’’|

‘‘sutavā ca khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau ariyānaṃ dassāvī…pai॰… sappurisadhammai suvinītau na rūpaṃ attatau samanupassati, na rūpavantaṃ vā attānaṃ; na attani vā rūpaṃ, na rūpasmiṃ vā attānaṃ| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau na rūpabandhanabaddhau, na santarabāhirabandhanabaddhau, tīradassī, pāradassī; ‘parimuttau sau dukkhasmā’ti vadāmi| na vaidanaṃ attatau…pai॰… na sañ‍ñaṃ attatau…pai॰… na saṅkhārai attatau…pai॰… na viñ‍ñāṇaṃ attatau samanupassati…pai॰… ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau na viñ‍ñāṇabandhanabaddhau, na santarabāhirabandhanabaddhau, tīradassī, pāradassī, ‘parimuttau sau dukkhasmā’ti vadāmī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. paripucchitasuttaṃ

118. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau  mai attā’ti samanupassathā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ  yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| ‘‘vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassathā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu, bhikkhavai! viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātīti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyaparipucchitasuttaṃ

119. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassathā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu bhikkhavai! rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti samanupassathā’’ti? ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘sādhu bhikkhavai! viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ…pai॰… aivaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. saṃyaujaniyasuttaṃ

120. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saṃyaujaniyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmī saṃyaujanañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā, katamaṃ saṃyaujanaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyau dhammau; yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  saṃyaujaniyau dhammau; yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha saṃyaujanaṃ| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, saṃyaujaniyā dhammā, idaṃ saṃyaujana’’nti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. upādāniyasuttaṃ

121. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘upādāniyai ca, bhikkhavai, dhammai daisaissāmi upādānañ‍ca| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā, katamaṃ upādānaṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, upādāniyau dhammau, yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha upādānaṃ| vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāniyau dhammau; yau tattha chandarāgau, taṃ tattha upādānaṃ| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, upādāniyā dhammā, idaṃ upādāna’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. sīlavantasuttaṃ

122. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau mahākauṭṭhitau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aitadavauca – ‘‘sīlavatāvusau, sāriputta, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā yaunisau manasikātabbā’’ti? ‘‘sīlavatāvusau, kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| sīlavatāvusau, kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau  dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| ṭhānaṃ khau  panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati yaṃ sīlavā bhikkhu  imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi karauntau sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘sautāpan‍naina panāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘sautāpan‍nainapi khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati yaṃ sautāpan‍nau bhikkhu imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi karauntau sakadāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘sakadāgāminā panāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘sakadāgāmināpi khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati yaṃ sakadāgāmī bhikkhu imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi karauntau anāgāmiphalaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘anāgāminā  panāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘anāgāmināpi khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati yaṃ anāgāmī bhikkhu imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau  yaunisau manasi karauntau arahattaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘arahatā panāvusau sāriputta, katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘arahatāpi khau, āvusau kauṭṭhika, imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| natthi, khvāvusau, arahatau uttari karaṇīyaṃ katassa vā paticayau; api ca imai  dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārā caiva saṃvattanti satisampajañ‍ñā cā’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. sutavantasuttaṃ

123. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aitadavauca –

‘‘sutavatāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘sutavatāvusau kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| katamai pañ‍ca? saiyyathidaṃ – rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| sutavatāvusau kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati – yaṃ sutavā bhikkhu imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi karauntau sautāpattiphalaṃ  sacchikaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘sautāpan‍naina panāvusau sāriputta, bhikkhunā katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘sautāpan‍nainapi khau āvusau kauṭṭhika, bhikkhunā imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| ṭhānaṃ khau panaitaṃ, āvusau, vij‍jati – yaṃ sautāpan‍nau bhikkhu imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhai anic‍catau…pai॰… anattatau yaunisau manasi karauntau sakadāgāmiphalaṃ…pai॰… anāgāmiphalaṃ…pai॰… arahattaphalaṃ sacchikaraiyyā’’ti|

‘‘arahatā panāvusau sāriputta, katamai dhammā yaunisau manasi kātabbā’’ti? ‘‘arahatāpi khvāvusau, kauṭṭhika, imai pañ‍cupādānakkhandhā anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau gaṇḍatau sal‍latau aghatau ābādhatau paratau palaukatau suñ‍ñatau anattatau yaunisau manasi kātabbā| natthi, khvāvusau, arahatau uttari karaṇīyaṃ, katassa vā paticayau; api ca khau imai dhammā bhāvitā bahulīkatā diṭṭhadhammasukhavihārāya caiva saṃvattanti satisampajañ‍ñā cā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. kappasuttaṃ

124. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā kappau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kappau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti?

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, kappa, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ  ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| yā kāci vaidanā…pai॰… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya passati| aivaṃ khau, kappa, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā na hauntī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. dutiyakappasuttaṃ

125. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā kappau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kathaṃ nu khau, bhantai, jānatau kathaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti?

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci, kappa, rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… sabbaṃ rūpaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādāvimuttau hauti| yā  kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā  auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya disvā anupādāvimuttau hauti| aivaṃ khau, kappa, jānatau aivaṃ passatau imasmiñ‍ca saviñ‍ñāṇakai kāyai bahiddhā ca sabbanimittaisu ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānāpagataṃ mānasaṃ hauti vidhā samatik‍kantaṃ santaṃ suvimutta’’nti| tairasamaṃ|

dhammakathikavaggau dvādasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

avij‍jā vij‍jā dvai kathikā, bandhanā paripucchitā duvai|

saṃyaujanaṃ upādānaṃ, sīlaṃ sutavā dvai ca kappaināti||

13. avij‍jāvaggau

1. samudayadhammasuttaṃ

126. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha  khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘avij‍jā avij‍jā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, assutavā puthuj‍janau samudayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; vayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘vayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘samudayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; vayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘vayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; samudayavayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samudayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘samudayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; vayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘vayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; samudayavayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘samudayavayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘samudayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; vayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘vayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā vij‍jā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, sutavā ariyasāvakau samudayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; vayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘vayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘samudayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; vayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘vayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; samudayavayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ… samudayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘samudayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; vayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘vayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; samudayavayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘samudayavayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘samudayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; vayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘vayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhu, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. dutiyasamudayadhammasuttaṃ

127. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau…pai॰… aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau assutavā puthuj‍janau samudayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti; vayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… vayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… ‘samudayavayadhammā vaidanā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samudayadhammai saṅkhārai…pai॰… vayadhammai saṅkhārai…pai॰… samudayavayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘samudayavayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ…pai॰… samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍cati, āvusau, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. tatiyasamudayadhammasuttaṃ

128. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā ca sāriputtau āyasmā ca mahākauṭṭhikau bārāṇasiyaṃ  viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā, vij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau samudayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti; vayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ…pai॰… samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ rūpa’nti yathābhūtaṃ  pajānāti; samudayadhammaṃ vaidanaṃ…pai॰… samudayavayadhammā vaidanā … samudayadhammaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ…pai॰… samudayadhammai saṅkhārai… vayadhammai saṅkhārai… samudayavayadhammai saṅkhārai ‘samudayavayadhammā saṅkhārā’ti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| samudayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… vayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ… samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ ‘samudayavayadhammaṃ viñ‍ñāṇa’nti yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. assādasuttaṃ

129. bārāṇasiyaṃ  viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dutiyaassādasuttaṃ

130. bārāṇasiyaṃ viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… ‘‘‘vij‍jā,  vij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa  assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samudayasuttaṃ

131. bārāṇasiyaṃ  viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. dutiyasamudayasuttaṃ

132. bārāṇasiyaṃ  viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā mahākauṭṭhikau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā, vij‍jā’ti, āvusau sāriputta, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. kauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

133. bārāṇasiyaṃ  viharanti isipatanai migadāyai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau kauṭṭhika, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā, vij‍jā’ti, āvusau kauṭṭhika, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyakauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

134. bārāṇasiyaṃ  viharanti isipatanai migadāyai…pai॰… ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau  kauṭṭhika, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ  vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā, vij‍jā’ti, āvusau kauṭṭhika, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhārānaṃ… viñ‍ñāṇassa samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyakauṭṭhikasuttaṃ

135. tañ‍ñaiva nidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘avij‍jā, avij‍jā’ti, āvusau kauṭṭhika, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, avij‍jā, kittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti?

‘‘idhāvusau, assutavā puthuj‍janau rūpaṃ nappajānāti, rūpasamudayaṃ nappajānāti, rūpaniraudhaṃ nappajānāti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ  paṭipadaṃ nappajānāti| vaidanaṃ nappajānāti…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nappajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ nappajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ nappajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ nappajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, avij‍jā; aittāvatā ca avij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti|

aivaṃ  vuttai, āyasmā sāriputtau āyasmantaṃ mahākauṭṭhikaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vij‍jā, vij‍jā’ti, āvusau kauṭṭhika, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, āvusau, vij‍jā, kittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti? ‘‘idhāvusau, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpaṃ pajānāti, rūpasamudayaṃ  pajānāti, rūpaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, rūpaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhaṃ pajānāti, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiṃ paṭipadaṃ pajānāti| ayaṃ vuc‍catāvusau, vij‍jā; aittāvatā ca vij‍jāgatau hautī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

avij‍jāvaggau tairasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

samudayadhammai tīṇi, assādau aparai duvai|

samudayai ca dvai vuttā, kauṭṭhikai aparai tayauti||

14. kuk‍kuḷavaggau

1. kuk‍kuḷasuttaṃ

136. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, kuk‍kuḷaṃ, vaidanā kuk‍kuḷā, sañ‍ñā kuk‍kuḷā, saṅkhārā kuk‍kuḷā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ kuk‍kuḷaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. anic‍casuttaṃ

137. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā anic‍cā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dutiyaanic‍casuttaṃ

138. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| vaidanā anic‍cā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. tatiyaanic‍casuttaṃ

139. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| kiñ‍ca, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ, tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| vaidanā anic‍cā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| yaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dukkhasuttaṃ

140. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyadukkhasuttaṃ

141. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. tatiyadukkhasuttaṃ

142. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰… yaṃ, bhikkhavai, dukkhaṃ; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. anattasuttaṃ

143. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| kau ca, bhikkhavai, anattā? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| vaidanā anattā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau| yau, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandau pahātabbau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyaanattasuttaṃ

144. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, bhikkhavai, anattā? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| vaidanā anattā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau| yau, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau rāgau pahātabbau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. tatiyaanattasuttaṃ

145. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, bhikkhavai, anattā? rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| vaidanā anattā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau| yau, bhikkhavai, anattā; tatra vau chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. nibbidābahulasuttaṃ

146. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saddhāpabbajitassa, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa ayamanudhammau hauti – yaṃ rūpai nibbidābahulau nibbidābahulaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰ ka॰) viharaiyya| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai nibbidābahulau viharaiyya| yau rūpai nibbidābahulau viharantau, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai nibbidābahulau viharantau rūpaṃ parijānāti, vaidanaṃ… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānāti; sau rūpaṃ parijānaṃ  vaidanaṃ parijānaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ parijānaṃ saṅkhārai parijānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānaṃ parimuc‍cati rūpamhā, parimuc‍cati vaidanāya, parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi; ‘parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. anic‍cānupassīsuttaṃ

147. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saddhāpabbajitassa, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa ayamanudhammau hauti – yaṃ rūpai anic‍cānupassī viharaiyya| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai anic‍cānupassī viharaiyya…pai॰… ‘parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. dukkhānupassīsuttaṃ

148. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saddhāpabbajitassa, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa ayamanudhammau hauti – yaṃ rūpai dukkhānupassī viharaiyya| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai dukkhānupassī viharaiyya…pai॰… ‘parimuc‍cati  dukkhasmā’ti vadāmī’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

14. anattānupassīsuttaṃ

149. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘saddhāpabbajitassa, bhikkhavai, kulaputtassa ayamanudhammau hauti – yaṃ rūpai anattānupassī viharaiyya| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai anattānupassī viharaiyya| (sau rūpai) anattānupassī viharantau, vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai anattānupassī viharantau rūpaṃ parijānāti, vaidanaṃ…pai॰… sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānāti| sau rūpaṃ parijānaṃ vaidanaṃ parijānaṃ sañ‍ñaṃ parijānaṃ saṅkhārai parijānaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ parijānaṃ parimuc‍cati rūpamhā, parimuc‍cati vaidanāya, parimuc‍cati sañ‍ñāya, parimuc‍cati saṅkhāraihi, parimuc‍cati viñ‍ñāṇamhā, parimuc‍cati jātiyā jarāya maraṇaina saukaihi paridaivaihi dukkhaihi daumanassaihi upāyāsaihi; ‘parimuc‍cati dukkhasmā’ti vadāmī’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

kuk‍kuḷavaggau cuddasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

kuk‍kuḷā tayau anic‍caina, dukkhaina aparai tayau|

anattaina tayau vuttā, kulaputtaina dvai dukāti||

15. diṭṭhivaggau

1. ajjhattasuttaṃ

150. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkha’’nti? bhagavaṃmūlakā  nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati rūpaṃ upādāya uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya  sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya uppaj‍jati ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkhaṃ| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyya ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkha’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyya  ajjhattaṃ sukhadukkha’’nti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. aitaṃmamasuttaṃ

151. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassatī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa  …pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti samanupassati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti samanupassaiyyāti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attāti samanupassaiyyāti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ..pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sauattāsuttaṃ

152. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…| ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’ti| vaidanāya…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu  …pai॰…  viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati  – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sau attā sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātīti| tatiyaṃ|

4. naucamaisiyāsuttaṃ

153. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi  uppaj‍jati – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai  sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa, aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. micchādiṭṭhisuttaṃ

154. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa micchādiṭṭhi uppaj‍jatī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa micchādiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| vaidanāya sati… micchādiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu  sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa micchādiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya micchādiṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya micchādiṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti|‘pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sak‍kāyadiṭṭhisuttaṃ

155. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi uppaj‍jatī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya sak‍kāyadiṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. attānudiṭṭhisuttaṃ

156. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa attānudiṭṭhi uppaj‍jatī’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa attānudiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| vaidanāya  sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa attānudiṭṭhi uppaj‍jati| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya attānudiṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya attānudiṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyyā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. abhinivaisasuttaṃ

157. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhā’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhā| vaidanāya  sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dutiyaabhinivaisasuttaṃ

158. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhājjhausānā’’ti? vinibandhā ajjhausānāti (sī॰ ka॰) bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhājjhausānā| vaidanāya sati … sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa uppaj‍janti saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhājjhausānā| taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya uppaj‍jaiyyuṃ saṃyaujanābhinivaisavinibandhājjhausānā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. ānandasuttaṃ

159. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā ānandau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā…pai॰… bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñasi, ānanda, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ  – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’ nau haitaṃ bhantai| tasmātihānanda yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ…pai॰… daṭṭhabbaṃ| (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰)| ‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

diṭṭhivaggau pañ‍cadasamau|

tassuddānaṃ –

ajjhattikaṃ aitaṃmama, sauattā naucamaisiyā|

micchāsak‍kāyattānu dvai, abhinivaisā ānandaināti||

uparipaṇṇāsakau samattau|

tassa uparipaṇṇāsakassa vagguddānaṃ –

antau  dhammakathikā vij‍jā, kuk‍kuḷaṃ diṭṭhipañ‍camaṃ|

tatiyau paṇṇāsakau vuttau, nipātauti pavuc‍catīti nipātau taina vuc‍catīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰)||

khandhasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



2. rādhasaṃyuttaṃ

1. paṭhamavaggau

1. mārasuttaṃ

160. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā rādhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca –

‘‘‘mārau, mārau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, mārau’’ti? ‘‘rūpai khau, rādha, sati mārau vā assa māraitā vā yau vā pana mīyati| tasmātiha tvaṃ, rādha, rūpaṃ mārauti passa, māraitāti passa, mīyatīti passa, raugauti passa, gaṇḍauti passa, sal‍lanti passa, aghanti passa, aghabhūtanti passa| yai naṃ aivaṃ passanti tai sammā passanti| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati mārau vā assa māraitā vā yau vā pana mīyati| tasmātiha tvaṃ, rādha, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mārauti passa, māraitāti passa, mīyatīti passa, raugauti passa, gaṇḍauti passa, sal‍lanti passa, aghanti passa, aghabhūtanti passa| yai naṃ aivaṃ passanti, tai sammā passantī’’ti|

‘‘sammādassanaṃ pana, bhantai, kimatthiya’’nti? ‘‘sammādassanaṃ khau, rādha, nibbidatthaṃ’’| ‘‘nibbidā pana, bhantai, kimatthiyā’’ti? ‘‘nibbidā khau, rādha, virāgatthā’’| ‘‘virāgau  pana, bhantai, kimatthiyau’’ti? ‘‘virāgau khau, rādha, vimuttatthau’’| ‘‘vimutti pana, bhantai, kimatthiyā’’ti? ‘‘vimutti khau, rādha, nibbānatthā’’| ‘‘nibbānaṃ pana, bhantai, kimatthiya’’nti? ‘‘ac‍cayāsi ac‍casarā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), assa (pī॰), ac‍cayā (ka॰), rādha, pañhaṃ, nāsakkhi pañhassa pariyantaṃ gahaituṃ| nibbānaugadhañhi, rādha, brahmacariyaṃ vussati, nibbānaparāyanaṃ nibbānapariyausāna’’nti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sattasuttaṃ

161. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘‘sattau, sattau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| kittāvatā nu khau, bhantai, sattauti vuc‍catī’’ti? ‘‘rūpai khau, rādha, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, tatra sattau, tatra visattau, tasmā sattauti vuc‍cati| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu… viñ‍ñāṇai yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, tatra sattau, tatra visattau, tasmā sattauti vuc‍cati’’|

‘‘saiyyathāpi, rādha, kumārakā vā kumārikāyau vā paṃsvāgārakaihi kīḷanti| yāvakīvañ‍ca taisu paṃsvāgārakaisu avigatarāgā haunti avigatacchandā avigatapaimā avigatapipāsā avigatapariḷāhā avigatataṇhā, tāva tāni paṃsvāgārakāni al‍līyanti kaiḷāyanti dhanāyanti  manāyanti (sī॰ pī॰ ka॰) mamāyanti| yatau ca khau, rādha, kumārakā vā kumārikāyau vā taisu paṃsvāgārakaisu vigatarāgā haunti vigatacchandā vigatapaimā vigatapipāsā vigatapariḷāhā vigatataṇhā, atha khau tāni paṃsvāgārakāni hatthaihi ca pādaihi ca vikiranti vidhamanti viddhaṃsainti vikīḷaniyaṃ vikīḷanikaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) karaunti| aivamaiva khau, rādha, tumhaipi rūpaṃ vikiratha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaitha vikīḷaniyaṃ karautha taṇhākkhayāya paṭipaj‍jatha| vaidanaṃ vikiratha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaitha vikīḷaniyaṃ karautha taṇhākkhayāya paṭipaj‍jatha| sañ‍ñaṃ… saṅkhārai vikiratha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaitha vikīḷaniyaṃ karautha taṇhākkhayāya paṭipaj‍jatha| viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vikiratha vidhamatha viddhaṃsaitha vikīḷaniyaṃ karautha taṇhākkhayāya paṭipaj‍jatha| taṇhākkhayau hi, rādha, nibbāna’’nti| dutiyaṃ|

3. bhavanaittisuttaṃ

162. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘bhavanaittiniraudhau  bhavanaitti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), bhavanaittiniraudhau’ti bhavanaittīti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamā nu khau, bhantai, bhavanaitti, katamau bhavanaittiniraudhau’’ti? ‘‘rūpai  khau, rādha, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā – ayaṃ vuc‍cati bhavanaitti| taisaṃ niraudhau niraudhā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) bhavanaittiniraudhau| vaidanāya… sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu … viñ‍ñāṇai yau chandau…pai॰… adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā  – ayaṃ vuc‍cati bhavanaitti| taisaṃ niraudhau bhavanaittiniraudhau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. pariñ‍ñaiyyasuttaṃ

163. sāvatthinidānaṃ| āyasmā rādhau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘pariñ‍ñaiyyai ca, rādha, dhammai daisaissāmi pariñ‍ñañ‍ca pariñ‍ñātāviṃ puggalañ‍ca| taṃ suṇāhi, sādhukaṃ manasi karauhi; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavatau pac‍cassausi| bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘katamai ca, rādha, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā? rūpaṃ khau, rādha, pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, vaidanā pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, sañ‍ñā pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, saṅkhārā pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pariñ‍ñaiyyau dhammau| imai vuc‍canti, rādha, pariñ‍ñaiyyā dhammā| katamā ca, rādha, pariñ‍ñā? yau khau, rādha, rāgakkhayau dausakkhayau mauhakkhayau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rādha, pariñ‍ñā| katamau ca, rādha, pariñ‍ñātāvī puggalau? ‘arahā’tissa vacanīyaṃ| yvāyaṃ āyasmā aivaṃnāmau aivaṃgauttau – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rādha, pariñ‍ñātāvī puggalau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samaṇasuttaṃ

164. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, rādha, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau, vaidanupādānakkhandhau, sañ‍ñupādānakkhandhau, saṅkhārupādānakkhandhau, viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yai hi  kaici, rādha, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ  upādānakkhandhānaṃ assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti; na mai tai, rādha, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu vā samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu vā brāhmaṇasammatā, na ca pana tai āyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā brahmañ‍ñatthaṃ vā diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharanti| yai ca khau kaici, rādha, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānanti; tai khau mai, rādha, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā samaṇaisu caiva samaṇasammatā brāhmaṇaisu ca brāhmaṇasammatā, tai ca panāyasmantau sāmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca brahmañ‍ñatthañ‍ca diṭṭhaiva dhammai sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dutiyasamaṇasuttaṃ

165. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, rādha, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yai hi kaici, rādha, samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ nappajānanti…pai॰… sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā upasampaj‍ja viharantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sautāpan‍nasuttaṃ

166. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, rādha, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yatau  khau, rādha, ariyasāvakau imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ pajānāti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rādha, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. arahantasuttaṃ

167. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘pañ‍cimai, rādha, upādānakkhandhā| katamai pañ‍ca? rūpupādānakkhandhau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇupādānakkhandhau| yatau khau, rādha, bhikkhu imaisaṃ pañ‍can‍naṃ upādānakkhandhānaṃ samudayañ‍ca atthaṅgamañ‍ca assādañ‍ca ādīnavañ‍ca nissaraṇañ‍ca yathābhūtaṃ viditvā anupādāvimuttau hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, rādha, arahaṃ khīṇāsavau vusitavā katakaraṇīyau auhitabhārau anuppattasadatthau parikkhīṇabhavasaṃyaujanau sammadañ‍ñāvimuttau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. chandarāgasuttaṃ

168. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘rūpai khau, rādha, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| vaidanāya yau  chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ sā vaidanā pahīnā bhavissati ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ tai saṅkhārā pahīnā bhavissanti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| viñ‍ñāṇai  yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā, taṃ pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati…pai॰… anuppādadhamma’’nti| navamaṃ|

10. dutiyachandarāgasuttaṃ

169. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘rūpai khau, rādha, yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ rūpaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhammaṃ| vaidanāya yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahatha| aivaṃ sā vaidanā pahīnā  bhavissati ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| sañ‍ñāya… saṅkhāraisu yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahatha| aivaṃ tai saṅkhārā pahīnā bhavissanti ucchin‍namūlā tālāvatthukatā anabhāvaṃkatā āyatiṃ anuppādadhammā| viñ‍ñāṇai  yau chandau yau rāgau yā nandī yā taṇhā yai upayupādānā caitasau adhiṭṭhānābhinivaisānusayā, tai pajahatha| aivaṃ taṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ pahīnaṃ bhavissati ucchin‍namūlaṃ tālāvatthukataṃ anabhāvaṃkataṃ āyatiṃ anuppādadhamma’’nti| dasamaṃ|

rādhasaṃyuttassa paṭhamau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mārau sattau bhavanaitti, pariñ‍ñaiyyā samaṇā duvai|

sautāpan‍nau arahā ca, chandarāgāparai duvaiti||

2. dutiyavaggau

1. mārasuttaṃ

170. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘mārau, mārau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, mārau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, mārau, vaidanā mārau, sañ‍ñā mārau, saṅkhārā mārau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mārau| aivaṃ passaṃ, rādha, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. māradhammasuttaṃ

171. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘māradhammau, māradhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, māradhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, māradhammau, vaidanā māradhammau, sañ‍ñā māradhammau, saṅkhārā māradhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ māradhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. anic‍casuttaṃ

172. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘anic‍caṃ, anic‍ca’nti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu  khau, bhantai, anic‍ca’’nti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, anic‍caṃ, vaidanā anic‍cā, sañ‍ñā anic‍cā, saṅkhārā anic‍cā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. anic‍cadhammasuttaṃ

173. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘anic‍cadhammau, anic‍cadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, anic‍cadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, anic‍cadhammau, vaidanā anic‍cadhammau, sañ‍ñā anic‍cadhammau, saṅkhārā anic‍cadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍cadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. dukkhasuttaṃ

174. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dukkhaṃ, dukkha’nti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamaṃ nu khau, bhantai, dukkha’’nti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, dukkhaṃ, vaidanā dukkhā, sañ‍ñā dukkhā, saṅkhārā dukkhā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhaṃ| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. dukkhadhammasuttaṃ

175. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘dukkhadhammau, dukkhadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, dukkhadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, dukkhadhammau, vaidanā dukkhadhammau, sañ‍ñā dukkhadhammau, saṅkhārā dukkhadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ dukkhadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. anattasuttaṃ

176. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘‘anattā, anattā’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, anattā’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, anattā, vaidanā anattā, sañ‍ñā anattā, saṅkhārā anattā, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattā| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰…  nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. anattadhammasuttaṃ

177. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘anattadhammau, anattadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, anattadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, anattadhammau, vaidanā anattadhammau, sañ‍ñā anattadhammau, saṅkhārā anattadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anattadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9.khayadhammasuttaṃ

178. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘khayadhammau, khayadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, khayadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, khayadhammau, vaidanā khayadhammau, sañ‍ñā  khayadhammau, saṅkhārā khayadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ khayadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. vayadhammasuttaṃ

179. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘vayadhammau, vayadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, vayadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, vayadhammau, vaidanā vayadhammau, sañ‍ñā vayadhammau, saṅkhārā vayadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ vayadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. samudayadhammasuttaṃ

180. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘samudayadhammau, samudayadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, samudayadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, samudayadhammau, vaidanā samudayadhammau, sañ‍ñā samudayadhammau, saṅkhārā samudayadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ samudayadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. niraudhadhammasuttaṃ

181. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘‘niraudhadhammau, niraudhadhammau’ti, bhantai, vuc‍cati| katamau nu khau, bhantai, niraudhadhammau’’ti? ‘‘rūpaṃ khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau, vaidanā niraudhadhammau, sañ‍ñā niraudhadhammau, saṅkhārā niraudhadhammau, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ niraudhadhammau| aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

rādhasaṃyuttassa dutiyau vaggau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mārau  ca māradhammau ca, anic‍caina aparai duvai|

dukkhaina ca duvai vuttā, anattaina anattaihi (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) tathaiva ca|

khayavayasamudayaṃ, niraudhadhammaina dvādasāti||

3. āyācanavaggau

1-11. mārādisuttaaikādasakaṃ

182. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘yau khau, rādha, mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau sīhaḷapautthakai pana ‘‘tatra tai chandau pahātabbauti aikaṃ suttaṃ, tatra tai rāgau pahātabbauti aikaṃ suttaṃ, tatra tai chandarāgau pahātabbauti aikaṃ sutta’’nti aivaṃ visuṃ visuṃ tīṇi suttāni vibhajitvā dassitāni| aivamuparisuttaisupi| kau ca, rādha, mārau? rūpaṃ khau, rādha, mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau sīhaḷapautthakai pana ‘‘tatra tai chandau pahātabbauti aikaṃ suttaṃ, tatra tai rāgau pahātabbauti aikaṃ suttaṃ, tatra tai chandarāgau pahātabbauti aikaṃ sutta’’nti aivaṃ visuṃ visuṃ tīṇi suttāni vibhajitvā dassitāni| aivamuparisuttaisupi| vaidanā mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… sañ‍ñā mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… saṅkhārā mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… yau khau, rādha, mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

183. yau khau, rādha, māradhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰…|

184. yaṃ  khau, rādha, anic‍caṃ…pai॰…|

185. yau  khau, rādha, anic‍cadhammau…pai॰…|

186. yaṃ khau, rādha, dukkhaṃ…pai॰…|

187. yau khau, rādha, dukkhadhammau…pai॰…|

188. yau  khau, rādha, anattā…pai॰…|

189. yau  khau, rādha, anattadhammau…pai॰…|

190. yau khau, rādha, khayadhammau…pai॰…|

191. yau khau, rādha, vayadhammau…pai॰…|

192. yau khau, rādha, samudayadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau…pai॰…|

12. niraudhadhammasuttaṃ

193. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau āyasmā rādhau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘sādhu mai, bhantai, bhagavā saṃkhittaina dhammaṃ daisaitu, yamahaṃ bhagavatau dhammaṃ sutvā aikau vūpakaṭṭhau appamattau ātāpī pahitattau viharaiyya’’nti|

‘‘yau  khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, rādha, niraudhadhammau? rūpaṃ khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… vaidanā niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… sañ‍ñā niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… saṅkhārā niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… yau khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti sīhaḷapautthakai pana imasmiṃ vaggai chattiṃsa suttāni vibhattāni aikaikaṃ suttaṃ tīṇi tīṇi katvā, aivaṃ catutthavaggaipi|

āyācanavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mārau  ca māradhammau ca, anic‍caina aparai duvai|

dukkhaina ca duvai vuttā, anattaina tathaiva ca|

khayavayasamudayaṃ, niraudhadhammaina dvādasāti||

4. upanisin‍navaggau

1-11. mārādisuttaaikādasakaṃ

194. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘yau khau, rādha, mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, rādha, mārau? rūpaṃ khau, rādha, mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau…pai॰… yau khau, rādha, mārau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

195. yau khau, rādha, māradhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau  pahātabbau…pai॰…|

196. yaṃ khau, rādha, anic‍caṃ…pai॰…|

197. yau khau, rādha, anic‍cadhammau…pai॰…|

198. yaṃ  khau, rādha, dukkhaṃ…pai॰…|

199. yau khau, rādha, dukkhadhammau…pai॰…|

200. yau khau, rādha, anattā…pai॰…|

201. yau khau, rādha, anattadhammau…pai॰…|

202. yau khau, rādha, khayadhammau…pai॰…|

203. yau khau, rādha, vayadhammau…pai॰…|

204. yau  khau, rādha, samudayadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau  pahātabbau…pai॰…|

12. niraudhadhammasuttaṃ

205. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍naṃ khau āyasmantaṃ rādhaṃ bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘yau khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| kau ca, rādha, niraudhadhammau? rūpaṃ  khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā…pai॰… saṅkhārā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau| yau khau, rādha, niraudhadhammau; tatra tai chandau pahātabbau, rāgau pahātabbau, chandarāgau pahātabbau’’ti|

upanisin‍navaggau catutthau|

tassuddānaṃ –

mārau ca māradhammau ca, anic‍caina aparai duvai|

dukkhaina ca duvai vuttā, anattaina tathaiva ca|

khayavayasamudayaṃ, niraudhadhammaina dvādasāti||

rādhasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



3. diṭṭhisaṃyuttaṃ

1. sautāpattivaggau

1. vātasuttaṃ

206. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai| bhagavā aitadavauca  – ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti?

‘‘bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā bhagavaṃnaittikā bhagavaṃpaṭisaraṇā| sādhu vata, bhantai, bhagavantañ‍ñaiva paṭibhātu aitassa bhāsitassa atthau| bhagavatau sutvā bhikkhū dhāraissantī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, bhikkhavai, suṇātha, sādhukaṃ manasi karautha; bhāsissāmī’’ti| ‘‘aivaṃ, bhantai’’ti khau tai bhikkhū bhagavatau pac‍cassausuṃ| bhagavā aitadavauca –

‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’ti| taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘vaidanā nic‍cā vā anic‍cā vā’’ti… ‘‘sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti  vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ yamidaṃ (añ‍ñattha) diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca imaisu chasu (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā  hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhasamudayaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaniraudhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. aitaṃmamasuttaṃ

207. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai  khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati … saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi  nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sauattāsuttaṃ

208. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā  nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|
