‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ bhantai’’…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ  diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sau attā…pai॰… avipariṇāmadhammau’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sau attā…pai॰… avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sau attā, sau laukau, sau paic‍ca bhavissāmi nic‍cau dhuvau sassatau avipariṇāmadhammau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. naucamaisiyāsuttaṃ

209. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai  bhavissatī’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|

‘‘rūpai  khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa  aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘nau cassaṃ, nau ca mai siyā, nābhavissa, na mai bhavissatī’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. natthidin‍nasuttaṃ

210. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ, natthi hutaṃ, natthi sukataduk‍kaṭānaṃ  suk‍kaṭaduk‍kaṭānaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) kammānaṃ phalaṃ vipākau; natthi ayaṃ laukau, natthi parau laukau, natthi mātā, natthi pitā, natthi sattā aupapātikā; natthi laukai  samaṇabrāhmaṇā sammaggatā samaggatā (ka॰) sammāpaṭipan‍nā yai imañ‍ca laukaṃ parañ‍ca laukaṃ sayaṃ abhiñ‍ñā sacchikatvā pavaidainti| cātumahābhūtikau cātummahābhūtikau (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) ayaṃ purisau yadā  kālaṅkarauti pathavī pathavīkāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, āpau āpaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, taijau taijaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati, vāyau vāyaukāyaṃ anupaiti anupagacchati| ākāsaṃ indriyāni saṅkamanti| āsandipañ‍camā purisā mataṃ ādāya gacchanti| yāva āḷāhanā padāni pañ‍ñāyanti| kāpautakāni aṭṭhīni bhavanti| bhassantā āhutiyau| dattupañ‍ñattaṃ yadidaṃ dānaṃ dattupañ‍ñattamidaṃ dānaṃ nāma (sabbattha)| taisaṃ tucchaṃ musā vilāpau yai kaici atthikavādaṃ vadanti| bālai ca paṇḍitai ca kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… kāyassa  bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘natthi din‍naṃ, natthi yiṭṭhaṃ…pai॰… yai kaici atthikavādaṃ  vadanti; bālai ca paṇḍitai ca kāyassa bhaidā ucchij‍janti vinassanti na haunti paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa  kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. karautausuttaṃ

211. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘karautau kārayatau chindatau chaidāpayatau pacatau pācāpayatau saucatau saucāpayatau kilamatau kilamāpayatau phandatau phandāpayatau pāṇamatipātayatau adin‍naṃ ādiyatau sandhiṃ chindatau nil‍laupaṃ haratau aikāgārikaṃ karautau paripanthai tiṭṭhatau paradāraṃ gacchatau musā bhaṇatau karautau na karīyati pāpaṃ| khurapariyantaina caipi cak‍kaina yau imissā pathaviyā pāṇai aikamaṃsakhalaṃ aikamaṃsapuñ‍jaṃ karaiyya, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| dakkhiṇaṃ caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya; hanantau  ghātaintau chindantau chaidāpaintau pacantau pācaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ pāpaṃ, natthi pāpassa āgamau| uttaraṃ caipi gaṅgāya tīraṃ gacchaiyya; dadantau dāpaintau yajantau yajāpaintau, natthi tataunidānaṃ puñ‍ñaṃ, natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau| dānaina damaina saṃyamaina sac‍cavaj‍jaina natthi puñ‍ñaṃ natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’’ti| bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘karautau kārayatau…pai॰… natthi puñ‍ñaṃ natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai  sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘karautau kārayatau…pai॰… natthi puñ‍ñaṃ natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘karautau…pai॰… natthi puñ‍ñaṃ natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘karautau kārayatau  …pai॰… natthi puñ‍ñaṃ natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘karautau kārayatau…pai॰… natthi puñ‍ñaṃ natthi puñ‍ñassa āgamau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. haitusuttaṃ

212. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ  saṃkilaisāya| ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā saṃkilissanti| natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau sattānaṃ visuddhiyā| ahaitū appac‍cayā sattā visujjhanti| natthi balaṃ natthi vīriyaṃ natthi purisathāmau natthi purisaparak‍kamau| sabbai sattā sabbai pāṇā sabbai bhūtā sabbai jīvā avasā abalā avīriyā niyatisaṅgatibhāvapariṇatā chasvaivābhijātīsu sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘natthi haitu, natthi pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api  nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘natthi haitu natthi pac‍cayau…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ paṭisaṃvaidaintī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti  …pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. mahādiṭṭhisuttaṃ

213. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sattimai kāyā akaṭā, akaṭavidhā, animmitā, animmātā, vañjhā, kūṭaṭṭhā, aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā; tai na iñ‍janti, na vipariṇamanti na vipariṇāmainti (pī॰ ka॰), na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ  byābādhainti; nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| katamai satta? pathavīkāyau, āpaukāyau, taijaukāyau, vāyaukāyau, sukhai, dukkhai, jīvai sattamai| imai satta jīvai| sattimai (bahūsu) kāyā akaṭā, akaṭavidhā, animmitā, animmātā, vañjhā, kūṭaṭṭhā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā; tai na iñ‍janti, na vipariṇamanti, na añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ byābādhainti; nālaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñassa sukhāya vā dukkhāya vā sukhadukkhāya vā| yaupi tiṇhaina satthaina sīsaṃ chindati, na saupi kañ‍ci na kauci kañ‍ci (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), na kauci taṃ (pī॰ ka॰) jīvitā vauraupaiti; sattan‍naṃtvaiva kāyānamantaraina satthaṃ vivaramanupavisati vivaramanupatati (katthaci) dīghamajjhimaisupi| cuddasa khau panimāni yaunipamukhasatasahassāni saṭṭhi ca satāni cha ca satāni pañ‍ca ca kammunau satāni pañ‍ca ca kammāni, tīṇi ca kammāni, kammai ca aḍḍhakammai ca dvaṭṭhipaṭipadā, dvaṭṭhantarakappā, chaḷābhijātiyau, aṭṭhapurisabhūmiyau, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa ājīvakasatai, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa paribbājakasatai, aikūnapañ‍ñāsa nāgavāsasatai, vīsai indriyasatai, tiṃsai nirayasatai, chattiṃsarajaudhātuyau, satta sañ‍ñīgabbhā, satta asañ‍ñīgabbhā, satta nigaṇṭhigabbhā, satta daivā, satta mānusā, satta paisācā, satta sarā, satta pavuṭā sapuṭā (ka॰), pavudhā (pī॰), satta papātā, satta ca papātasatāni, satta supinā, satta supinasatāni, cul‍lāsīti mahākappinau mahākappunau (sī॰ pī॰) satasahassāni, yāni bālai ca paṇḍitai ca sandhāvitvā  saṃsaritvā dukkhassantaṃ karissanti| tattha natthi imināhaṃ sīlaina vā vataina vā tapaina vā brahmacariyaina vā aparipak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ paripācaissāmi; paripak‍kaṃ vā kammaṃ phussa phussa byantīkarissāmīti haivaṃ natthi dauṇamitai sukhadukkhai pariyantakatai saṃsārai, natthi hāyanavaḍḍhanai, natthi uk‍kaṃsāvakaṃsai| saiyyathāpi nāma suttaguḷai khittai nibbaiṭhiyamānamaiva palaiti; aivamaiva bālai ca paṇḍitai ca nibbaiṭhiyamānā sukhadukkhaṃ palaintī’’’ti?

bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sattimai kāyā akaṭā, akaṭavidhā…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ palaintī’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ  abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sattimai kāyā akaṭā, akaṭavidhā…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ palaintī’’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰…  ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sattimai kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā…pai॰… sukhadukkhaṃ palaintī’’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sattimai kāyā akaṭā akaṭavidhā…pai॰… nibbaiṭhiyamānā sukhadukkhaṃ palaintī’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. sassatadiṭṭhisuttaṃ

214. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sassatau laukau’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā  nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sassatau laukau’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai  sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘sassatau laukau’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sassatau laukau’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā … sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sassatau laukau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ  vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘sassatau laukau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. asassatadiṭṭhisuttaṃ

215. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘asassatau laukau’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – asassatau laukauti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai…pai॰… api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘asassatau laukau’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau  khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti…pai॰… dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11. antavāsuttaṃ

216. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘antavā laukau’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. anantavāsuttaṃ

217. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘anantavā laukau’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…  niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. taṃjīvaṃtaṃsarīraṃsuttaṃ

218. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīra’’’nti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

14. añ‍ñaṃjīvaṃañ‍ñaṃsarīraṃsuttaṃ

219. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīra’’’nti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…  niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

15. hautitathāgatausuttaṃ

220. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| pan‍narasamaṃ|

16. nahautitathāgatausuttaṃ

221. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa  aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| sauḷasamaṃ|

17. hauticanacahautitathāgatausuttaṃ

222. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| sattarasamaṃ|

18. naivahautinanahautitathāgatausuttaṃ

223. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai  khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti…pai॰…|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yampidaṃ diṭṭhaṃ sutaṃ mutaṃ viñ‍ñātaṃ pattaṃ pariyaisitaṃ anuvicaritaṃ manasā tampi nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘yatau khau, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakassa imaisu ca ṭhānaisu kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhasamudayaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaniraudhaipissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti, dukkhaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāyapissa kaṅkhā pahīnā hauti – ayaṃ vuc‍cati, bhikkhavai, ariyasāvakau sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| aṭṭhārasamaṃ|

sautāpattivaggau|

aṭṭhārasavaiyyākaraṇaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

vātaṃ  aitaṃ mama, sau attā nau ca mai siyā|

natthi karautau haitu ca, mahādiṭṭhaina aṭṭhamaṃ||

sassatau laukau ca, asassatau ca antavā ca|

anantavā ca taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti|

añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti ca||

hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti|

na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti|

naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti||

2. dutiyagamanavaggau

1. vātasuttaṃ

224. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā, aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati…pai॰… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? ‘‘nau  haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā, aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

225-240. (purimavaggai viya aṭṭhārasa vaiyyākaraṇāni vitthāraitabbānīti|) sattarasamaṃ|

18. naivahautinanahautisuttaṃ

241. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā … saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘naiva hauti, na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| aṭṭhārasamaṃ|

19. rūpīattāsuttaṃ

242. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘rūpī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘rūpī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘rūpī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘rūpī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘rūpī  attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘vaidanā…pai॰…  ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘rūpī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| aikūnavīsatimaṃ|

20. arūpīattāsuttaṃ

243. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘arūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? (paiyyālau) vīsatimaṃ|

21. rūpīcaarūpīcaattāsuttaṃ

244. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti…pai॰…| aikavīsatimaṃ|

22. naivarūpīnārūpīattāsuttaṃ

245. ‘‘naiva rūpī nārūpī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti…pai॰…| bāvīsatimaṃ|

23. aikantasukhīsuttaṃ

246. ‘‘aikantasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti…pai॰…| taivīsatimaṃ|

24. aikantadukkhīsuttaṃ

247. ‘‘aikantadukkhī  attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti…pai॰…| catuvīsatimaṃ|

25. sukhadukkhīsuttaṃ

248. ‘‘sukhadukkhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti…pai॰…| pañ‍cavīsatimaṃ|

26. adukkhamasukhīsuttaṃ

249. ‘‘adukkhamasukhī  attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰… ‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā  hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, dukkhai sati, dukkhaṃ upādāya, dukkhaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| chabbīsatimaṃ|

dutiyapaiyyālau|

tassuddānaṃ –

vātaṃ aitaṃ mama sau, attā nau ca mai siyā|

natthi karautau haitu ca, mahādiṭṭhaina aṭṭhamaṃ||

sassatau asassatau  caiva, antānantavā ca vuc‍cati|

taṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ jīvañ‍ca, tathāgataina cattārau||

rūpī attā hauti, arūpī ca attā hauti|

rūpī ca arūpī ca attā hauti|

naiva rūpī nārūpī attā hauti, aikantasukhī attā hauti||

aikantadukkhī attā hauti, sukhadukkhī attā hauti|

adukkhamasukhī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇāti|

imai chabbīsati suttā, dutiyavāraina daisitā||

3. tatiyagamanavaggau

1. navātasuttaṃ

250. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau  vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|

‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| tasmiṃ sati, tadupādāya, aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| tasmiṃ sati, tadupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

251-274. (dutiyavaggai viya catuvīsati suttāni pūraitabbāni|) pañ‍cavīsatimaṃ|

26. adukkhamasukhīsuttaṃ

275. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|

‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| vaidanāya sati  …pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| tasmiṃ sati, tadupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| ‘‘vaidanā…pai॰… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’…pai॰… vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, api nu taṃ anupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jaiyya – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘iti khau, bhikkhavai, yadanic‍caṃ taṃ dukkhaṃ| tasmiṃ sati, tadupādāya aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti| chabbīsatimaṃ|

tatiyapaiyyālau|

4. catutthagamanavaggau

1. navātasuttaṃ

276. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti, na naj‍jau sandanti, na gabbhiniyau vijāyanti, na candimasūriyā udainti vā apainti vā aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|

‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’ti| vaidanāya sati…pai॰… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘na vātā vāyanti…pai॰… aisikaṭṭhāyiṭṭhitā’’’ti| ‘‘taṃ kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā … sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ…pai॰… nāparaṃ itthattāyāti pajānātī’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

277-300. (dutiyavaggai viya catuvīsati suttāni pūraitabbāni|) pañ‍cavīsatimaṃ|

26. adukkhamasukhīsuttaṃ

301. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘kismiṃ nu khau, bhikkhavai, sati, kiṃ upādāya, kiṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti? bhagavaṃmūlakā nau, bhantai, dhammā…pai॰…|

‘‘rūpai khau, bhikkhavai, sati, rūpaṃ upādāya, rūpaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’ti| vaidanāya sati… sañ‍ñāya sati… saṅkhāraisu sati… viñ‍ñāṇai sati, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ upādāya, viñ‍ñāṇaṃ abhinivissa aivaṃ diṭṭhi uppaj‍jati – ‘adukkhamasukhī attā hauti, araugau paraṃ maraṇā’’’ti|

‘‘taṃ  kiṃ mañ‍ñatha, bhikkhavai, rūpaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ, dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai ’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘vaidanā… sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā… viñ‍ñāṇaṃ nic‍caṃ vā anic‍caṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘anic‍caṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ vā taṃ sukhaṃ vā’’ti? ‘‘dukkhaṃ, bhantai’’| ‘‘yaṃ panānic‍caṃ dukkhaṃ  vipariṇāmadhammaṃ, kal‍laṃ nu taṃ samanupassituṃ – ‘aitaṃ mama, aisauhamasmi, aisau mai attā’’’ti? ‘‘nau haitaṃ, bhantai’’|

‘‘tasmātiha, bhikkhavai, yaṃ kiñ‍ci rūpaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ rūpaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ| yā kāci vaidanā… yā kāci sañ‍ñā… yai kaici saṅkhārā… yaṃ kiñ‍ci viñ‍ñāṇaṃ atītānāgatapac‍cuppan‍naṃ ajjhattaṃ vā bahiddhā vā, auḷārikaṃ vā sukhumaṃ vā hīnaṃ vā paṇītaṃ vā yaṃ dūrai santikai vā, sabbaṃ viñ‍ñāṇaṃ – ‘naitaṃ mama, naisauhamasmi, na maisau attā’’’ti aivamaitaṃ yathābhūtaṃ sammappañ‍ñāya daṭṭhabbaṃ|

‘‘aivaṃ passaṃ, bhikkhavai, sutavā ariyasāvakau rūpasmimpi nibbindati, vaidanāyapi nibbindati, sañ‍ñāyapi nibbindati, saṅkhāraisupi nibbindati, viñ‍ñāṇasmimpi nibbindati| nibbindaṃ viraj‍jati; virāgā vimuc‍cati| vimuttasmiṃ vimuttamiti ñāṇaṃ hauti| ‘khīṇā jāti, vusitaṃ brahmacariyaṃ, kataṃ karaṇīyaṃ, nāparaṃ itthattāyā’ti pajānātī’’ti| chabbīsatimaṃ|

tassuddānaṃ –

purimagamanai aṭṭhārasa vaiyyākaraṇā|

dutiyagamanai chabbīsaṃ vitthāraitabbāni||

tatiyagamanai  chabbīsaṃ vitthāraitabbāni|

catutthagamanai chabbīsaṃ vitthāraitabbāni||

diṭṭhisaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|



4. auk‍kantasaṃyuttaṃ

1. cakkhusuttaṃ

302. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cakkhuṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi; sautaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi; ghānaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi; jivhā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī vipariṇāminī añ‍ñathābhāvinī (?); kāyau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; manau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati – ayaṃ vuc‍cati saddhānusārī, auk‍kantau sammattaniyāmaṃ, sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kantau, vītivattau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ; abhabbau taṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ katvā nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jaiyya; abhabbau ca abhabbauva (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰) tāva kālaṃ kātuṃ yāva na sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikarauti’’|

‘‘yassa khau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammā aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamanti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘dhammānusārī, auk‍kantau sammattaniyāmaṃ, sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kantau, vītivattau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ; abhabbau taṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ katvā nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jaiyya; abhabbau ca tāva kālaṃ kātuṃ yāva na sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikarauti’| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ pajānāti aivaṃ passati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rūpasuttaṃ

303. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau; saddā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau; gandhā anic‍cā  vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau; rasā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau; phauṭṭhabbā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau; dhammā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati saddhānusārī, auk‍kantau sammattaniyāmaṃ, sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kantau, vītivattau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ; abhabbau taṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ katvā nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ  vā upapaj‍jaiyya; abhabbau ca tāva kālaṃ kātuṃ yāva na sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikarauti’’|

‘‘yassa khau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammā aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamanti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘dhammānusārī, auk‍kantau sammattaniyāmaṃ, sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kantau, vītivattau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ; abhabbau taṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ katvā nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jaiyya; abhabbau ca tāva kālaṃ kātuṃ yāva na sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikarauti’| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ pajānāti aivaṃ passati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. viñ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

304. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi; sautaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… ghānaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… jivhāviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… kāyaviñ‍ñāṇaṃ… manauviñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| yau bhikkhavai…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samphassasuttaṃ

305. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cakkhusamphassau, bhikkhavai, anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; sautasamphassau… ghānasamphassau… jivhāsamphassau… kāyasamphassau… manausamphassau anic‍cau vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘saddhānusārī…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samphassajāsuttaṃ

306. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cakkhusamphassajā, bhikkhavai, vaidanā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; sautasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… ghānasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… jivhāsamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… kāyasamphassajā vaidanā…pai॰… manausamphassajā vaidanā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘saddhānusārī…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. rūpasañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

307. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpasañ‍ñā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; saddasañ‍ñā… gandhasañ‍ñā… rasasañ‍ñā… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñā… dhammasañ‍ñā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘saddhānusārī…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. rūpasañ‍caitanāsuttaṃ

308. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpasañ‍caitanā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; saddasañ‍caitanā… gandhasañ‍caitanā… rasasañ‍caitanā… phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanā… dhammasañ‍caitanā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘saddhānusārī…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. rūpataṇhāsuttaṃ

309. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpataṇhā, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; saddataṇhā… gandhataṇhā… rasataṇhā… phauṭṭhabbataṇhā… dhammataṇhā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘saddhānusārī…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. pathavīdhātusuttaṃ

310. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘pathavīdhātu, bhikkhavai, anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; āpaudhātu… taijaudhātu… vāyaudhātu… ākāsadhātu… viñ‍ñāṇadhātu anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati ‘saddhānusārī…pai॰… sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. khandhasuttaṃ

311. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘rūpaṃ, bhikkhavai, anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi; vaidanā anic‍cā vipariṇāmī añ‍ñathābhāvī; sañ‍ñā… saṅkhārā anic‍cā vipariṇāminau añ‍ñathābhāvinau; viñ‍ñāṇaṃ anic‍caṃ vipariṇāmi añ‍ñathābhāvi| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ saddahati adhimuc‍cati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati saddhānusārī, auk‍kantau sammattaniyāmaṃ, sappurisabhūmiṃ  auk‍kantau, vītivattau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ; abhabbau taṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ katvā nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jaiyya; abhabbau ca tāva kālaṃ kātuṃ yāva na sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikarauti’’|

‘‘yassa khau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammā aivaṃ pañ‍ñāya mattasau nijjhānaṃ khamanti, ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘dhammānusārī, auk‍kantau sammattaniyāmaṃ, sappurisabhūmiṃ auk‍kantau, vītivattau puthuj‍janabhūmiṃ; abhabbau taṃ kammaṃ kātuṃ, yaṃ kammaṃ katvā nirayaṃ vā tiracchānayauniṃ vā paittivisayaṃ vā upapaj‍jaiyya; abhabbau ca tāva kālaṃ kātuṃ yāva na sautāpattiphalaṃ sacchikarauti’| yau, bhikkhavai, imai dhammai aivaṃ pajānāti aivaṃ passati, ayaṃ vuc‍cati – ‘sautāpan‍nau avinipātadhammau niyatau sambaudhiparāyanau’’’ti| dasamaṃ|

auk‍kantasaṃyuttaṃ auk‍kantikasaṃyuttaṃ (pī॰ ka॰) samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

cakkhu  rūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, phassau ca vaidanāya ca|

sañ‍ñā ca caitanā taṇhā, dhātu khandhaina tai dasāti||


5. uppādasaṃyuttaṃ

1. cakkhusuttaṃ

312. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau sautassa uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… yau ghānassa uppādau ṭhiti… yau jivhāya uppādau ṭhiti… yau kāyassa uppādau ṭhiti… yau manassa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau  uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca, bhikkhavai, cakkhussa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau sautassa niraudhau…pai॰… yau ghānassa niraudhau… yau jivhāya niraudhau… yau kāyassa niraudhau… yau manassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rūpasuttaṃ

313. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau saddānaṃ… yau gandhānaṃ… yau rasānaṃ… yau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… yau dhammānaṃ uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpānaṃ niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau saddānaṃ… yau gandhānaṃ… yau rasānaṃ… yau phauṭṭhabbānaṃ… yau dhammānaṃ niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. viñ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

314. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau…pai॰… yau manauviñ‍ñāṇassa uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… yau manauviñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samphassasuttaṃ

315. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusamphassassa uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau…pai॰… yau manausamphassassa uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusamphassassa niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… yau manausamphassassa niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samphassajasuttaṃ

316. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau…pai॰…|

yau manausamphassajāya vaidanāya uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰…  jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya niraudhau  vūpasamau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… yau manausamphassajāya vaidanāya niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

317. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, rūpasañ‍ñāya uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau…pai॰… yau dhammasañ‍ñāya uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpasañ‍ñāya niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… yau dhammasañ‍ñāya niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sañ‍caitanāsuttaṃ

318. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, rūpasañ‍caitanāya uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau…pai॰… yau dhammasañ‍caitanāya uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpasañ‍caitanāya niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… yau dhammasañ‍caitanāya niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. taṇhāsuttaṃ

319. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, rūpataṇhāya uppādau ṭhiti…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa  pātubhāvau…pai॰… yau dhammataṇhāya uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau  ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpataṇhāya niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau…pai॰… yau dhammataṇhāya niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhātusuttaṃ

320. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau; yau āpaudhātuyā… yau taijaudhātuyā… yau vāyaudhātuyā… yau ākāsadhātuyā… yau viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā niraudhau…pai॰… jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau; yau āpaudhātuyā niraudhau… yau taijaudhātuyā niraudhau… yau vāyaudhātuyā niraudhau… yau ākāsadhātuyā niraudhau… yau viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. khandhasuttaṃ

321. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau khau, bhikkhavai, rūpassa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau vaidanāya… yau sañ‍ñāya… yau saṅkhārānaṃ… yau viñ‍ñāṇassa uppādau ṭhiti abhinibbatti pātubhāvau, dukkhassaisau uppādau, raugānaṃ ṭhiti, jarāmaraṇassa pātubhāvau| yau ca khau, bhikkhavai, rūpassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau| yau vaidanāya… yau sañ‍ñāya… yau saṅkhārānaṃ… yau viñ‍ñāṇassa niraudhau vūpasamau atthaṅgamau, dukkhassaisau niraudhau, raugānaṃ vūpasamau, jarāmaraṇassa atthaṅgamau’’ti| dasamaṃ|

uppādasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

cakkhu  rūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, phassau ca vaidanāya ca|

sañ‍ñā ca caitanā taṇhā, dhātu khandhaina tai dasāti||


6. kilaisasaṃyuttaṃ

1. cakkhusuttaṃ

322. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau sautasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau ghānasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau jivhāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau kāyasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau manasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imaisu chasu ṭhānaisu caitasau upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti, naikkhammanin‍nañ‍cassa cittaṃ hauti| naikkhammaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kammaniyaṃ khāyati, abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. rūpasuttaṃ

323. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpaisu chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau saddaisu… yau gandhaisu… yau rasaisu… yau phauṭṭhabbaisu… yau dhammaisu chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imaisu chasu ṭhānaisu caitasau upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti, naikkhammanin‍nañ‍cassa cittaṃ hauti| naikkhammaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kammaniyaṃ khāyati, abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. viñ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

324. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, cakkhuviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau sautaviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ… yau ghānaviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ… yau jivhāviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ… yau  kāyaviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ… yau manauviñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imaisu chasu ṭhānaisu caitasau upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti, naikkhammanin‍nañ‍cassa cittaṃ  hauti| naikkhammaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kammaniyaṃ khāyati, abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samphassasuttaṃ

325. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusamphassasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau sautasamphassasmiṃ… yau ghānasamphassasmiṃ… yau jivhāsamphassasmiṃ… yau kāyasamphassasmiṃ… yau manausamphassasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samphassajasuttaṃ

326. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, cakkhusamphassajāya vaidanāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau sautasamphassajāya vaidanāya… yau ghānasamphassajāya vaidanāya… yau jivhāsamphassajāya vaidanāya… yau kāyasamphassajāya vaidanāya… yau manausamphassajāya vaidanāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. sañ‍ñāsuttaṃ

327. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpasañ‍ñāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau saddasañ‍ñāya… yau gandhasañ‍ñāya… yau rasasañ‍ñāya… yau phauṭṭhabbasañ‍ñāya… yau dhammasañ‍ñāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sañ‍caitanāsuttaṃ

328. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpasañ‍caitanāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau saddasañ‍caitanāya… yau gandhasañ‍caitanāya… yau  rasasañ‍caitanāya… yau phauṭṭhabbasañ‍caitanāya… yau dhammasañ‍caitanāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. taṇhāsuttaṃ

329. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpataṇhāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau saddataṇhāya… yau gandhataṇhāya… yau rasataṇhāya… yau phauṭṭhabbataṇhāya… yau dhammataṇhāya chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau…pai॰… abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. dhātusuttaṃ

330. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, pathavīdhātuyā chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yau āpaudhātuyā… yau taijaudhātuyā… yau vāyaudhātuyā… yau ākāsadhātuyā… yau viñ‍ñāṇadhātuyā chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imaisu chasu ṭhānaisu caitasau upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti, naikkhammanin‍nañ‍cassa cittaṃ  hauti| naikkhammaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kammaniyaṃ khāyati, abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. khandhasuttaṃ

331. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘yau, bhikkhavai, rūpasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau…pai॰… yau viñ‍ñāṇasmiṃ chandarāgau, cittassaisau upak‍kilaisau| yatau khau, bhikkhavai, bhikkhunau imaisu pañ‍casu ṭhānaisu caitasau upak‍kilaisau pahīnau hauti, naikkhammanin‍nañ‍cassa cittaṃ hauti| naikkhammaparibhāvitaṃ cittaṃ kammaniyaṃ khāyati, abhiñ‍ñā sacchikaraṇīyaisu dhammaisū’’ti| dasamaṃ|

kilaisasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

cakkhu  rūpañ‍ca viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, phassau ca vaidanāya ca|

sañ‍ñā ca caitanā taṇhā, dhātu khandhaina tai dasāti||


7. sāriputtasaṃyuttaṃ

1. vivaikajasuttaṃ

332. aikaṃ  samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya sāvatthiṃ piṇḍāya pāvisi| sāvatthiyaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā pacchābhattaṃ piṇḍapātapaṭik‍kantau yaina andhavanaṃ tainupasaṅkami divāvihārāya| andhavanaṃ ajjhaugāhaitvā añ‍ñatarasmiṃ rukkhamūlai divāvihāraṃ nisīdi|

atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau sāyanhasamayaṃ paṭisal‍lānā vuṭṭhitau yaina jaitavanaṃ anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmau tainupasaṅkami| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ dūratauva āgacchantaṃ| disvāna āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau sāriputta, indriyāni; parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaināyasmā sāriputtau aj‍ja vihāraina vihāsī’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, vivic‍caiva kāmaihi vivic‍ca akusalaihi dhammaihi savitak‍kaṃ savicāraṃ vivaikajaṃ pītisukhaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ  paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ paṭhamā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| ‘‘tathā  hi panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā susamūhatā| tasmā āyasmatau sāriputtassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ paṭhamaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ paṭhamā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. avitak‍kasuttaṃ

333. sāvatthinidānaṃ| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau sāriputta, indriyāni; parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaināyasmā sāriputtau aj‍ja vihāraina vihāsī’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, vitak‍kavicārānaṃ vūpasamā ajjhattaṃ sampasādanaṃ caitasau aikaudibhāvaṃ avitak‍kaṃ avicāraṃ samādhijaṃ pītisukhaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ dutiyā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| ‘‘tathā hi panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā susamūhatā| tasmā āyasmatau sāriputtassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ dutiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ dutiyā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. pītisuttaṃ

334. sāvatthinidānaṃ| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… ‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau sāriputta, indriyāni; parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaināyasmā sāriputtau aj‍ja vihāraina vihāsī’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, pītiyā ca virāgā upaikkhakau ca vihāsiṃ satau ca sampajānau sukhañ‍ca kāyaina paṭisaṃvaidaimi; yaṃ taṃ ariyā ācikkhanti ‘upaikkhakau satimā sukhavihārī’ti  tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja  viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ tatiyā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| ‘‘tathā hi panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā susamūhatā| tasmā āyasmatau sāriputtassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ tatiyaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ tatiyā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. upaikkhāsuttaṃ

335. sāvatthinidānaṃ| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… ‘‘vippasan‍nāni khau tai, āvusau sāriputta, indriyāni; parisuddhau mukhavaṇṇau pariyaudātau| katamaināyasmā sāriputtau aj‍ja vihāraina vihāsī’’ti?

‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sukhassa ca pahānā dukkhassa ca pahānā pubbaiva saumanassadaumanassānaṃ atthaṅgamā adukkhamasukhaṃ upaikkhāsatipārisuddhiṃ catutthaṃ  jhānaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ catutthā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| ‘‘tathā hi panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā susamūhatā| tasmā āyasmatau sāriputtassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ catutthaṃ jhānaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ catutthā jhānā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. ākāsānañ‍cāyatanasuttaṃ

336. sāvatthinidānaṃ| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau rūpasañ‍ñānaṃ samatik‍kamā paṭighasañ‍ñānaṃ atthaṅgamā nānattasañ‍ñānaṃ amanasikārā anantau ākāsauti ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi…pai॰… vuṭṭhitauti vā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanasuttaṃ

337. sāvatthinidānaṃ| addasā khau āyasmā ānandau…pai॰… ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau ākāsānañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma anantaṃ viñ‍ñāṇanti viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi…pai॰… vuṭṭhitauti vā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanasuttaṃ

338. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau…pai॰… ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau  viñ‍ñāṇañ‍cāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma, natthi kiñ‍cīti ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi…pai॰… vuṭṭhitauti vā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanasuttaṃ

339. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau…pai॰… ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, ākiñ‍cañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ upasampaj‍ja viharāmi…pai॰… vuṭṭhitauti vā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. niraudhasamāpattisuttaṃ

340. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau…pai॰…| ‘‘idhāhaṃ, āvusau, sabbasau naivasañ‍ñānāsañ‍ñāyatanaṃ samatik‍kamma sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ  upasampaj‍ja viharāmi| tassa mayhaṃ, āvusau, na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| ‘‘tathā hi panāyasmatau sāriputtassa dīgharattaṃ ahaṅkāramamaṅkāramānānusayā susamūhatā| tasmā āyasmatau sāriputtassa na aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpaj‍jāmī’ti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhaṃ samāpan‍nau’ti vā ‘ahaṃ sañ‍ñāvaidayitaniraudhā vuṭṭhitau’ti vā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. sūcimukhīsuttaṃ

341. aikaṃ samayaṃ āyasmā sāriputtau rājagahai viharati vaiḷuvanai kalandakanivāpai| atha khau āyasmā sāriputtau pubbaṇhasamayaṃ nivāsaitvā pattacīvaramādāya rājagahai piṇḍāya pāvisi| rājagahai sapadānaṃ piṇḍāya caritvā taṃ piṇḍapātaṃ añ‍ñataraṃ kuṭṭamūlaṃ kuḍḍamūlaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰), kuḍḍaṃ (pī॰) nissāya paribhuñ‍jati| atha khau sūcimukhī paribbājikā yaināyasmā sāriputtau tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā āyasmantaṃ sāriputtaṃ aitadavauca  –

‘‘kiṃ  nu khau, samaṇa, adhaumukhau bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, adhaumukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samaṇa, ubbhamukhau uddhaṃmukhau (sī॰ aṭṭha॰) bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, ubbhamukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti| ‘‘taina  hi, samaṇa, disāmukhau bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, disāmukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti| ‘‘taina hi, samaṇa, vidisāmukhau bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, vidisāmukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti|

‘‘‘kiṃ nu, samaṇa, adhaumukhau bhuñ‍jasī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, adhaumukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’ti vadaisi| ‘taina hi, samaṇa, ubbhamukhau bhuñ‍jasī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, ubbhamukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’ti vadaisi| ‘taina hi, samaṇa, disāmukhau bhuñ‍jasī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, disāmukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’ti vadaisi| ‘taina hi, samaṇa, vidisāmukhau bhuñ‍jasī’ti iti puṭṭhau samānau ‘na khvāhaṃ, bhagini, vidisāmukhau bhuñ‍jāmī’ti vadaisi’’|

‘‘kathañ‍carahi, samaṇa, bhuñ‍jasī’’ti? ‘‘yai hi kaici, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā samaṇā vā brāhmaṇā vā (sī॰) nigamanavākyai pana sabbatthāpi samāsauyaiva dissati vatthuvij‍jātiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ jīvitaṃ (ka॰) kappainti, imai vuc‍canti, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā ‘adhaumukhā bhuñ‍jantī’ti| yai hi kaici, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā nakkhattavij‍jātiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappainti, imai vuc‍canti, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā ‘ubbhamukhā bhuñ‍jantī’ti| yai hi kaici, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugāya … nuyaugā (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰), … nuyaugaina (?) micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappainti, imai vuc‍canti, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā ‘disāmukhā bhuñ‍jantī’ti| yai hi kaici, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā aṅgavij‍jātiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina  jīvikaṃ kappainti, imai vuc‍canti, bhagini, samaṇabrāhmaṇā ‘vidisāmukhā bhuñ‍jantī’’’ti|

‘‘sau khvāhaṃ, bhagini, na vatthuvij‍jātiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaimi, na nakkhattavij‍jātiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaimi, na dūtaiyyapahiṇagamanānuyaugāya micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaimi, na aṅgavij‍jātiracchānavij‍jāya micchājīvaina jīvikaṃ kappaimi| dhammaina bhikkhaṃ pariyaisāmi; dhammaina bhikkhaṃ pariyaisitvā bhuñ‍jāmī’’ti|

atha  khau sūcimukhī paribbājikā rājagahai rathiyāya rathiyaṃ, siṅghāṭakaina siṅghāṭakaṃ upasaṅkamitvā aivamāraucaisi – ‘‘dhammikaṃ samaṇā sakyaputtiyā āhāraṃ āhārainti; anavaj‍jaṃ anavaj‍jaina (ka॰) samaṇā sakyaputtiyā āhāraṃ āhārainti| daitha samaṇānaṃ sakyaputtiyānaṃ piṇḍa’’nti| dasamaṃ|

sāriputtasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

vivaikajaṃ avitak‍kaṃ, pīti upaikkhā catutthakaṃ|

ākāsañ‍caiva viñ‍ñāṇaṃ, ākiñ‍caṃ naivasañ‍ñinā|

niraudhau navamau vuttau, dasamaṃ sūcimukhī cāti||


8. nāgasaṃyuttaṃ

1. suddhikasuttaṃ

342. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, nāgayauniyau| katamā catassau? aṇḍajā nāgā, jalābujā nāgā, saṃsaidajā nāgā, aupapātikā nāgā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau nāgayauniyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. paṇītatarasuttaṃ

343. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, nāgayauniyau| katamā catassau? aṇḍajā nāgā, jalābujā nāgā, saṃsaidajā nāgā, aupapātikā nāgā| tatra, bhikkhavai, aṇḍajaihi nāgaihi jalābujā ca saṃsaidajā ca aupapātikā ca nāgā paṇītatarā| tatra, bhikkhavai, aṇḍajaihi ca jalābujaihi ca nāgaihi saṃsaidajā ca aupapātikā ca nāgā paṇītatarā| tatra, bhikkhavai, aṇḍajaihi ca jalābujaihi ca saṃsaidajaihi ca nāgaihi aupapātikā nāgā paṇītatarā| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau nāgayauniyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. upausathasuttaṃ

344. aikaṃ samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai aṇḍajā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cānaṃ  aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai kāyaina dvayakārinau ahumha, vācāya dvayakārinau, manasā dvayakārinau| tai mayaṃ kāyaina dvayakārinau, vācāya dvayakārinau, manasā dvayakārinau, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapan‍nā| sacaj‍ja mayaṃ kāyaina sucaritaṃ caraiyyāma, vācāya sucaritaṃ caraiyyāma, manasā sucaritaṃ caraiyyāma, aivaṃ mayaṃ  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyāma| handa, mayaṃ aitarahi kāyaina sucaritaṃ carāma, vācāya sucaritaṃ carāma, manasā sucaritaṃ carāmā’ti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai aṇḍajā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. dutiyaupausathasuttaṃ

345. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā…pai॰… aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai jalābujā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu…pai॰… ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai jalābujā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. tatiyaupausathasuttaṃ

346. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai saṃsaidajā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu…pai॰… ayaṃ  khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai saṃsaidajā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. catutthaupausathasuttaṃ

347. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai aupapātikā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cānaṃ aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ aivaṃ hauti – ‘mayaṃ khau pubbai kāyaina dvayakārinau ahumha, vācāya dvayakārinau, manasā dvayakārinau| tai mayaṃ kāyaina dvayakārinau, vācāya dvayakārinau, manasā dvayakārinau, kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapan‍nā| sacaj‍ja  mayaṃ kāyaina sucaritaṃ caraiyyāma, vācāya… manasā sucaritaṃ caraiyyāma, aivaṃ mayaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sugatiṃ saggaṃ laukaṃ upapaj‍jaiyyāma| handa, mayaṃ aitarahi kāyaina sucaritaṃ carāma, vācāya… manasā sucaritaṃ carāmā’ti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cai aupapātikā nāgā upausathaṃ upavasanti vaussaṭṭhakāyā ca bhavantī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. sutasuttaṃ

348. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī hauti, manasā dvayakārī hauti| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aṇḍajā nāgā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. dutiyasutasuttaṃ

349. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau  nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā jalābujānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?…pai॰… ayaṃ  khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā jalābujānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatīti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. tatiyasutasuttaṃ

350. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā saṃsaidajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?…pai॰… ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā saṃsaidajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatīti| navamaṃ|

10. catutthasutasuttaṃ

351. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa  bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aupapātikā nāgā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dasamaṃ|

11-20. aṇḍajadānūpakārasuttadasakaṃ

352-361. aikamantaṃ  nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aṇḍajā nāgā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu…pai॰… upapaj‍jatīti…pai॰… sau pānaṃ daiti…pai॰… vatthaṃ daiti…pai॰… yānaṃ daiti…pai॰… mālaṃ daiti…pai॰… gandhaṃ daiti…pai॰… vilaipanaṃ daiti…pai॰… saiyyaṃ daiti…pai॰… āvasathaṃ daiti…pai॰… padīpaiyyaṃ  daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina  midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| vīsatimaṃ|

21-50. jalābujādidānūpakārasuttattiṃsakaṃ

362-391. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā jalābujānaṃ nāgānaṃ…pai॰… saṃsaidajānaṃ nāgānaṃ…pai॰… aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti?

‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aupapātikā nāgā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… pānaṃ daiti…pai॰… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau  kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ nāgānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti|

(iminā paiyyālaina dasa dasa suttantā kātabbā| aivaṃ catūsu yaunīsu cattālīsaṃ vaiyyākaraṇā haunti| purimaihi pana dasahi suttantaihi saha haunti paṇṇāsasuttantāti|)

nāgasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

suddhikaṃ  paṇītataraṃ, caturau ca upausathā|

tassa sutaṃ caturau ca, dānūpakārā ca tālīsaṃ|

paṇṇāsa piṇḍatau suttā, nāgamhi suppakāsitāti||


9. supaṇṇasaṃyuttaṃ

1. suddhikasuttaṃ

392. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, supaṇṇayauniyau| katamā catassau? aṇḍajā supaṇṇā, jalābujā supaṇṇā, saṃsaidajā supaṇṇā, aupapātikā supaṇṇā – imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau supaṇṇayauniyau’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. harantisuttaṃ

393. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘catassau imā, bhikkhavai, supaṇṇayauniyau| katamā catassau? aṇḍajā…pai॰… imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau supaṇṇayauniyau| tatra, bhikkhavai, aṇḍajā supaṇṇā aṇḍajaiva nāgai haranti, na jalābujai, na saṃsaidajai, na aupapātikai| tatra, bhikkhavai, jalābujā supaṇṇā aṇḍajai ca jalābujai ca nāgai haranti, na saṃsaidajai, na aupapātikai| tatra, bhikkhavai, saṃsaidajā supaṇṇā aṇḍajai ca jalābujai ca saṃsaidajai ca nāgai haranti, na aupapātikai| tatra, bhikkhavai, aupapātikā supaṇṇā aṇḍajai ca jalābujai ca saṃsaidajai ca aupapātikai ca nāgai haranti| imā khau, bhikkhavai, catassau supaṇṇayauniyau’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. dvayakārīsuttaṃ

394. sāvatthinidānaṃ| añ‍ñatarau bhikkhu yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavantaṃ abhivādaitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā  aṇḍajānaṃ  supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aṇḍajā supaṇṇā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4-6. dutiyādidvayakārīsuttattikaṃ

395-397. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā jalābujānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ…pai॰… saṃsaidajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ…pai॰… aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aupapātikā supaṇṇā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7-16. aṇḍajadānūpakārasuttadasakaṃ

398-407. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aṇḍajā supaṇṇā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… pānaṃ daiti… vatthaṃ daiti… yānaṃ daiti… mālaṃ daiti… gandhaṃ daiti… vilaipanaṃ daiti… saiyyaṃ daiti… āvasathaṃ daiti… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aṇḍajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| sauḷasamaṃ|

17-46. jalābujādidānūpakārasuttatiṃsakaṃ

408-437. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā jalābujānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ…pai॰… saṃsaidajānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ…pai॰… aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina dvayakārī hauti, vācāya dvayakārī, manasā dvayakārī| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘aupapātikā supaṇṇā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… pānaṃ daiti…pai॰… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā aupapātikānaṃ supaṇṇānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| chacattālīsamaṃ|

(aivaṃ piṇḍakaina chacattālīsaṃ suttantā haunti|)

supaṇṇasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

suddhikaṃ haranti caiva, dvayakārī ca caturau|

dānūpakārā tālīsaṃ, supaṇṇai suppakāsitāti||


10. gandhabbakāyasaṃyuttaṃ

1. suddhikasuttaṃ

438. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai …pai॰… bhagavā aitadavauca – ‘‘gandhabbakāyikai  vau, bhikkhavai, daivai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamā ca, bhikkhavai, gandhabbakāyikā daivā? santi, bhikkhavai, mūlagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, sāragandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, phaiggugandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, tacagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, papaṭikagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, pattagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, pupphagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, phalagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, rasagandhai adhivatthā daivā| santi, bhikkhavai, gandhagandhai adhivatthā daivā| imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, gandhabbakāyikā daivā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sucaritasuttaṃ

439. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhabbakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘gandhabbakāyikā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā  paraṃ maraṇā gandhabbakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhabbakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhabbakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. mūlagandhadātāsuttaṃ

440. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘mūlagandhai adhivatthā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau dātā hauti mūlagandhānaṃ| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu…pai॰… yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4-12. sāragandhādidātāsuttanavakaṃ

441-449. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sāragandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… phaiggugandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… tacagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… papaṭikagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… pattagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… pupphagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… phalagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… rasagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘sāragandhai adhivatthā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sāragandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… phaiggugandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… tacagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… papaṭikagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… pattagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… pupphagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… phalagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… rasagandhai adhivatthānaṃ  daivānaṃ… gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau dātā hauti sāragandhānaṃ…pai॰… sau dātā hauti phaiggugandhānaṃ… sau dātā hauti tacagandhānaṃ… sau  dātā hauti papaṭikagandhānaṃ… sau dātā hauti pattagandhānaṃ… sau dātā hauti pupphagandhānaṃ… sau dātā hauti phalagandhānaṃ… sau dātā hauti rasagandhānaṃ… sau dātā hauti gandhagandhānaṃ| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhagandhai  adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ  pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13-22. mūlagandhadānūpakārasuttadasakaṃ

450-459. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘mūlagandhai adhivatthā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… pānaṃ daiti… vatthaṃ daiti… yānaṃ daiti… mālaṃ daiti… gandhaṃ daiti… vilaipanaṃ daiti… saiyyaṃ daiti… āvasathaṃ daiti… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā mūlagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| bāvīsatimaṃ|

23-112. sāragandhādidānūpakārasuttanavutikaṃ

460-549. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sāragandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… phaiggugandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… tacagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ … papaṭikagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… pattagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… pupphagandhai  adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… phalagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… rasagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ… gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘gandhagandhai adhivatthā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… pānaṃ daiti… vatthaṃ daiti… yānaṃ daiti… mālaṃ daiti… gandhaṃ daiti… vilaipanaṃ  daiti… saiyyaṃ daiti… āvasathaṃ daiti… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā gandhagandhai adhivatthānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dvādasasatimaṃ|

(aivaṃ piṇḍakaina aikasatañ‍ca dvādasa ca suttantā haunti|)

gandhabbakāyasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

suddhikañ‍ca sucaritaṃ, dātā hi aparai dasa|

dānūpakārā satadhā, gandhabbai suppakāsitāti||


11. valāhakasaṃyuttaṃ

1. suddhikasuttaṃ

550. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘valāhakakāyikai vau, bhikkhavai, daivai daisaissāmi| taṃ suṇātha| katamai ca, bhikkhavai, valāhakakāyikā daivā? santi, bhikkhavai, sītavalāhakā daivā; santi uṇhavalāhakā daivā; santi abbhavalāhakā daivā; santi vātavalāhakā daivā; santi vassavalāhakā daivā – imai vuc‍canti, bhikkhavai, ‘valāhakakāyikā daivā’’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. sucaritasuttaṃ
