551. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā valāhakakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘valāhakakāyikā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā valāhakakāyikānaṃ  daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā valāhakakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā valāhakakāyikānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3-12. sītavalāhakadānūpakārasuttadasakaṃ

552-561. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca  – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sītavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘sītavalāhakā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ  hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sītavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sītavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā sītavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13-52. uṇhavalāhakadānūpakārasuttacālīsakaṃ

562-601. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā uṇhavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… abbhavalāhakānaṃ  daivānaṃ…pai॰… vātavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ…pai॰… vassavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti? ‘‘idha, bhikkhu, aikac‍cau kāyaina sucaritaṃ carati, vācāya sucaritaṃ carati, manasā sucaritaṃ carati| tassa sutaṃ hauti – ‘vassavalāhakā daivā dīghāyukā vaṇṇavantau sukhabahulā’ti| tassa aivaṃ hauti – ‘ahau vatāhaṃ kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā vassavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jaiyya’nti| sau an‍naṃ daiti…pai॰… padīpaiyyaṃ daiti| sau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā vassavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yaina midhaikac‍cau kāyassa bhaidā paraṃ maraṇā vassavalāhakānaṃ daivānaṃ sahabyataṃ upapaj‍jatī’’ti| dvaipañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

53. sītavalāhakasuttaṃ

602. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaikadā sītaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘santi, bhikkhu, sītavalāhakā nāma daivā| taisaṃ yadā aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūna mayaṃ sakāya ratiyā vasaiyyāmā’ti  ramaiyyāmāti (sī॰ syā॰ kaṃ॰ pī॰) aivamuparipi, taisaṃ taṃ caitaupaṇidhimanvāya sītaṃ hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaikadā sītaṃ hautī’’ti| taipañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

54. uṇhavalāhakasuttaṃ

603. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaikadā uṇhaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘santi, bhikkhu, uṇhavalāhakā nāma daivā| taisaṃ yadā aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūna mayaṃ sakāya ratiyā vasaiyyāmā’ti, taisaṃ taṃ caitaupaṇidhimanvāya uṇhaṃ hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaikadā uṇhaṃ hautī’’ti| catupañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

55. abbhavalāhakasuttaṃ

604. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaikadā abbhaṃ hautī’’ti? ‘‘santi, bhikkhu, abbhavalāhakā nāma daivā| taisaṃ yadā aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūna mayaṃ sakāya ratiyā vasaiyyāmā’ti, taisaṃ taṃ caitaupaṇidhimanvāya abbhaṃ hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaikadā abbhaṃ hautī’’ti| pañ‍capañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

56. vātavalāhakasuttaṃ

605. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaikadā vātau hautī’’ti? ‘‘santi, bhikkhu, vātavalāhakā nāma daivā| taisaṃ yadā  aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūna mayaṃ sakāya ratiyā vasaiyyāmā’ti, taisaṃ taṃ caitaupaṇidhimanvāya vātau hauti| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaikadā vātau hautī’’ti| chappañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

57. vassavalāhakasuttaṃ

606. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau sau bhikkhu bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca  – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhantai, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yainaikadā daivau vassatī’’ti? ‘‘santi, bhikkhu, vassavalāhakā nāma daivā| taisaṃ yadā  aivaṃ hauti – ‘yaṃnūna mayaṃ sakāya ratiyā vasaiyyāmā’ti, taisaṃ taṃ caitaupaṇidhimanvāya daivau vassati| ayaṃ khau, bhikkhu, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yainaikadā daivau vassatī’’ti| sattapañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

sattapañ‍ñāsasuttantaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|

valāhakasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

suddhikaṃ sucaritañ‍ca dānūpakārapañ‍ñāsaṃ|

sītaṃ uṇhañ‍ca abbhañ‍ca vātavassavalāhakāti||


12. vacchagauttasaṃyuttaṃ

1. rūpaañ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

607. aikaṃ  samayaṃ bhagavā sāvatthiyaṃ viharati jaitavanai anāthapiṇḍikassa ārāmai| atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ  aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni yainimāni (?) anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā, anantavā laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? ‘‘rūpai khau, vaccha, añ‍ñāṇā, rūpasamudayai añ‍ñāṇā, rūpaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇā, rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇā; aivamimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vāti| ayaṃ khau, vaccha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yānimāni yaina (sī॰), yainimāni (?) anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ  maraṇāti vā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. vaidanāañ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

608. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni  laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? ‘‘vaidanāya khau, vaccha, añ‍ñāṇā, vaidanāsamudayai añ‍ñāṇā, vaidanāniraudhai añ‍ñāṇā, vaidanāniraudhagāminiyā  paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇā; aivamimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vāti| ayaṃ khau, vaccha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. sañ‍ñāañ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

609. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau  bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? ‘‘sañ‍ñāya khau, vaccha, añ‍ñāṇā, sañ‍ñāsamudayai añ‍ñāṇā, sañ‍ñāniraudhai añ‍ñāṇā, sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminiyā  paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇā; aivamimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vāti| ayaṃ khau, vaccha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. saṅkhāraañ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

610. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? ‘‘saṅkhāraisu khau, vaccha, añ‍ñāṇā, saṅkhārasamudayai  añ‍ñāṇā, saṅkhāraniraudhai añ‍ñāṇā, saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇā; aivamimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vāti| ayaṃ khau, vaccha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai  uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. viñ‍ñāṇaañ‍ñāṇasuttaṃ

611. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā  …pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, añ‍ñāṇā, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayai añ‍ñāṇā, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhai añ‍ñāṇā, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya añ‍ñāṇā; aivamimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vāti| ayaṃ khau, vaccha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6-10. rūpaadassanādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

612-616. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? rūpai khau, vaccha, adassanā…pai॰… rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya adassanā…pai॰… vaidanāya … sañ‍ñāya … saṅkhāraisu khau, vaccha, adassanā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, adassanā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya adassanā…pai॰…| dasamaṃ|

11-15. rūpaanabhisamayādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

617-621. sāvatthinidānaṃ| rūpai khau, vaccha, anabhisamayā…pai॰… rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya anabhisamayā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| vaidanāya khau, vaccha, anabhisamayā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| sañ‍ñāya khau, vaccha, anabhisamayā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| saṅkhāraisu khau, vaccha, anabhisamayā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, anabhisamayā…pai॰…| pan‍narasamaṃ|

16-20. rūpaananubaudhādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

622-626. sāvatthinidānaṃ| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau…pai॰… rūpai khau, vaccha, ananubaudhā…pai॰… rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ananubaudhā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| vaidanāya khau, vaccha…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| sañ‍ñāya khau, vaccha…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| saṅkhāraisu khau, vaccha…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha ananubaudhā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya ananubaudhā| vīsatimaṃ|

21-25. rūpaappaṭivaidhādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

627-631. sāvatthinidānaṃ| kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau…pai॰…| rūpai khau, vaccha, appaṭivaidhā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, appaṭivaidhā…pai॰…| pañ‍cavīsatimaṃ|

26-30. rūpaasal‍lakkhaṇādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

632-636. sāvatthinidānaṃ| rūpai khau, vaccha, asal‍lakkhaṇā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, asal‍lakkhaṇā…pai॰…| tiṃsatimaṃ|

31-35. rūpaanupalakkhaṇādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

637-641. sāvatthinidānaṃ| rūpai khau, vaccha, anupalakkhaṇā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, anupalakkhaṇā…pai॰…| pañ‍catiṃsatimaṃ|

36-40. rūpaappac‍cupalakkhaṇādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

642-646. sāvatthinidānaṃ| rūpai khau, vaccha, appac‍cupalakkhaṇā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, appac‍cupalakkhaṇā…pai॰…| cattālīsamaṃ|

41-45. rūpaasamapaikkhaṇādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

647-651. sāvatthinidānaṃ| rūpai khau, vaccha, asamapaikkhaṇā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, asamapaikkhaṇā…pai॰…| pañ‍cacattālīsamaṃ|

46-50. rūpaappac‍cupaikkhaṇādisuttapañ‍cakaṃ

652-656. sāvatthinidānaṃ| rūpai khau, vaccha, appac‍cupaikkhaṇā…pai॰… viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, appac‍cupaikkhaṇā…pai॰…| pañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

51-54. rūpaappac‍cakkhakammādisuttacatuk‍kaṃ

657-660. sāvatthinidānaṃ| atha khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau yaina bhagavā tainupasaṅkami; upasaṅkamitvā bhagavatā saddhiṃ sammaudi| sammaudanīyaṃ kathaṃ sāraṇīyaṃ vītisāraitvā aikamantaṃ nisīdi| aikamantaṃ nisin‍nau khau vacchagauttau paribbājakau bhagavantaṃ aitadavauca – ‘‘kau nu khau, bhau gautama, haitu, kau pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti? rūpai khau, vaccha, appac‍cakkhakammā, rūpasamudayai appac‍cakkhakammā, rūpaniraudhai appac‍cakkhakammā, rūpaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| vaidanāya khau, vaccha, appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰… vaidanāniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| sañ‍ñāya khau, vaccha, appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰… sañ‍ñāniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰…|

sāvatthinidānaṃ| saṅkhāraisu khau, vaccha, appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰… saṅkhāraniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya appac‍cakkhakammā…pai॰…| catupañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

55. viñ‍ñāṇaappac‍cakkhakammasuttaṃ

661. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘viñ‍ñāṇai khau, vaccha, appac‍cakkhakammā, viñ‍ñāṇasamudayai appac‍cakkhakammā, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhai appac‍cakkhakammā, viñ‍ñāṇaniraudhagāminiyā paṭipadāya appac‍cakkhakammā; aivamimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti  – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā…pai॰… naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vāti| ayaṃ khau, vaccha, haitu, ayaṃ pac‍cayau, yānimāni anaikavihitāni diṭṭhigatāni laukai uppaj‍janti – sassatau laukauti vā, asassatau laukauti vā, antavā laukauti vā, anantavā laukauti vā, taṃ jīvaṃ taṃ sarīranti vā, añ‍ñaṃ jīvaṃ añ‍ñaṃ sarīranti vā, hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, hauti ca na ca hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā, naiva hauti na na hauti tathāgatau paraṃ maraṇāti vā’’ti| pañ‍capañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

vacchagauttasaṃyuttaṃ samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

añ‍ñāṇā adassanā caiva, anabhisamayā ananubaudhā|

appaṭivaidhā asal‍lakkhaṇā, anupalakkhaṇaina appac‍cupalakkhaṇā|

asamapaikkhaṇā appac‍cupaikkhaṇā, appac‍cakkhakammanti||


13. jhānasaṃyuttaṃ

1. samādhimūlakasamāpattisuttaṃ

662. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau pāmaukkhau (syā॰ kaṃ॰) aivamuparipi ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau tatra aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| paṭhamaṃ|

2. samādhimūlakaṭhitisuttaṃ

663. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  jhāyī samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ  catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā  sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau tatra aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| dutiyaṃ|

3. samādhimūlakavuṭṭhānasuttaṃ

664. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ  jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| tatiyaṃ|

4. samādhimūlakakal‍litasuttaṃ

665. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca  maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| catutthaṃ|

5. samādhimūlakaārammaṇasuttaṃ

666. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau  ca hauti, samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| pañ‍camaṃ|

6. samādhimūlakagaucarasuttaṃ

667. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| chaṭṭhaṃ|

7. samādhimūlakaabhinīhārasuttaṃ

668. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| sattamaṃ|

8. samādhimūlakasak‍kac‍cakārīsuttaṃ

669. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| aṭṭhamaṃ|

9. samādhimūlakasātac‍cakārīsuttaṃ

670. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva  samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| navamaṃ|

10. samādhimūlakasappāyakārīsuttaṃ

671. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samādhikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| dasamaṃ| (samādhimūlakaṃ|)

11. samāpattimūlakaṭhitisuttaṃ

672. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| aikādasamaṃ|

12. samāpattimūlakavuṭṭhānasuttaṃ

673. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca  samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau  ca hauti, samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī …pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| dvādasamaṃ|

13. samāpattimūlakakal‍litasuttaṃ

674. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau  ca hauti, samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau ca| tatra…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| tairasamaṃ|

14. samāpattimūlakaārammaṇasuttaṃ

675. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau ca| tatra…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| cuddasamaṃ|

15. samāpattimūlakagaucarasuttaṃ

676. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau  hauti, na samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau  ca hauti, samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau ca| tatra…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| pan‍narasamaṃ|

16. samāpattimūlakaabhinīhārasuttaṃ

677. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ  abhinīhārakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau ca| tatra…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| sauḷasamaṃ|

17. samāpattimūlakasak‍kac‍casuttaṃ

678. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī ca| tatra…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| sattarasamaṃ|

18. samāpattimūlakasātac‍casuttaṃ

679. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau  ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca| tatra…pai॰… pavarau cā’’ti| aṭṭhārasamaṃ|

19. samāpattimūlakasappāyakārīsuttaṃ

680. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau tatra aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ samāpattikusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| aikūnavīsatimaṃ| (samāpattimūlakaṃ|)

20-27. ṭhitimūlakavuṭṭhānasuttādiaṭṭhakaṃ

681-688. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau  jhāyī samādhismiṃ ṭhitikusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| vīsatimaṃ| (purimamūlakāni viya yāva sattavīsatimā ṭhitimūlakasappāyakārīsuttā aṭṭha suttāni pūraitabbāni| ṭhitimūlakaṃ|)

28-34. vuṭṭhānamūlakakal‍litasuttādisattakaṃ

689-695. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘cattāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, na  samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau… samādhismiṃ  kal‍litakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau… naiva samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau… samādhismiṃ vuṭṭhānakusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| aṭṭhavīsatimaṃ| (purimamūlakāni viya yāva catuttiṃsatimā vuṭṭhānamūlakasappāyakārīsuttā satta suttāni pūraitabbāni| vuṭṭhānamūlakaṃ|)

35-40. kal‍litamūlakaārammaṇasuttādichak‍kaṃ

696-701. sāvatthinidānaṃ … ‘‘samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau… samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau… naiva samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau… samādhismiṃ kal‍litakusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| pañ‍catiṃsatimaṃ| (purimamūlakāni viya yāva cattālīsamā kal‍litamūlakasappāyakārīsuttā cha suttāni pūraitabbāni| kal‍litamūlakaṃ|)

41-45. ārammaṇamūlakagaucarasuttādipañ‍cakaṃ

702-706. sāvatthinidānaṃ … ‘‘samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau… samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau… naiva samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ  gaucarakusalau… samādhismiṃ ārammaṇakusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| aikacattālīsamaṃ| (purimamūlakāni viya yāva pañ‍cacattālīsamā ārammaṇamūlakasappāyakārīsuttā pañ‍ca suttāni pūraitabbāni| ārammaṇamūlakaṃ|)

46-49. gaucaramūlakaabhinīhārasuttādicatuk‍kaṃ

707. sāvatthinidānaṃ… ‘‘samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau… samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau… naiva samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau… samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau ca… saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau tatra aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau ca hauti samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| chacattālīsamaṃ|

708. samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī…pai॰…| vitthāraitabbaṃ| sattacattālīsamaṃ|

709. samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī…pai॰…| aṭṭhacattālīsamaṃ|

710. samādhismiṃ gaucarakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī…pai॰…| aikūnapañ‍ñāsamaṃ| (gaucaramūlakaṃ|)

50-52. abhinīhāramūlakasak‍kac‍casuttāditikaṃ

711. sāvatthinidānaṃ … ‘‘samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na  samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī… samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau… naiva samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī… samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| pañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

712. samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī…pai॰…| aikapañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

713. samādhismiṃ abhinīhārakusalau hauti, na samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī…pai॰…| dvaipañ‍ñāsamaṃ| (abhinīhāramūlakaṃ|)

53-54. sak‍kac‍camūlakasātac‍cakārīsuttādidukaṃ

714. sāvatthinidānaṃ … ‘‘samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī… samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī … naiva samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī… samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ…pai॰… uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| taipañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

715. samādhismiṃ sak‍kac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī…pai॰…| catupañ‍ñāsamaṃ|

55. sātac‍camūlakasappāyakārīsuttaṃ

716. sāvatthinidānaṃ| ‘‘catāraumai, bhikkhavai, jhāyī| katamai cattārau? idha, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī hauti, na samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī| idha pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī naiva samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī hauti, na ca samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī| idha  pana, bhikkhavai, aikac‍cau jhāyī samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca| tatra, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca hauti samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ca ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau ca| saiyyathāpi, bhikkhavai, gavā khīraṃ, khīramhā dadhi, dadhimhā navanītaṃ, navanītamhā sappi, sappimhā sappimaṇḍau tatra aggamakkhāyati; aivamaiva khau, bhikkhavai, yvāyaṃ jhāyī samādhismiṃ sātac‍cakārī ca hauti, samādhismiṃ sappāyakārī ayaṃ imaisaṃ catun‍naṃ jhāyīnaṃ aggau ca saiṭṭhau ca maukkhau ca uttamau ca pavarau cā’’ti| idamavauca bhagavā| attamanā tai bhikkhū bhagavatau bhāsitaṃ abhinandunti| pañ‍capañ‍ñāsamaṃ| (yathā pañ‍capañ‍ñāsaṃ vaiyyākaraṇāni haunti tathā vitthāraitabbāni|)

jhānasaṃyuttaṃ samādhisaṃyuttaṃ (syā॰ kaṃ॰) samattaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

samādhi  samāpatti ṭhiti ca, vuṭṭhānaṃ kal‍litārammaṇaina ca|

gaucarā abhinīhārau sak‍kac‍ca, sātac‍ca athaupi sappāyanti||

khandhavaggau tatiyau|

tassuddānaṃ –

khandha rādhasaṃyuttañ‍ca, diṭṭhiauk‍kanta auk‍kanti (sabbattha) uppādā|

kilaisa sāriputtā ca, nāgā supaṇṇa gandhabbā|

valāha vacchajhānanti, khandhavaggamhi tairasāti||

khandhavaggasaṃyuttapāḷi niṭṭhitā|
